</details>


### [124] [Spectral Analysis Beyond the Spectral Gap: Rapid Mixing of Quantum Gibbs Samplers in One Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05848)
*Hongrui Chen,Zhiyan Ding*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了在一维非对易局部自旋系统中，量子Gibbs采样器在任意固定正温度下都能快速混合，收敛时间达到 O(log^4(n) + log(1/ε))。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前的混合时间上界为 O(n + log(1/ε))，且快速混合的结论主要局限于高温或弱相互作用区域。作者希望突破这些限制，解释为何谱隙可能显著低估平衡化速度。

Method: 基于CKG2023的KMS细致平衡Gibbs采样器，作者提出超越谱隙的谱分析框架。核心思想是：远离平衡的态必然在低权重Pauli算子张成空间之外有显著分量，而在此区域可以使用更强的耗散估计。

Result: 对n个格点的系统，采样器从任意初始态收敛到迹范数误差ε所需时间为 O(log^4(n) + log(1/ε))。衰减估计等价于在夹层Rényi-2散度下的指数收敛；结合谱隙还可推出速率为 Ω(log^{-4}(n)) 的修正对数Sobolev不等式。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了超越高温和弱相互作用区域的快速混合，给出了两阶段弛豫机制：远离平衡时双指数收敛，接近平衡时指数收敛，并为超越谱隙的量子混合分析提供了通用框架。

Abstract: We prove rapid mixing of a quantum Gibbs sampler for general one-dimensional non-commuting local spin systems at every fixed positive temperature. In particular, for a system of $n$ sites, we show that the Kubo--Martin--Schwinger (KMS) detailed-balanced Gibbs sampler of~\cite{CKG2023} converges from any initial state to trace-norm error $ε$ in time $O(\log^4(n)+\log(1/ε))$. This improves the previous $O(n+\log(1/ε))$ bound and establishes rapid mixing beyond high-temperature and weak-interaction regimes. Our proof explains why the slowest decay rate, captured by the spectral gap, can substantially underestimate the speed of equilibration. The key insight is that a state far from equilibrium must have a substantial component outside the span of low-weight Pauli operators, where stronger dissipation estimates apply. This yields two stages of relaxation: a double-exponential convergence from a bad initial state, followed by exponential convergence near equilibrium. We turn this mechanism into a framework for proving rapid mixing through spectral analysis beyond the gap. Our decay estimate is equivalent to exponential convergence in sandwiched Rényi-2 divergence. Together with the spectral gap, it also implies a modified logarithmic Sobolev inequality with rate $Ω(\log^{-4}(n))$.

</details>


### [125] [Classical observable coalescence as a discriminating diagnostic for spectral degeneracies in non-Hermitian systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05877)
*P. K. Nkaimbi,C. A. Mba,A. B. Moubissi,M. R. Kamsap,L. C. Fai,M. E. Ateuafack,M. N. Jipdi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出以“经典可观测量的合并”作为诊断异常点（EP）的判据，并在非厄米Ising链中验证其能区分EP与diabolic point，同时给出电路实现方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异常点通常依赖本征矢合并来识别，但经典平台没有哈密顿量谱，难以直接获得本征矢，因此需要一种仅基于物理构型（可观测量）的EP诊断方法。

Method: 选取耦合为J、交错虚场为γ的非厄米Ising链；对Heisenberg动力学作平均场约化，得到两支对称相关平衡分支，其融合出现在EP超曲面边界|γ|=|J|，呈现√(J²−γ²)分支点结构与双叶Riemann拓扑；并用Jordan-Wigner与Bogoliubov-de Gennes精确处理进行对照验证；提出耦合增益-损耗电路实现。

Result: 平均场分支在|γ|=|J|处融合并显示√(J²−γ²)分支点与双叶Riemann结构；γ=0, η=0处的简并被判定为diabolic point，不产生可观测合并；精确JW与BdG处理确认在同一阈值处发生本征矢合并。

Conclusion: 经典可观测合并可作为识别非厄米临界性/EP的实验可及判据，且具有区分EP与diabolic point的判别力；耦合增益-损耗电路为实现该诊断提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Exceptional points (EPs) are conventionally identified through eigenvector coalescence, inaccessible in classical platforms lacking a Hamiltonian spectrum. We introduce classical observable coalescence as a diagnostic identifying EPs directly from physical configurations, and demonstrate it in a known non-Hermitian Ising chain with coupling $J$ and staggered imaginary field $γ$, chosen for verifiability against established quantum results. A mean-field reduction of the Heisenberg dynamics yields equilibrium branches organising into two symmetry-related families whose fusion at the boundary of the EP supersurface $|γ|=|J|$ exhibits the characteristic $\sqrt{J^2-γ^2}$ branch-point structure and two-sheeted Riemann topology. Crucially, the degeneracy at $γ=0,η=0 $ is shown to be a diabolic point, producing no observable coalescence, establishing the diagnostic as discriminating rather than merely suggestive. An exact Jordan-Wigner and Bogoliubov-de Gennes treatment confirms eigenvector coalescence at the same threshold, and a coupled gain-loss circuit realisation is proposed, establishing classical observables as experimentally accessible signatures of non-Hermitian criticality.

</details>


### [126] [Fusion-assisted decoding of non-Abelian topological order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05901)
*Rohith Sajith,Yabo Li,Zijian Song,Tsung-Cheng Lu,Carolyn Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明在非阿贝尔任意子码中，采用分辨融合结果的精细化错误症候集可系统性提升最大似然解码性能；以D4量子双重模型在电荷噪声下的显式解码器为例，通过映射到经典统计力学模型数值求得阈值，发现存在非零噪声阈值，使得单轮测量即可恢复整个逻辑qudit。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大似然解码器在阿贝尔任意子的环面码及其他表面码中已被充分理解，可饱和信息论解码阈值。但支持横向或常深度非克利福德门、并具备高效制备协议的非阿贝尔任意子码日益重要，其解码面临新挑战：非阿贝尔任意子一般不能被确定性湮灭，逻辑算子也无法用阿贝尔同调分类。因此需要研究适用于非阿贝尔任意子码的解码策略。

Method: 将包括可分辨非阿贝尔任意子融合结果的测量在内的精细化错误症候集纳入最大似然解码框架；针对D4量子双重模型在电荷噪声下构造显式解码器，并将解码问题映射到经典统计力学模型以进行阈值数值计算。

Result: 数值结果表明，精细化错误症候集能系统性改进最大似然解码器的性能；在D4量子双重模型电荷噪声下存在非零噪声阈值，低于该阈值时仅用单轮测量即可恢复整个逻辑qudit。

Conclusion: 精细化错误症候（包括分辨非阿贝尔任意子融合结果）是提升非阿贝尔任意子码最大似然解码的有效手段，且D4模型在电荷噪声下具备非零阈值与单轮测量恢复逻辑qudit的能力，为非阿贝尔拓扑码的实用化解码提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Maximum likelihood (ML) decoders perform quantum error correction by measuring a set of syndromes, classically computing an optimal recovery based on these syndromes and the noise model, and then performing that recovery. ML decoders are well understood for the toric code and generalize straightforwardly to other surface codes with Abelian anyons, where they can saturate information-theoretic decoding thresholds. On the other hand, codes with non-Abelian anyons have become increasingly relevant because they can support transversal or constant-depth non-Clifford gates and admit efficient preparation protocols in modern quantum devices. However, they present new challenges for decoding: non-Abelian anyons cannot in general be deterministically annihilated, and the logical operators cannot be classified by Abelian homology. In this work, we show that refined syndrome sets, including measurements that resolve non-Abelian anyon fusion outcomes, systematically improve the performance of ML decoders. We demonstrate this concretely by constructing explicit decoders for the $D_4$ quantum double model under charge noise, and obtaining numerical results for thresholds via mappings to classical statistical-mechanics models. We show that there is a nonzero noise threshold below which the entire logical qudit can be recovered with a single round of measurements.

</details>


### [127] [Robust Diamond Quantum Sensing in Double-Quantum 4-Ramsey Interferometry by Dual-Feedback Scheme](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05907)
*Yuta Araki,Takeharu Sekiguchi,Yuji Hatano,Naota Sekiguchi,Zehan Li,Chikara Shinei,Masashi Miyakawa,Takashi Taniguchi,Tokuyuki Teraji,Hiroshi Abe,Shinobu Onoda,Takeshi Ohshima,Mutsuko Hatano,Takayuki Iwasaki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于双反馈系统的金刚石量子磁强计，通过同步频率调制独立跟踪并补偿双量子4-Ramsey干涉中的共模与差模频率漂移，实现了长时间高稳定磁场测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极弱磁场信号检测需长时间信号积累以提升信噪比，但温度漂移与环境磁噪声会引起工作频率偏离最优值，严重限制传感系统的长期稳定性。因此需要一种能补偿共模与差模频率偏移、维持最优响应的方案。

Method: 基于包含失谐项的哈密顿量，推导双量子4-Ramsey传感对共模和差模失谐的信号响应解析解，从理论上明确鲁棒性极限；据此引入同步频率调制与双反馈方案，独立跟踪并补偿两类频率漂移，同时保持最优磁场响应。

Result: 在2.8 K温度变化范围内实现稳定连续运行；在5–100 Hz频段维持7.0 pT/Hz^1/2的磁场灵敏度长达4小时，噪声本底低至64 fT/Hz^1/2；长时间连续传感中呈现预期的1/T^1/2积分时间标度关系，并避免振幅低估与波形畸变。

Conclusion: 该方法为长时间双量子4-Ramsey金刚石磁强计提供了鲁棒方案，展示了其在弱生物磁信号检测中的应用潜力。

Abstract: We developed a highly sensitive and long-term stable diamond quantum magnetometer by integrating a dual-feedback system that compensates for common- and differential-mode frequency shifts in double-quantum (DQ) 4-Ramsey interferometry. Detecting extremely weak magnetic-field signals requires extensive signal accumulation to enhance the signal-to-noise ratio. However, deviations from the optimal operating frequencies caused by thermal drift or environmental magnetic noise severely hinder the long-term stability of such a sensing system. To overcome the limitation, we first derived analytical solutions for the signal response to common- and differential-mode detunings based on a detuning-inclusive Hamiltonian to theoretically clarify the robustness limit in DQ 4-Ramsey sensing. Guided by this theoretical model, we introduced synchronized frequency modulation and a dual-feedback scheme to independently track and compensate for these frequency shifts while maintaining an optimal magnetic-field response. The proposed method enabled stable continuous operation against temperature variations of 2.8 K. By maintaining a magnetic field sensitivity of 7.0 pT/Hz^1/2 in the frequency range of 5-100 Hz for a prolonged duration of 4 hours, we achieved an extremely low noise floor of 64 fT/Hz^1/2. During prolonged continuous sensing, the system exhibited the expected 1/T^1/2 scaling with integration time T, and prevented both the underestimation of amplitude and waveform distortion. These results establish a robust approach for long-term DQ 4-Ramsey diamond magnetometry and demonstrate its potential for detecting weak biomagnetic signals.

</details>


### [128] [Evaluating the performance of QEC primitives on quantum processors at large width and depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05928)
*J. A. Montanez-Barrera; Kristel Michielsen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种基于线性参数QAOA（LR-QAOA）的少样本基准测试，用MCM实现，通过近似比r随深度衰减映射为等效双比特去极化率λeff，用于评估QEC相关原语与硬件性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QEC依赖重复宇称提取、MCM、复位、前馈与调度等原语，但现有评估多将其孤立测试，或依赖资源消耗巨大的逻辑内存实验。

Method: 以选定码的校验结构构造QAOA哈密顿量，使电路模拟症状提取的连接性与MCM模式；LR-QAOA层数类比重复的症候提取轮数；由r随深度的衰减定义有效硬件误差并映射为等效双比特去极化率λeff；在IBM、IQM、Quantinuum共10台QPU上对比直接与MCM中介实现。

Result: 电路含多达2950个MCM操作；在Quantinuum Helios-1与H2-1上运行表面码、三角色码、双变量单车qLDPC结构基准，分别达81、91、48个数据比特，MCM多达480个；在ibm_phoenix上实现表面码结构，其基准响应与逻辑内存实验在不同QPU区域间呈相关性。

Conclusion: 该基准构建简单、资源需求低，可在完整逻辑内存实验之前，用于比较不同硬件代际与QEC实现。

Abstract: Quantum error correction (QEC) relies on repeated parity extraction, mid-circuit measurement (MCM), reset, feed-forward, and scheduling, yet these primitives are usually assessed either in isolation or through resource-demanding experiments. We introduce a few-sample benchmark of QEC-relevant primitives based on an MCM implementation of the quantum approximate optimization algorithm (QAOA) with a fixed set of linear parameters (LR-QAOA). For a chosen code, the QAOA Hamiltonian is constructed from its check structure, such that the resulting circuit mimics the syndrome-extraction connectivity and MCM pattern while producing a direct algorithmic signal, the approximation-ratio r. The LR-QAOA depth, defined by the number of QAOA layers, plays a role analogous to the number of repeated syndrome-extraction rounds in a QEC experiment. From QPU executions, the decay of r with depth defines an effective hardware error, which we map to an equivalent two-qubit depolarizing rate λeff. We compare direct and MCM-mediated implementations across 10 QPUs from IBM, IQM, and Quantinuum, with circuits containing up to 2950 MCM operations. On Quantinuum's Helios-1 and H2-1, we run code-structured LR-QAOA benchmarks for surface-code, triangular color-code, and bivariate-bicycle qLDPC Hamiltonians up to 81, 91, and 48 data qubits, respectively, using up to 480 MCM operations. On IBM ibm_phoenix, we implement the surface-code structure and compare the LR-QAOA response with logical-memory experiments, observing a correlation between the benchmark and the logical performance across different regions of the QPU. Its construction and low resource requirements provide a practical benchmark for comparing hardware generations and QEC implementations before full logical-memory experiments are performed.

</details>


### [129] [Fast mixing of the SYK model at high temperatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05953)
*Yiyi Cai,Yongtao Zhan,Alexander Zlokapa*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明SYK模型在足够高的常数温度下、以高概率存在具有与系统规模无关谱隙的量子Gibbs采样器，从而可在量子计算机上多项式时间制备SYK Gibbs态，误差可达任意逆多项式迹距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SYK模型是全对全无序相互作用的强关联费米子系统，其动力学难以用Lieb-Robinson界和集团展开分析。此前关于SYK热化的非严格计算仅局限于局域关联函数，作者希望给出更严格的、涵盖任意可观测量的热化保证。

Method: 证明中引入适用于费米子的伪Lindblad量（pseudo-Lindbladian）方法，并采用一种特别简单的SYK分析，将其与经典动力学进行对比。整个论证以高概率关于无序成立。

Result: 在足够高的常数温度下，SYK模型以高概率允许存在具有系统规模无关谱隙的量子Gibbs采样器，并由此实现SYK Gibbs态的多项式时间量子制备。该结果在任意逆多项式迹距离意义下成立，强于此前仅针对局域关联函数的结论。

Conclusion: 该工作为SYK模型的热化提供了严格的量子算法层面保证，并展示了伪Lindblad方法与经典动力学对比技术在强关联费米子系统分析中的有效性。

Abstract: The Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev (SYK) model is a strongly interacting fermionic system. Its dynamics are difficult to analyze with Lieb-Robinson bounds and cluster expansions due to its all-to-all disordered interactions. We prove that, with high probability over the disorder, the SYK model admits a quantum Gibbs sampler with a system-size-independent spectral gap at sufficiently high constant temperatures. This yields polynomial-time preparation of SYK Gibbs states on a quantum computer. While prior non-rigorous computations that suggested SYK thermalization were limited to studying local correlators, we emphasize that our result holds up to arbitrary inverse polynomial trace distance. Our proof introduces the pseudo-Lindbladian approach to fermions and uses an especially simple SYK analysis based on a comparison to classical dynamics.

</details>


### [130] [Joint GKP Encoding for GHZ States Transmission over Bosonic Quantum MIMO Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05992)
*Seid Koudia,Selssabil Kadid,Symeon Chatzinotas,Mérouane Debbah*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究有限能量GKP编码的GHZ态在非幺正QuMIMO信道上的传输，通过联合格形整形与接收机设计，揭示了奇异帧几何是超越衰减知识之外的关键资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GHZ态在含相干混合、模式相关衰减与高斯噪声的非幺正多模信道中传输会退化，传统仅依赖衰减知识进行模式解混的策略难以保持量子关联，因此需要探究联合GKP处理能否适应各向异性玻色噪声并明确所需信道信息类型。

Method: 构建耦合模模型刻画相干混合、模式相关衰减与高斯噪声；收发端结合可编程无源网格、量子极限MMSE增益、局部GKP恢复与联合经典校验子解码；分别利用瞬时CSI、当前奇异值加信道统计、以及纯统计信息优化复Givens格形。

Result: 仿真表明联合格形整形优于乘积式GKP处理；在相干基漂移下，完整瞬时CSI更好地保持GHZ态，而奇异值条件化设计与纯统计设计表现相近，因为二者均无法解析当前奇异帧。

Conclusion: 奇异帧几何是区别于衰减知识的关键资源；联合GKP处理的本质是让逻辑格与接收机适配各向异性玻色噪声，而非仅仅反转多模混合。

Abstract: We study transmission of a Greenberger-Horne-Zeilinger (GHZ) state over nonunitary quantum multiple-input multiple-output (QuMIMO) channels using finite-energy Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill (GKP) encoding. A coupled-mode model describes coherent mixing, mode-dependent attenuation, and Gaussian noise. The transceiver combines programmable passive meshes, quantum-limited MMSE gain, local GKP recovery, and joint classical syndrome decoding. We optimize complex-Givens lattices using instantaneous channel-state information (CSI), current singular values with channel statistics, or statistics alone. Simulations show that joint lattice shaping outperforms product GKP processing. Under coherent-basis drift, full instantaneous CSI improves GHZ-state preservation, whereas singular-value conditioned and fully statistical designs perform similarly because neither resolves the current singular frames. These findings identify singular-frame geometry as a critical resource beyond attenuation knowledge where joint GKP processing adapts the logical lattice and receiver to anisotropic bosonic noise rather than merely inverting multimode mixing.

</details>


### [131] [Free fermion models with an operator entanglement growing as $O(\sqrt{t})$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05995)
*Federico Tonetto,Jérôme Dubail*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究含随机时空依赖演化的一维粒子数守恒自由费米子中算符空间纠缠熵（OSEE）的时间演化。结果表明，在随机性存在下，含 Jordan-Wigner 弦的局域自旋算符的 OSEE 随 $\sqrt{t}$ 增长，演化算符的 OSEE 亦为 $\sqrt{t}$，与无随机时演化算符预期的线性增长形成对比；并分析了平移不变随机演化与淬火无序两种情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由费米子系统在无随机性时，局域算符的算符空间纠缠熵最多随时间对数增长。作者希望探究引入随机时空依赖演化后，OSEE 的增长规律是否发生根本改变，特别是对局域算符与演化算符的熵动力学进行区分。

Method: 在两种模型中研究：布朗哈密顿量与砖墙匹配门电路。通过将量子模型映射到对称简单排他过程（symmetric simple exclusion process），在自平均假设下解析计算两类算符的平均熵，并用数值验证该假设。此外比较了保持空间平移不变的时间随机演化与淬火无序两种随机类型。

Result: 随机情形下，含 Jordan-Wigner 弦的局域自旋算符的 OSEE 对所有 Rényi 指数均按 $\mathcal O(\sqrt{t})$ 增长；演化算符的 OSEE 同样按 $\mathcal O(\sqrt{t})$ 增长，而非无随机时的线性增长。平移不变时间随机演化下 OSEE 亦为 $\sqrt{t}$。淬火无序下，哈密顿量演化中局域算符（含 Jordan-Wigner 弦）与演化算符的熵按 $\mathcal O(\log\log t)$ 增长，而砖墙匹配门电路中熵始终有界。

Conclusion: 随机性显著改变了自由费米子系统 OSEE 的动力学标度：从无随机时的对数或线性增长转变为 $\sqrt{t}$ 或 $\log\log t$ 等不同形式，取决于随机类型与模型。该工作揭示了随机演化对算符空间纠缠结构的深刻影响，并通过与对称简单排他过程的映射提供了可解析处理的框架。

Abstract: We study the time evolution of the Operator Space Entanglement Entropy (OSEE) of one-dimensional number-conserving free fermions subject to a random space- and time-dependent evolution. It is known that, in free-fermion systems without randomness, the OSEE of \emph{local} operators grows at most \emph{logarithmically} in time. Here we show that, in the presence of randomness, the OSEE of local spin operators involving a Jordan-Wigner string grows as $\mathcal O(\sqrt t)$, for all values of the Rényi index. We establish this in two models: a Brownian Hamiltonian and a brick-wall matchgate circuit. We also consider the OSEE of the evolution operator, and we show that it also grows as $\mathcal O(\sqrt{t})$, in contrast with the linear growth generically expected without randomness. We analytically compute the average entropies of the two operators by mapping the quantum models to the symmetric simple exclusion process, relying on an assumption of self-averaging which we support numerically. We also consider two other types of randomness: random evolution in time that keeps spatial translation invariance, and quenched disorder. In the former case, we find that the OSEE also grows as $\sqrt{t}$. In the latter case, we find that the entropy grows as $\mathcal O( \log \log t)$ for Hamiltonian evolution both for local operators with a Jordan-Wigner string and for the evolution operator, whereas it remains bounded at all times in the brick-wall matchgate circuit.

</details>


### [132] [Metastability in Quantum Dynamics for Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06000)
*Yuchen Lu,Zherui Chen,Tongyang Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文以量子朗之万动力学(QLD)为例，研究相干演化与耗散如何共同塑造亚稳态，推导谱分离判据并给出加速逼近目标态的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 亚稳态会使系统长时间停留在中间态，从而延迟到达位于全局极小值附近的目标态。现有研究缺乏对相干演化与耗散如何一般性地塑造亚稳态机制的理解。

Method: 将目标函数编码于势能中，以QLD为具体模型，精确求解两态模型，导出以隧穿频率、耗散强度和阻尼不平衡表示的谱分离判据；并将分析推广到多态模型，考察井间弱隧穿下慢布居转移与井内快弛豫的分离。

Result: 两态模型得到精确解与谱分离判据；多态模型表明足够弱的井间隧穿可将慢布居转移与井内快弛豫分离；给出通过改变耗散结构缩短逼近目标态时间的条件；双势阱与多势阱数值实验在固定目标与精度并计入控制耗时的前提下验证了改进。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了相干演化与耗散塑造量子亚稳态的一般机制，并表明调控耗散结构可加速优化动力学中向目标态的收敛。

Abstract: Metastability separates apparent equilibration from true convergence: a dynamical system can remain near an intermediate state long before reaching its stationary state. In quantum dynamics used for optimization, such a plateau can delay progress toward a target concentrated near the global minimizers of a nonconvex potential. In this paper, we initiate the study of the general mechanisms by which coherent evolution and dissipation shape metastability, using quantum Langevin dynamics (QLD) as a concrete example [CLW+25], where the objective function is encoded in the potential and coherent transfer between wells interacts with tunable dissipation. To quantify this interaction, we solve a two-state model precisely and derive a spectral-separation criterion in terms of tunneling frequency, dissipation strength, and damping imbalance. Extending the analysis to multi-state models, we show that sufficiently weak tunneling between wells can separate slow population transfer from fast relaxation within each well. In addition, we establish conditions under which changing the structure of dissipation shortens the time needed to approach a prescribed target state. Numerical experiments on double-well and multi-well potentials demonstrate these improvements while keeping the target and accuracy fixed and accounting for the time spent on control.

</details>


### [133] [Polynomial neural surrogates for designing photonic quantum experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06032)
*Rohit Chaurasiya,Xuemei Gu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出物理启发的多项式神经网络代理模型替代PyTheus光子学模拟器，用于高效预测量子态并实现逆向设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子实验发现依赖模拟器预测实验配置产生的量子态，但PyTheus等模拟器计算开销大，反复调用限制了寻找能生成目标量子态的实验的效率。

Method: 构建物理启发的多项式神经代理模型，其多项式激活函数的灵感来源于图完美匹配与量子态振幅之间的关系；针对四、六、八光子系统分别训练独立代理模型，并与标准多层感知机对比。

Result: 代理模型以更少可训练参数实现更高预测精度；用于GHZ、W和线性簇态的逆向设计；在较大系统中，代理模型比直接用PyTheus优化实现更快的逆向设计。

Conclusion: 将底层物理规律融入神经代理模型可为量子实验设计提供高效途径。

Abstract: Physics simulators can support the discovery of quantum experiments by predicting the states generated by experimental configurations. When these simulators are computationally expensive, repeated simulator calls can limit the search for experiments that generate a desired quantum state. Here, we develop a physics-inspired polynomial neural surrogate for PyTheus, a graph-based quantum-optics simulator, to predict quantum states and use it to design quantum experiments. Its polynomial activations are motivated by the relation between graph perfect matchings and the resulting state amplitudes. We train separate surrogate models for four-, six-, and eight-photon systems and show that they achieve higher prediction accuracy with fewer trainable parameters than standard multilayer perceptrons. We then use the trained surrogates for inverse design of GHZ, W, and linear-cluster states. For the larger systems, the surrogates also enable faster inverse design than direct optimization with PyTheus. These results suggest that incorporating the underlying physics into neural surrogates can provide an efficient approach to quantum experiment design.

</details>


### [134] [Materialised symmetries of 2D translationally invariant codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06036)
*Andrew Li,Stephen D. Bartlett,Andrew C. Doherty,Campbell K. McLauchlan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究二维平移不变（TI）CSS码的对称性空间结构，将无限格点上的对称空间分解为平移不变子空间，给出平面波形式的显式基，并说明如何通过限制到与周期边界兼容的基元素来获得任意矩形周期格点上的基。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期qLDPC码（如双变量自行车码）作为表面码和颜色码的高性能替代方案受到关注，其匹配型解码器依赖"materialised symmetries"并具备类似环面码最小权完美匹配解码器的渐近性能保证，但对称性的数学结构及其在平移和有限尺寸格点限制下的性质研究不足。

Method: 将无限格点上二维CSS TI码的对称空间分解为平移不变子空间，写出平面波形式的显式对称性基；随后将该无限格点基适配到任意矩形周期格点——通过限制到与周期边界兼容的基元素获得对应基，并描述对称空间随矩形尺寸的变化；此外讨论扭曲边界情形。

Result: 给出了对称空间的平移不变子空间分解及平面波形式的显式基；建立了无限格点基与矩形周期格点基之间的限制关系；表明实践中通常无需重新计算分解即可确定扭曲边界上的对称性基；并以gross码等为例展示其对称性。

Conclusion: 该框架为2D CSS TI码的对称性提供了系统的数学描述，可用于匹配型解码器的构造，并适用于gross码等具体码。

Abstract: There has been significant recent interest in near-term qLDPC codes as high-performance alternatives to surface and color codes. One such class of codes is 2-dimensional translationally invariant (TI) codes, such as bivariate bicycle codes, which share similar properties to topological codes. Fundamental objects in the study of such codes are the materialised symmetries, which can be used for the construction of matching-based decoders. These decoders generalise the minimum-weight perfect matching decoder for toric codes and provide similar asymptotic performance guarantees that heuristic decoders such as BP and its variants lack. Despite this, the mathematical structure of symmetries along with their properties under translation and restriction to finite-sized lattices has not been well-studied. We describe a decomposition of symmetry spaces of 2D CSS TI codes on infinite lattices into translation-invariant subspaces which allow us to write an explicit basis of symmetries in a plane-wave-like form. We then describe how to adapt this infinite lattice basis so that any corresponding rectangular periodic lattice basis can be obtained by restriction to basis elements compatible with the periodic boundaries. This allows us to describe how the symmetry space varies with different rectangular dimensions. We illustrate via examples that it is often straightforward in practice to determine a basis of symmetries on twisted boundaries as well, without needing to recompute the decomposition. We comment on the applications of this framework to matching-based decoders and provide examples of symmetries of such codes as the gross code.

</details>


### [135] [Comparing thresholds of XZZX and rotated surface codes under spatio-temporal correlated noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06059)
*Dhruv Koul*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在相关及时空噪声模型下评估XZZX与旋转表面码的性能，采用MWPF解码器原生处理超图，揭示了XZZX码阈值随偏置比呈现非单调依赖的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算研究通常假设独立同分布噪声，而相关噪声与时空误差等复杂模型研究不足；且传统基于图的解码器难以处理偏置定制码中相关误差产生的超边。

Method: 采用最小权重奇偶因子（MWPF）解码器，直接处理症状超图而无需人工边分解，在强Z偏置电路级相关噪声下对XZZX码与旋转表面码进行仿真评估。

Result: 在强Z偏置电路级相关噪声（η=100）下，旋转码阈值降至约0.36%，XZZX码降至约0.70%；XZZX码阈值随偏置比呈非单调依赖，在中等偏置（η≈10）处出现阈值最小值。

Conclusion: 超图感知解码器至关重要，因为偏置定制码的性能对时空相关噪声的几何结构高度敏感。

Abstract: While independent and identically distributed noise is typically assumed in quantum computing research, complex error models such as correlated and spatio-temporal errors remain underexplored. In this paper, the performance of $\mathrm{XZZX}$ and rotated surface codes is evaluated under these complex noise models. Standard graph-based decoders struggle with the hyperedges generated by correlated errors on bias-tailored codes. To address this, we employ the Minimum Weight Parity Factor (MWPF) decoder, which natively processes syndrome hypergraphs without artificial edge decomposition. Our simulations show that under strong Z-biased circuit-level correlated noise ($η=100$), the threshold of the rotated code degrades to $\approx 0.36\%$, while the $\mathrm{XZZX}$ code drops to $\approx 0.70\%$. Furthermore, a distinct non-monotonic threshold dependence is observed for the $\mathrm{XZZX}$ code across different bias ratios. While the $\mathrm{XZZX}$ code effectively absorbs high-density Z-type hyperedges at extreme biases, it exhibits a threshold minimum at moderate biases ($η\approx 10$) because transverse X and Y errors actively bridge the diagonal strings, breaking the code's one-dimensional decoupling symmetry. These findings demonstrate that utilizing hypergraph-aware decoders is critical, as the performance of bias-tailored codes is highly sensitive to the geometric structure of spatio-temporal correlated noise.

</details>


### [136] [Quantum data loading from the learned shared structure of real signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06076)
*Pablo Herrero Gómez,Antonio Jimeno Morenilla,David Muñoz-Hernández,Higinio Mora Mora*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种"量子原生"数据加载器，只需一次性学习数据集的低维描述，即可用一套固定电路、每个信号仅需少量参数完成量子态制备，并在盲复制实验中验证其参数可从小样本推断且不随信号规模增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从经典数据制备量子态的开销可能超过其服务的计算本身，而现有加载器多数需要为每个输入定制电路，通用性与可扩展性差。作者希望利用真实数据集信号之间共享的结构，实现"学一次、重复使用"，从而降低每个信号的参数与门成本。

Method: 设计量子原生加载器：学习数据集的低维描述，用一套由少量数值确定的固定电路来制备每个信号。评估涵盖五个公开数据集、七个视角，与最强结构化加载器在相同门成本下对比每个信号的参数量，并采用预注册盲复制实验检验参数是否可由随机子集推断。

Result: 在相同门成本下达到最强结构化加载器的目标，同时每个信号所需数值减少数倍；这些数值可从随机子集推断。预注册盲复制中，达到全信号精度90%所需子集规模在预设误差范围内保持不变，而信号规模扩大16倍时结构化加载器所需子集持续增加。该方法会拒识无法表示的样本，覆盖案例数少于基线，且不处理心电图数据。

Conclusion: 数据集共享结构可被一次性学习并复用，使固定电路、少参数的量子态制备成为可能，参数量不随信号规模增长，具有可扩展性与可扩展性优势；代价是对无法表示样本的主动拒识与部分数据类型（如心电图）的缺失。

Abstract: Preparing quantum states from classical data can cost more than the computation they serve; most loaders tailor a circuit to each input. Here we show that the signals of a real dataset share structure that can be learned once and reused. Our quantum-native loader learns a low-dimensional description of a dataset and prepares every signal with one fixed circuit set by a few numbers. Across seven views of five public datasets it meets the targets of the strongest structured loader at equal gate cost with several times fewer numbers per signal. These numbers can be inferred from a random subset: in a preregistered blind replication the subset needed to come within ten per cent of full-signal accuracy stayed constant within a prespecified margin as signals grew sixteenfold, whereas the structured loader needed ever more. It declines what it cannot represent, covering fewer cases than that baseline and no electrocardiogram.

</details>


### [137] [Gaussian Universality and Its Breakdown in Tensor-Network Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06080)
*Shi-Tuan Wang,Zidu Liu,Li-Wei Yu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于矩方法解析地给出了张量网络（以矩阵乘积态为重点）学习模型中高斯普适性出现与失效的精确条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯过程极限在描述过参数化机器学习模型中很有效，但其在结构化张量网络架构中的有效性尚不明确。

Method: 提出一种基于矩的分析方法，通过显式的高阶矩偏离有限尺寸界限，推导高斯普适性成立的条件；分别考察大键维极限与大物理维极限，并对比局域与全局可观测量。

Result: 在大键维极限下，局域和全局可观测量的学习模型均收敛到高斯过程，并给出高阶矩偏离的显式有限尺寸界限；在大物理维极限下，高斯普适性不再保持——局域可观测量模型仍呈高斯过程行为，而全局情形表现出持续的非高斯修正。

Conclusion: 张量网络学习中的高斯过程行为不仅由参数数量控制，还受架构标度、可观测量局域性以及谱性质的影响。

Abstract: Gaussian-process limits are powerful in describing overparameterized machine learning models, yet their validity in structured tensor-network architectures remains unclear. Here we analytically present a moment-based approach that identifies precise conditions for the emergence and breakdown of Gaussian universality in tensor-network learning models, with a focus on matrix product states. We prove that in the large bond dimension limit, the learning models with both local and global observables converge to Gaussian processes, with explicit finite-size bounds on higher-order moment deviations. Whereas in the large physical dimension limit, the Gaussian universality no longer persists: while the models with local observables retain Gaussian-process behavior, those global cases exhibit persistent non-Gaussian corrections. Our results reveal that Gaussian-process behavior in tensor-network learning is controlled not only by parameter number, but also by architectural scaling, observable locality, and the spectral properties.

</details>


### [138] [Collective Quantum State Preparation Is Nonadditive](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06113)
*Lin Zhu,Ranyiliu Chen,Xin Wang,Shenggen Zheng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究加权泡利控制下的多副本量子态制备问题，推导了精确的单量子比特制备代价，发现其严格非可加性，并给出维度无关的Rényi熵速度极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态制备是量子计算与控制的基本原语，但其资源代价通常仅针对单个目标态或在独立副本构造下分析，多副本情形下是否存在集体优势尚不明确。

Method: 在加权泡利控制模型下（Pauli积旋转e^{iφP}的代价为旋转角|φ|，与P的权重无关），推导精确的单量子比特制备代价；构造四脉冲集体协议并与两个最优单副本协议比较；利用Rényi熵速度极限进行下界分析。

Result: 四脉冲集体协议以更低代价制备两个T态，严格优于两个独立最优单副本协议；识别出Bloch球上存在集体节省的有限区域，包括始终保持乘积态的轨迹，表明瞬态纠缠并非优势的唯一机制；Rényi熵速度极限给出维度无关的广延下界，并在有限制备速率以下呈指数保真度惩罚。

Conclusion: 结果建立了态合成的多副本几何图像，其中非关联输出仍可从集体控制中获益。

Abstract: Quantum state preparation is a basic primitive of quantum computation and control, but its resource cost is usually analyzed for one target state or under an independent-copy construction. Here we study the many-copy problem under weighted Pauli control, where a Pauli-product rotation $e^{iφP}$ is charged by its rotation angle $|φ|$, independently of the weight of $P$. We derive the exact single-qubit preparation cost and show that it is strictly nonadditive: a four-pulse collective protocol prepares two $T$ states at lower cost than two optimal one-copy protocols. We further identify finite regions of the Bloch sphere with collective saving, including trajectories that remain product throughout, showing that transient entanglement is not the only mechanism behind the advantage. Complementarily, a dimension-independent Rényi-entropy speed limit gives an extensive lower bound for every coherent target and an exponential fidelity penalty below a finite preparation rate. These results establish a many-copy geometry of state synthesis in which uncorrelated outputs can nevertheless benefit from collective control.

</details>


### [139] [Geometric Measure of Entanglement of Rank-2 Mixed Multiqubit States and its Estimation on a Quantum Processor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06127)
*N. A. Susulovska,Kh. P. Gnatenko*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于纠缠与自旋关联的关系，研究混合态的几何纠缠度量，推导秩-2混合态解析表达式，并用量子计算（AerSimulator与IBM量子计算机）设计并验证纠缠检测协议及误差分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合态纠缠的量化在理论与实验上均较困难，而几何纠缠度量与自旋关联之间存在可利用的联系，可用于推导解析结果并设计可行的量子检测方案。

Method: 基于几何纠缠度量与自旋关联的关系，推导由任意多比特纯态构成的二维Hilbert子空间内秩-2混合态纠缠的解析表达式；对特定类混合态（任意多比特态与薛定谔猫态的等概率混合）构造基于自旋关联的几何纠缠检测量子协议；使用AerSimulator与ibm_marrakesh量子计算机进行量化，并分析量子计算误差。

Result: 仿真结果重现了解析依赖关系；量子设备测量结果在估计误差范围内与理论预测良好吻合。

Conclusion: 所提出的解析表达式和量子协议可有效刻画并检测秩-2多比特混合态的几何纠缠，且在当前含噪量子硬件上具备可行性。

Abstract: The geometric measure of entanglement of mixed states is investigated based on its relation to spin correlations. We derive an analytical expression for the entanglement of rank-2 mixed states composed of arbitrary multiqubit pure states within a two-dimensional Hilbert subspace. The dependence of the entanglement of rank-2 mixed multiqubit states on the parameters characterizing arbitrary pure states in the mixture is studied both analytically and using quantum computing. For a particular class of mixed states, namely, equal-probability mixtures of an arbitrary multiqubit state and the Schrödinger cat state, we construct quantum protocols for detecting the geometric measure of entanglement based on its relation to spin correlations. We quantify the entanglement using both the AerSimulator and the ibm_marrakesh quantum computer. The effects of quantum-computing errors are also analyzed. The simulation results reproduce the analytical dependencies, while measurements on the quantum device demonstrate good agreement with theoretical predictions within estimated error bounds.

</details>


### [140] [When Equivalent Quantum Circuits Lose Synthesis Choices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06142)
*Boshuai Ye,Peng Liang,Arif Ali Khan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出"综合可用性(synthesis availability)"概念:高层操作在序列化、OpenQASM交换或转换过程中丢失后,接收编译器无法应用所需的综合方法。作者在Qiskit、TKET、Cirq及MQT管线中系统研究该问题,并提出Verified机制在表示变更前记录高层操作、在验证匹配后重建,从而恢复综合能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子编译器需将量子傅里叶变换、多控X门等高层操作综合为门级电路;但跨编译阶段时电路常被序列化、以OpenQASM交换、在编译器间转换或直接下降为门。这些表示变更虽保持计算等价,却会移除高层操作本身,使接收编译器无法调用所请求的综合方法。现有工作通常只关注功能等价,忽视了高层结构保留对后续综合决策的必要性。

Method: 通过受控实验、仓库分析、项目测试中捕获的电路以及Munich Quantum Toolkit(MQT)管线,研究Qiskit、TKET和Cirq中的综合可用性。定义多种评测条件与恢复运行,统计综合可用性在序列化、反序列化、编译器转换及OpenQASM路径下的存留情况;并提出Verified机制:在表示变更前记录高层操作,仅当校验确认记录仍与最终交付电路匹配时才重建该操作。

Result: 综合可用性在反序列化能恢复高层操作时可经受序列化,在接收编译器支持该操作时方可经受编译器转换,但在所评测的OpenQASM路径中均不能存留。直接OpenQASM 3往返后,全部360个交付条件中请求的综合均无效果,尽管编译仍成功。在MQT管线中,OpenQASM 2交换使八量子比特Grover电路的双量子比特门数增加37.2%。Verified在全部830次恢复运行中均恢复所请求的综合,各操作族的验证时间中位数为2.0至638毫秒;该检查会拒绝所有过期记录,但也会拒绝部分有效记录。

Conclusion: 结果表明,当后续综合选择依赖于保留的高层结构时,仅保持功能等价是不充分的。需要显式记录并校验高层操作,以确保综合可用性不因表示变更而丢失。

Abstract: Quantum compilers synthesize high-level operations, such as the quantum Fourier transform and multi-controlled X gates, into gate-level circuits. Across compilation stages, a circuit may be serialized, exchanged as OpenQASM, converted between compilers, or lowered to gates. These representation changes can preserve computation while removing the high-level operation itself, leaving the receiving compiler unable to apply a requested synthesis method. We call this loss synthesis availability. We study synthesis availability in Qiskit, TKET, and Cirq through controlled experiments, repository analysis, circuits captured from project tests, and a Munich Quantum Toolkit pipeline. Synthesis availability survives serialization when deserialization restores the high-level operation and survives compiler conversion only when the receiving compiler supports that operation. It does not survive the evaluated OpenQASM routes. After a direct OpenQASM 3 round trip, requested synthesis has no effect in all 360 delivered conditions even though compilation succeeds. In the MQT pipeline, OpenQASM 2 interchange increases the two-qubit gate count by 37.2 percent for an eight-qubit Grover circuit. We also introduce Verified, which records high-level operations before a representation change and reconstructs them only when a check confirms that the record still matches the delivered circuit. Verified restores requested synthesis in all 830 recovery runs, with median verification times from 2.0 to 638 ms per operation family. The check rejects every stale record but also some valid records. These results show that preserving functional equivalence alone is insufficient when later synthesis choices depend on retained high-level structure.

</details>


### [141] [What Does It Cost to Simulate a Quantum Sentence Classifier? An Energy and Compute Perspective on Near-Term QNLP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06176)
*Kishlay Kashyap,Sandipan Ganguly*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文实测了二分类情感任务上变分量子分类器（VQC）在态矢量模拟器上的计算开销与精度表现。在27组配置中，VQC仅在6组单种子对比中优于匹配的逻辑回归基线，多种子复核后仅1组优势超过配对波动且统计检验不显著；同时VQC训练墙钟时间比经典拟合慢886–21,127倍（中位数3,158倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期待量子自然语言处理（QNLP）实验多依赖经典模拟器运行，模拟器成本构成该领域实际算力负担的一部分，但现有精度报表通常不体现这一开销。作者希望以可复现的可行性测量方式，量化VQC在真实任务上的模拟成本与相对经典基线的精度，填补"只报精度、不报成本"的空白。

Method: 在二分类SST-2情感分类任务上训练变分量子分类器（VQC），使用PennyLane态矢量模拟器，构建27组受控配置网格：3种平衡训练集规模（N=200、500、1000）×3种量子比特数（4、6、8）×3种电路深度。每组VQC与在同一PCA降维输入上的逻辑回归进行匹配对比，并以完整TF-IDF逻辑回归作为未压缩参照。每组配置在三个随机种子下重复运行，统计精度优势并进行固定验证集上的配对检验；同时测量墙钟训练时间，并用CodeCarbon估计能耗与CO₂。

Result: VQC在27组单种子对比中有6组超过其匹配基线。经另外两个种子复跑后，6组中仅1组保持大于配对种子间波动的正的平均优势，且在固定验证集上的配对检验未能确证该优势。训练时间方面，VQC比匹配的经典拟合慢886–21,127倍（中位数3,158倍）；量子比特从4增至8约使模拟时间翻倍，在测试范围内每步成本可较好地用参数数量的线性函数近似。CodeCarbon的能耗与CO₂估计为次要结果：其隐含功率近乎恒定为约41 W，除运行时间外提供的信息有限，作者未据此构建能耗比。

Conclusion: 该研究是一项范围受限（单一数据集、表示、拟设、模拟器与CPU环境）的可复现可行性测量，而非对QNLP的一般性结论。其核心启示是：在经典模拟器上，VQC相对匹配经典基线的精度优势脆弱且随种子波动，而训练成本高出数个数量级，因此QNLP报告的精度表应同时披露模拟器成本；能耗估计因功率近似恒定，附加值有限。

Abstract: Near-term quantum natural language processing (QNLP) experiments often run on classical simulators, so simulator cost is part of the field's practical compute burden, yet accuracy tables do not show it. We measure that cost for a variational quantum classifier (VQC) on binary SST-2 sentiment classification, using PennyLane's state-vector simulator over a controlled grid of 27 configurations: three balanced training-set sizes (N = 200, 500, 1000), three qubit counts (4, 6, 8), and three circuit depths. Each VQC is compared with logistic regression on the same PCA-reduced input; full TF-IDF logistic regression gives an uncompressed reference. The VQC beats its matched baseline in 6 of 27 single-seed comparisons. After reruns at two further seeds, only 1 of these 6 keeps a positive mean advantage larger than its paired seed-to-seed variability, and paired tests on the fixed validation set do not establish it. VQC training is 886-21,127 times slower in measured wall-clock time than the matched classical fit (median 3,158 times); going from 4 to 8 qubits roughly doubles simulator time, and within the tested range per-step cost is well approximated by a linear function of the parameter count. CodeCarbon energy and CO2 estimates are secondary: they imply an almost constant power of about 41 W, so they add little beyond runtime, and we do not build an energy ratio from them. The study is narrow (one dataset, representation, ansatz, simulator, and CPU environment) and is a reproducible feasibility measurement, not a general verdict on QNLP.

</details>


### [142] [The superb supersinglet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06183)
*Gabriele Cobucci,Armin Tavakoli,Alexander Bernal,Shishir Khandelwal*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究高维全反对称态（supersinglets）的纠缠性质与应用，证明其纠缠在噪声下异常鲁棒，提出基于集体自旋与随机测量的见证方法以认证纠缠深度和维度，并证明其可实现海森堡标度的梯度传感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维多体系统可承载量子比特系统无法实现的纠缠形式，其中全反对称态（supersinglets）在任意集体局域幺正变换下保持不变，具有独特对称性，但其纠缠特性与量子信息应用尚未被系统探索。

Method: 采用凸优化分析纠缠的鲁棒性；发展基于集体自旋测量或随机测量的见证（witness）方法，用于认证纠缠的深度与维度；并将supersinglets用于高维梯度传感方案，分析其标度。

Result: 凸优化表明supersinglets的核心纠缠特征对噪声高度鲁棒，在某些松弛条件下显著优于若干典型纠缠态；见证方法可同时认证纠缠深度与维度；作为高维梯度传感资源，supersinglets可实现海森堡标度。

Conclusion: supersinglets是一类异常鲁棒且多用途的多体纠缠态，兼具显著对称性与强纠缠，并在量子传感中具有切实应用潜力。

Abstract: Multipartite systems of high-dimensional particles can host forms of quantum entanglement inaccessible to qubit systems. A particularly fascinating class is formed by the fully antisymmetric states, also known as supersinglets, which possess a remarkable symmetry: they are invariant under arbitrary collective local unitaries. Here, we explore their entanglement properties and establish their quantum information applications. Using convex optimisation, we show that central entanglement features of supersinglets are remarkably robust to noise, substantially surpassing the robustness of several paradigmatic entangled states under certain relaxations. We then develop witness methods, based on collective-spin or randomised measurements, that certify both the depth and dimensionality of their entanglement. Finally, we identify supersinglets as a natural resource for high-dimensional gradient sensing and prove that they enable Heisenberg scaling. Together, these results reveal supersinglets as an exceptionally robust and versatile class of multipartite entangled states, combining striking symmetry and strong entanglement with concrete potential for quantum sensing.

</details>


### [143] [A robust framework for the comprehensive characterisation of parametric down-conversion sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06194)
*Denis A. Kopylov,Michael Stefszky,Christine Silberhorn*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种器件无关的PDC压缩光源表征框架，将光源视为黑盒产生的混合多模高斯态，通过模式未分辨测量给出态纯度的下界，实现对真实压缩光源质量的整体评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维量子光网络需要单模、纯的压缩态，但光学元件的不完美使实际生成态非纯且含内禀模式结构。现有基于联合谱强度或归一化二阶关联函数的表征技术均假设被测态为纯态，导致对光源质量的评估不完整甚至误导。因此需要一种不依赖纯态假设的表征方法。

Method: 针对信号光与闲频光可区分的PDC光源，将其视为产生混合多模高斯态的黑盒，建立器件无关的表征框架，并推导态纯度的下界，该下界可通过模式未分辨（mode-unresolved）的测量在实验上确定。

Result: 给出了态纯度的可实验测定的下界，无需对非线性器件及其周围光学网络建立详细模型，即可对真实压缩光源质量进行整体评估，并可作为优化光源的实用基准。

Conclusion: 该器件无关框架突破了现有方法依赖纯态假设的局限，为面向先进量子应用的压缩光源提供了无需详细器件模型的实用质量评估与优化基准。

Abstract: Reliable verification of squeezed-light sources is essential for photonic quantum technologies. High-dimensional quantum optical networks demand single-mode, pure squeezed states which, in principle, can be generated through careful engineering of parametric down-conversion-based (PDC) sources. However, due to the imperfections of optical components, the generated state is never pure and commonly contains internal mode structure. Existing characterisation techniques, based on either joint spectral intensity or normalized second-order correlation functions, work under the assumption that the probed state is pure, and therefore can result in incomplete or misleading assessment of the source quality. Here, we develop a device-agnostic characterisation framework for PDC-based sources with distinguishable signal and idler fields. Treating the source as a black box producing a mixed multimode Gaussian state, we find a lower bound for the state purity, which can be experimentally determined via mode-unresolved measurements. Our approach enables a holistic assessment of realistic squeezed-light source quality without requiring a detailed model of the nonlinear device and its surrounding optical network, providing practical benchmarks for optimizing sources for state-of-the-art quantum applications.

</details>


### [144] [Improved Adaptive Estimation of Quantum Partition Functions with Heisenberg Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06197)
*Yufei Wang,Daniel Stilck França,Samuel Slezak*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出量子算法以海森堡标度估计n量子比特哈密顿量的对数配分函数，通过自适应缓慢变化冷却调度，将热态纯化制备调用降至Õ(n^{5/4}/ε)，在可控温度寄存器的热场双态情形进一步降至Õ(n/ε)并证明其块编码查询最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 估计量子哈密顿量的配分函数是量子统计物理与量子化学的核心问题。已有方法主要依赖非自适应策略，调用热态纯化制备的次数随系统规模增长较快，缺乏对查询次数的精细优化。本文旨在以海森堡标度达到加性误差ε，同时最小化热态纯化制备与块编码的调用次数。

Method: 输入为H的块编码、在指定逆温度下制备热场双态的酉算子及其逆与受控版本，以及自适应缓慢变化冷却调度。系统规模假设0≼H≼hI，h=O(n)，块编码归一化α=Θ(h)。恒温情形下证明长度O(√n)的调度存在，并可由重叠估计生成。核心工具为递归倍增恒等式，将每个调度增量表示为热场双态重叠对数与短虚时间步（由量子奇异值变换结合方差缩减技术实现）的加权和。若热场双态可按温度寄存器受控制备，则用量子逆二项对数估计器结合量子均值估计进一步降低查询数。

Result: 给定调度与经典选取的温度，以Õ(n^{5/4}/ε)期望调用次数估计对数配分函数，较非自适应策略改进n^{1/4}。若热场双态可受控于温度寄存器，查询次数降至Õ(n/ε)，并证明该标度在块编码查询上最多相差多对数因子意义下最优。讨论了在一维配分函数估计中的端到端复杂度。

Conclusion: 本文给出具有海森堡标度的量子对数配分函数估计算法，通过自适应冷却调度与递归倍增恒等式实现查询复杂度的显著改进，并在可受控制备热场双态的设定下达到块编码查询的最优标度。

Abstract: We give quantum algorithms that estimate the log partition function of an $n$-qubit quantum Hamiltonian to additive error $ε$ with Heisenberg scaling, while minimizing calls to thermal-state purification preparation. The input is a block encoding of $H$, unitaries preparing thermofield-double states at chosen inverse temperatures, with their inverses and controlled versions, and an adaptive slowly-varying cooling schedule. The system-size bounds below assume $0\preceq H\preceq hI$ with $h=O(n)$ and block-encoding normalization $α=Θ(h)$. For constant inverse temperature, we show that such a schedule of length $O(\sqrt n)$ always exists and can be generated from overlap estimates with $\widetilde O(\sqrt n)$ further preparations.
  Given such a schedule and classically chosen temperatures, we estimate the log partition function with $\widetilde O(n^{5/4}/ε)$ expected calls to state preparation and to the block encoding, improving by $n^{1/4}$ on non-adaptive strategies. To achieve our result, we use a recursive-doubling identity that expresses each schedule increment as a weighted sum of logarithms of thermofield-double overlaps and of short imaginary-time steps implemented by quantum singular value transformation combined with previously developed variance reduction techniques. If thermofield doubles can be prepared controlled on a temperature register, a quantum inverse-binomial log estimator combined with quantum mean estimation reduces both query counts to $\widetilde O(n/ε)$. We show that the latter scaling is optimal in block-encoding queries up to polylogarithmic factors and discuss end-to-end complexities for one-dimensional partition-function estimation.

</details>


### [145] [Casimir force screening by a quantum Boltzmann plasma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06202)
*Petr Brandyshev,Maksim Koniushenko,Yury Budkov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文发展了有限温度场论框架，描述两块理想导体板之间夹有双组分量子玻尔兹曼等离子体（置于均匀介质背景中）的卡西米尔相互作用，并给出主导量子修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典卡西米尔-屏蔽图像无法刻画热德布罗意波长带来的量子效应，需要建立统一处理量子运动（虚时世界线）与电磁场涨落（高斯近似）的有限温度场论描述。

Method: 采用虚时世界线表示带电粒子的量子运动，电磁场在Gaussian近似下处理；通过对配分函数分解出纵向、横向响应贡献及无经典对应的量子诱导源项，并考察静态长波极限下纵向与横向介电函数（响应函数）的展开结构。

Result: 配分函数含纵向与横向响应项及量子诱导源项。经典极限下恢复常规卡西米尔-屏蔽图像：静态纵向模被德拜屏蔽，横向电磁部门不受经典可动电荷影响。有限热德布罗意波长修正非零Matsubara响应，给出对力的主导量子修正。静态长波极限下，纵向介电函数经主导量子修正后与简并电子气的Lindhard响应具有相同展开结构（对应k_TF↔κ_D、k_F↔λ_dB^{-1}）；静态横向响应复现高温Landau轨道抗磁磁化率的主项。

Conclusion: 该场论框架自洽连接经典屏蔽图像与量子修正，并揭示量子玻尔兹曼等离子体纵向响应与简并电子气Lindhard响应的对应关系，以及横向响应对高温Landau抗磁性的再现。

Abstract: We develop a finite-temperature field-theoretical description of the Casimir interaction between two perfectly conducting boundaries with the intervening gap filled by a two-component quantum Boltzmann plasma embedded in a homogeneous dielectric background. The quantum motion of the charged particles is represented by imaginary-time worldlines, while the electromagnetic field is treated in the Gaussian approximation. The resulting partition function contains distinct longitudinal and transverse response contributions and an additional quantum-induced source term with no classical counterpart. In the classical limit the theory reduces to the usual Casimir-screening picture: the static longitudinal mode is Debye screened, whereas the transverse electromagnetic sector is unaffected by classical mobile charges. Finite thermal de Broglie wavelengths modify the nonzero Matsubara response and produce the leading quantum correction to the force. In the static long-wavelength limit, the longitudinal dielectric function has, through the leading quantum correction, the same expansion structure as the Lindhard response of a degenerate electron gas under the correspondence $k_{TF}\leftrightarrowκ_D$ and $k_F\leftrightarrowλ_{dB}^{-1}$, while the static transverse response reproduces the leading high-temperature Landau orbital diamagnetic susceptibility.

</details>


### [146] [Interferometric braiding of non-Abelian anyons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06203)
*Francesco Debortoli,Fabian Grusdt*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于量子杂质（可寻址内部自旋态）的方案，用于编织非阿贝尔任意子，测量刻画其非阿贝尔统计的R矩阵与F矩阵，并以Ising和Fibonacci任意子为例在Ramsey干涉仪中展示任意子特性，进而实现常规量子比特到拓扑量子比特的编码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非阿贝尔任意子的操控是拓扑量子计算的基础，也是拓扑保护量子存储的核心，但如何在实验上直接测量并利用其非阿贝尔统计仍是关键问题。

Method: 利用具有可寻址内部自旋态的移动量子杂质构建编织非阿贝尔任意子的协议；在Ramsey干涉测量中观测R、F矩阵元，并针对Ising与Fibonacci任意子具体分析；在此基础上设计常规-拓扑量子比特编码器。

Result: 给出测量R、F矩阵元的具体实验方案；在提出的Ramsey干涉仪中，Ising和Fibonacci任意子的任意子性质直接显现；实现将飞行量子比特的量子信息转移至拓扑保护量子存储。

Conclusion: 量子杂质方案为探测非阿贝尔统计、编织任意子及实现拓扑量子存储与量子信息编码提供了可实验实施的途径。

Abstract: The manipulation of non-Abelian anyons is a foundational ingredient for topological quantum computing and underlies topologically protected quantum memories. Here we analyze a quantum-impurity-based protocol for braiding non-Abelian anyons. Exploiting mobile impurities with addressable internal spin states, we provide a concrete protocol to experimentally measure the matrix elements of the $R$ and $F$ matrices that characterize the non-Abelian statistics of anyons. In particular, focusing on the case of Ising and Fibonacci anyons, we illustrate how the anyonic nature of the excitations emerges directly in the Ramsey interferometer we propose. Building on this framework, we further introduce a conventional-to-topological qubit encoder to transfer quantum information from conventional flying qubits into a topologically protected quantum memory.

</details>


### [147] [Unitary RQL Equals RQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06231)
*Quinten Tupker*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明在标准门集 Γ={H,T,CNOT} 下，单边错误无界误差量子对数空间与含中间测量的对应类相等：RQLΓ = RQULΓ，保持多项式时间、对数空间与 no-instance 上恰好零接受率；并将证明推广到更广的有限可逆门集及一类代数门集，同时给出量子对数空间验证中消除测量的构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中间测量可使量子计算丢弃信息并复用工作空间；若将所有测量推迟，则需存储完整测量历史，未必能保持对数空间。Fefferman 与 Remscrim 已证明双边有界错误下测量可被消除，但单边错误（no-instance 上零接受）是否同样成立仍为公开问题，本文旨在解决该问题并推广至更一般的门集与验证场景。

Method: 将 Girish、Raz 与 Zhan 的密度矩阵倍增方法（density-matrix doubling）推广到一般量子信道，涵盖重置（resets）与经典存储；在此基础上构造测量消除过程，并针对具备精确逆的更广有限门集（含适当超越元门）以及指定代数门集族证明门集无关性。另采用独立的历史检查（history-checking）构造处理量子对数空间验证中的测量消除。

Result: 证明 RQLΓ = RQULΓ（Γ={H,T,CNOT}），保持多项式时间、对数空间及 no-instance 上恰好零接受；测量消除亦适用于更广的有限可逆门集和含超越元门的情形，并给出指定代数门集族的门集无关性；另有 QMAL1,G = QUMAL1,G 在显式门假设下成立，即对数空间量子验证中可消除测量且保持完美完备性（perfect completeness）。

Conclusion: 中间测量可在单边有界错误下被消除而不损失对数空间与零错误接受性质，该结论可推广到一般信道与更广门集，并延伸至对数空间量子验证；这统一并强化了 Fefferman–Remscrim 的双边错误结果与 Girish–Raz–Zhan 的密度矩阵方法。

Abstract: Intermediate measurements let quantum computations discard information and reuse their workspace. Deferring all measurements can require storing their entire history, which need not preserve logarithmic space. Fefferman and Remscrim proved that measurements can nevertheless be eliminated with two-sided bounded error, and asked whether the same holds with one-sided error [FR21]. We prove RQLΓ = RQULΓ for the standard gate set Γ = {H, T, CNOT}, preserving polynomial time, logarithmic space, and exactly zero acceptance on no-instances. Our proof extends the density-matrix doubling method of Girish, Raz and Zhan [ GRZ21 ] to general channels, including resets and classical memory. We also prove measurement elimination for broader finite gate sets with exact inverses, including suitable gates with transcendental entries, and establish gate-set independence for a specified family of algebraic gate sets. A separate history-checking construction proves QMAL1,G = QUMAL1,G under explicit gate assumptions: measurements can also be eliminated from logarithmic-space quantum verification while preserving perfect completeness

</details>


### [148] [Probing Quantum Anomalous Hall Transport Under Microwave Irradiation Using a Topological Circulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06245)
*Athul Ashok,Frank Jin,Nick Du,Luis A. Martinez,Jenny Zhou,Sean O'Kelley,Zachary J. -R. Espley,Gang Qiu,Kang L. Wang,Dong-Xia Qu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用量子反常霍尔拓扑环形器，通过泵浦-探测实验研究微波光子对边缘磁等离激元(EMP)输运的扰动，并借助非厄米模式杂化在奇异点(EP)附近实现增强的微波响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EMP是探测拓扑量子材料中手性电荷动力学与非互易微波输运的重要平台，但EMP传播的微小扰动在传统微波散射测量中信号较弱，难以检测。作者希望寻找一种能够增强微波响应的探测方案。

Method: 采用量子反常霍尔拓扑环形器构建耦合的EMP-谐振器系统，开展泵浦-探测微波测量：改变泵浦频率，观测EMP透射的变化；并利用该系统的非厄米模式杂化，在奇异点(EP)附近探测扰动响应，与远离EP处进行对比。

Result: 响应呈现强频率选择性：某些泵浦频率下微波辐照显著改变EMP透射，而在包括4 GHz与7 GHz在内的特定频率下透射几乎不变。在EP附近，泵浦引起的透射幅度变化约为远离EP处的两倍，表明扰动响应被增强。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了手性EMP输运、微波光子诱导扰动与非厄米动力学之间的相互作用，并表明拓扑环形器可作为增强微波光谱与传感的潜力平台。

Abstract: Edge magnetoplasmons (EMPs) provide a platform for probing chiral charge dynamics and nonreciprocal microwave transport in topological quantum materials. However, detecting small perturbations to EMP propagation remains challenging because their signatures in conventional microwave scattering measurements can be weak. Here, we investigate microwave-photon-induced perturbations of EMP transport using a quantum anomalous Hall topological circulator. Pump--probe measurements reveal a strongly frequency-selective response: while microwave irradiation substantially modifies the EMP transmission at several pump frequencies, the transmission remains nearly unchanged at selected frequencies, including 4 and 7 GHz. We further exploit the non-Hermitian mode hybridization of the coupled EMP-resonator system to probe the response near an exceptional point (EP). The pump-induced change in transmission magnitude near the EP is approximately twice that observed away from the EP, demonstrating an enhanced microwave response to perturbations. These results reveal the interplay among chiral EMP transport, microwave-photon-induced perturbations, and non-Hermitian dynamics, and demonstrate the potential of topological circulators as a promising platform for enhanced microwave spectroscopy and sensing.

</details>


### [149] [High-Fidelity Remote Graph State Preparation for Blind Quantum Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06247)
*Jiawei Cai,Rex Fleur,Benedikt Tissot,Wolfgang Löffler,Tzula B. Propp*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出远程图态制备（RGSP）框架，利用单个高维光子qudit直接制备任意图态，降低甚至消除服务器端纠缠操作需求，并支持任意拓扑上的盲量子计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有远程态制备（RSP）协议只能制备可分离态，需要远程服务器后续执行纠缠门；MBQC依赖纠缠图态，因而现有方案在资源与操作上效率不足。

Method: 将多个量子比特及其图连通性编码进光子的结构化相位轮廓，从一个高维光子qudit直接制备任意图态；并在累积光纤相位漂移下提出"最高权重优先"的模式重排策略。

Result: 在合理噪声模型下，所得态保真度与图拓扑无关；累积光纤相位漂移下，该保真度经"最高权重优先"重排策略显著提升；RGSP支持任意拓扑上的减少SWAP通用盲量子计算，例如六比特量子傅里叶变换的qubit开销从标准brickwork拓扑的420个RSP qubit降至48个RSP qubit。

Conclusion: RGSP是一种资源高效、拓扑不变的量子原语，适用于量子安全云计算。

Abstract: Measurement-based quantum computation (MBQC) relies on entangled graph states, yet existing remote state preparation (RSP) protocols prepare only separable states, requiring subsequent entangling gates on the remote server. Here, we introduce Remote Graph State Preparation (RGSP), a framework that prepares arbitrary graph states directly from a single high-dimensional photonic qudit. By encoding multiple qubits and their graph connectivity into the photon's structured phase profile, RGSP can reduce or, for small computations, completely eliminate the need for server-side entangling operations among qubits. We show that under a reasonable noise model the resulting state fidelity is independent of the graph topology. Under cumulative fiber phase drift, we demonstrate that this fidelity is significantly enhanced by a ``highest-weight-first'' mode reordering strategy. Finally, we show that RGSP enables reduced-SWAP universal blind quantum computing on arbitrary topologies, reducing qubit overhead e.g., for a six-qubit Quantum Fourier Transform from $420$ RSP qubits with a standard brickwork topology to $48$ RSP qubits. These results establish RGSP as a resource-efficient, topology-invariant primitive for quantum-secured cloud computing.

</details>


### [150] [Suppressing Frequency Collisions in a Quantum Processor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06264)
*Kuljeet Kaur,Xuexin Xu,Mohammad H. Ansari*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将量子比特频率分配中的频率拥挤与碰撞机制建模为Max-k-Cut问题，提出基于哈密顿量的框架与簇拼接协议，并在多种架构上验证了DC-QAOA、标准QAOA与模拟退火的优化表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导量子处理器中频率拥挤（量子比特跃迁频率的密集排布）会导致串扰、泄漏和旁观者依赖误差，从而限制处理器性能。因此需要将微观相互作用物理与处理器级频率规划相连接，实现系统化的硬件感知优化。

Method: 提出基于哈密顿量的框架，将两体和三体碰撞机制映射为量子比特频率分配上的Max-k-Cut问题；为处理更大晶格，引入簇拼接协议，将大晶格分解为重叠子图并将其局部频率分配协调为全局一致配置；在多种量子比特架构上测试该框架，并对比DC-QAOA、标准QAOA与模拟退火。

Result: 该框架在多种量子比特架构上抑制了主要碰撞惩罚，并揭示出硬件残余串扰；在陡峭碰撞惩罚附近，反绝热项改善了QAOA的优化效果，而模拟退火在测试实例上取得了最低代价。

Conclusion: 结果表明，该硬件感知优化将微观相互作用物理与处理器级频率规划联系起来，为可扩展的软硬件协同设计提供了系统化的求解器与基准测试方法。

Abstract: Frequency crowding, the close spacing of qubit transitions, limits superconducting quantum processors through crosstalk, leakage, and spectator dependent errors. We introduce a Hamiltonian based framework that maps two- and three-body collision mechanisms onto a Max-k-Cut problem on qubit frequency allocation. In order to make larger lattices tractable, we introduce a cluster-stitching protocol that decomposes larger lattices into overlapping subgraphs and reconciles their local frequency assignments into globally consistent configurations. We tested our proposed framework across multiple qubit architectures to suppress dominant collision penalties by revealing a hardware residual crosstalk. We benchmark digitized counterdiabatic QAOA (DC-QAOA), standard QAOA, and simulated annealing. Counterdiabatic terms improve QAOA optimization near steep collision penalties, while simulated annealing achieves the lowest costs on the tested instances. These results indicate that our hardware-aware optimization connects microscopic interaction physics to processor-level frequency planning. These results enable a systematic solver for benchmarking of scalable software hardware co design.

</details>


### [151] [Adiabatic tracking and optimal schedules for classical and quantum Gibbs-state preparation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06268)
*Pedro C. S. Costa,Mauro E. S. Morales,Dong An,Kaur Kristjuhan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对经典与量子优化中的Gibbs态制备，提出离散绝热框架，给出马尔可夫链追迹定理、基于谱隙与能量涨落的调度策略，并证明量子Szegedy行走相较经典松弛实现二次加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gibbs态制备是采样与优化算法的核心，但有限时间下量子绝热方法的误差控制和调度设计缺乏统一分析，需要刻画演化分布与瞬时稳态的距离并确定加速条件。

Method: 对有限时间非齐次可逆马尔可夫链建立追迹定理，将误差分解为历史误差的收缩与新误差的产生；针对Gibbs路径推导阶复杂度界与gap/涨落自适应调度（弱阻尼下𝜷̇∝δ/V）；并将离散量子绝热定理应用于温度依赖的Szegedy量子行走，分析其本征相位隙Δ_w=Θ(√δ)及有限差分项。

Result: 得到显式步数复杂度界与由谱隙δ和能量涨落V(β)控制的调度；证明√δ谱放大仅在gap自适应调度下转化为二次改进，量子绝热代价为O(δ_min^{-1/2})，而经典松弛标度为O(δ_min^{-1})。

Conclusion: 在相应正则条件下，温度依赖Szegedy量子行走可实现相对经典松弛的二次加速，且该加速依赖于合适的谱隙自适应调度设计。

Abstract: We develop a discrete adiabatic framework for Gibbs-state preparation in classical and quantum optimisation algorithms. For finite time-inhomogeneous reversible Markov chains, we derive a tracking theorem that bounds the distance from the evolving distribution to the instantaneous stationary state by combining two effects: contraction of previously accumulated error by the Markov dynamics and the new error generated as the stationary distribution changes from one step to the next. For Gibbs paths, this leads to explicit step-complexity bounds and to gap- and fluctuation-adapted schedules controlled by the Markov-chain spectral gap and the energy-fluctuation scale $V(β)=\sqrt{\operatorname{Var}_{π_β}(E)}$. In the weak-damping regime, the resulting schedule takes the form $\dotβ\proptoδ(β)/V(β)$. We then apply the discrete quantum adiabatic theorem to temperature-dependent Szegedy quantum walks. The quantum-walk eigenphase gap satisfies $Δ_w=Θ(\sqrtδ)$, where $δ$ is the spectral gap of the underlying Markov chain. By analysing the finite-difference terms entering the theorem, we show that this square-root spectral amplification translates into a quadratic improvement in the gap dependence only for suitably gap-adapted schedules. Under the corresponding regularity conditions, the quantum adiabatic cost scales as $O(δ_{\min}^{-1/2})$, compared with the classical $O(δ_{\min}^{-1})$ relaxation scale.

</details>


### [152] [Two-Sided Bounds on Ground State Properties in Lattice Gauge Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06272)
*Eloïc Vallée,Ariel Kelman,Julius Mildenberger,Jordi Tura,Patrick Emonts*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于半定规划层次结构的框架，可给出格点规范理论基态能量的严格下界，并将其传播至Wilson圈和介子弦等扩展可观测量，实现对量子模拟器的认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 格点规范理论是研究粒子物理与凝聚态中规范场的重要工具，但蒙特卡洛算法在符号问题下失效，而量子与经典变分方法仅能给出基态能量的上界，且精度依赖所选试探波函数。因此需要一种能给出严格下界与可认证区间的方法。

Method: 采用半定规划（SDP）层次结构，逐级逼近并给出越来越好的基态能量下界；进一步将该下界传播到Wilson圈、介子弦等扩展可观测量的严格界。应用于含动态费米子物质的(1+1)维$\mathbb{Z}_2$理论及其(2+1)维纯规范版本进行数值验证。

Result: 在两个系统中均获得基态能量的可认证区间：一维129格点系统的相对展宽为0.04%；二维97格点（$7 \times 8$格点）系统的相对展宽为2.08%。此外还给出跨多格方的Wilson圈及介子弦幅值的严格上下界。

Conclusion: 该方法既可充当探索多体物理的计算显微镜，又可作为认证量子模拟器结果的手段，为规范理论提供严格的能量与观测量界限。

Abstract: Gauge theories describe fundamental interactions in the standard model of particle physics and build effective theories in condensed matter physics. Being notoriously hard to simulate, these theories are commonly regularized as lattice gauge theories which can be evaluated with high precision through Monte Carlo algorithms or variational methods. However, Monte Carlo algorithms are not applicable in all regimes due to the sign problem; while variational methods, quantum and classical alike, only give upper-bounds on the ground-state energy. Their precision crucially depends on the chosen ansatz. Here, we present a framework based on a hierarchy of semidefinite programs to yield increasingly good lower-bounds on the ground-state energy and propagate these bounds to extended observables like Wilson loops and mesonic strings. We demonstrate the numerical capabilities by applying the algorithm to a (1+1)-dimensional $\mathbb{Z}_2$ theory with dynamic fermionic matter and its pure-gauge version in (2+1)-dimensions. In both cases, we obtain certified intervals for the ground-state energy. For a one-dimensional system with 129 sites, the certified interval has a relative spread of $0.04\%$. For a two-dimensional system with 97 sites (corresponding to a $7 \times 8$ lattice), the relative spread is $2.08\%$. Beyond energy estimates, the proposed method provides rigorous bounds on both short- and long-range observables such as Wilson loops over multiple plaquettes and the magnitude of the mesonic string. The method can be both used as a computational microscope into many-body physics and a way to certify results of quantum simulators.

</details>


### [153] [Robust subspace designs and the power of a unique small quantum witness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06302)
*Simon Apers,Roman Edenhofer,Benjamin Mathieu-Bloise,Partha Mukhopadhyay*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"鲁棒子空间设计"概念，给出多项式规模的概率构造与超多项式规模的显式构造，并将其用于证明空间有界量子Valiant-Vazirani定理及零化度测试的随机化归约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Valiant-Vazirani定理通过随机化归约隔离唯一见证，量子情形下对应量是接受见证子空间的维数，需要新的组合工具来隔离唯一量子见证，而普通子空间设计不足以处理"接近"给定子空间的情形。

Method: 在Guruswami-Xing子空间设计基础上引入定量扩展：要求族中不能有太多子空间包含接近任意固定维数子空间的方向，即鲁棒子空间设计。给出多项式规模的概率构造，以及超多项式规模的显式非平凡构造。

Result: 证明空间有界量子Merlin-Arthur协议（完美完备性且有接受间隙）中唯一见证隔离的量子Valiant-Vazirani定理；给出良条件零化度测试到该协议类的随机化归约；并用普通子空间设计以更简证明恢复Allender-Beals-Ogihara的经典C_=L包容结果。

Conclusion: 鲁棒子空间设计是隔离量子唯一见证的有效组合工具，推广了子空间设计并连接经典与量子复杂性，为空间有界量子协议与零化度测试提供统一框架。

Abstract: The concept of subspace designs was introduced by Guruswami and Xing (STOC'13), and explicit constructions were given by Guruswami and Kopparty (FOCS'13). These are families of subspaces that have small intersection with any given subspace of a fixed dimension. We introduce \emph{robust subspace designs}. Informally, these are a quantitative extension in which we demand that not too many subspaces of the family contain directions that lie close to any given subspace of a fixed dimension. We give a probabilistic construction of such a robust subspace design of polynomial size, as well as a non-trivial explicit construction of superpolynomial size.
  Our main application of this new concept is a quantum space-bounded variant of the Valiant-Vazirani theorem (Theor.Comput.Sci.'86), which shows that restricting $\mathsf{NP}$-complete problems to instances with at most one accepting witness preserves hardness under randomized reductions. For quantum witnesses, the analogous quantity is the dimension of an accepting witness subspace. We use our probabilistic construction of robust subspace designs to isolate a unique witness for space-bounded quantum Merlin-Arthur protocols with perfect completeness and an acceptance gap outside their perfectly accepting subspace.
  As further applications, we give a randomized reduction of well-conditioned nullity testing to space-bounded quantum Merlin-Arthur protocols with perfect completeness. Using a similar idea, we find that ordinary subspace designs allow us to recover the classical $\mathsf{C_= L}$ containment of Allender, Beals, and Ogihara (STOC'96) for general nullity testing through a simpler proof.

</details>


### [154] [Quantum Leakage from Shamir Sharing with Correlated Resources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06350)
*Isaac Barouch Essayag,Aryeh Lev Zabokritskiy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究Shamir秘密共享在量子泄漏场景下的安全性：每个设备用其份额从与秘密和共享随机性无关的初始量子资源生成一个量子比特。作者证明，当重构需要足够大比例的份额时，任意两个秘密对应的泄漏态之间迹距离指数级小；该结论适用于满足上算子混合条件的固定平稳链，以及由固定有限量子存储和本原传输信道生成的资源，允许设备边缘的任意扩展作为敌手侧信息，并对大于份额数的素域一致成立。对于独立设备-参考对，作者确定了重构需要全部份额时的最优指数基：量子比特为2√2/π，经典比特为2/π。通过与乘积态比较选定边缘，将独立对的界转化为相关资源的安全性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子密码学中，设备可能泄漏关于秘密的信息。Shamir秘密共享是经典门限方案，但其在量子泄漏模型下的安全性需要严格刻画。本文旨在量化当每个设备用其份额生成量子比特时，泄漏态与秘密之间的关联程度，从而为量子环境下的秘密共享提供可证明的安全保证。

Method: 将每个设备的份额输入到与秘密和共享随机性无关的初始量子资源中，产生一个量子比特，形成泄漏态。通过分析独立设备-参考对，确定最优指数基（量子比特2√2/π，经典比特2/π）以给出定量输入。利用固定平稳链的上算子混合条件和有限量子存储/本原传输信道生成资源，证明任意两个秘密泄漏态之间的迹距离指数级小。通过比较选定边缘与乘积态，将独立对的界推广到相关资源。

Result: 证明了当重构需要足够大比例的份额时，任意两个秘密的泄漏态之间迹距离指数级小；结论对满足上算子混合条件的固定平稳链及有限量子存储生成的资源成立，允许任意设备边缘扩展作为敌手侧信息，并对大于份额数的素域一致成立。确定了重构需要全部份额时独立设备-参考对的最优指数基：量子比特为2√2/π，经典比特为2/π，均允许任意有限参考。通过乘积态比较，将独立对的界转化为相关资源的安全保证。

Conclusion: Shamir秘密共享在量子泄漏模型下具有指数级安全性：当重构需要足够多份额时，泄漏态几乎不区分不同秘密。独立设备-参考对的最优指数基被精确确定，并可通过边缘比较推广到相关资源，为量子环境下的门限秘密共享提供了严格的安全界。

Abstract: We study leakage from Shamir secret sharing when each device uses its share to produce one qubit from an initial quantum resource independent of the secret and sharing randomness. We prove exponentially small trace distance between the leakage states of any two secrets, when reconstruction requires a sufficiently large fraction of the shares, for fixed stationary chains satisfying an upper operator mixing condition and for resources generated by fixed finite quantum memories with primitive transfer channels. These guarantees permit arbitrary extensions of the device marginal as adversary side information and hold uniformly over prime fields larger than the number of shares. For independent device--reference pairs, which provide the quantitative input to the argument, we determine the optimal exponential base when reconstruction requires all shares: $2\sqrt2/π$ for a qubit and $2/π$ for a classical bit, in both cases allowing arbitrary finite references. A comparison of selected marginals with product states converts the bounds for independent device--reference pairs into security guarantees for the correlated resources.

</details>


### [155] [Adaptive complete quantum search with unknown number of solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06364)
*Raj Alexandru Guţoiu,Bogdan-Călin Ciobanu,Pantelimon George Popescu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究解数量未知时的非结构化数据库搜索问题，提出统一的贝叶斯自适应框架，在经典采样、Grover搜索与广义定点量子搜索之间动态选择，并给出最优性证明与转换阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Grover算法在标记元素数量已知时可实现二次加速，但当解的数量未知且需找出全部解时，问题变得更具挑战性。已有方法缺乏对不同搜索机制（经典、Grover、广义定点）组合的系统性比较与最优性分析，也缺少在单位执行时间内最大化成功概率的自适应策略。

Method: 将经典、Grover与广义定点量子搜索实例统一为自适应贝叶斯方法，在每个搜索阶段选择使单位执行时间期望成功概率最大的策略；利用解数量的概率分布的积分近似来确定经典采样与量子搜索之间的最优切换点；并通过数值模拟验证。

Result: 在已知解数量且远小于数据库规模的情形下，证明所提方法在所考虑的搜索模型内渐近最优；证明当经典验证时间不超过量子振幅放大时间的25%时，对任意解数量分布，混合经典与Grover的方法优于广义定点搜索；在该区间之外，给出了定点搜索相对于最佳Grover策略可实现的最大改进上界。

Conclusion: 该工作为解数量未知的非结构化搜索提供了统一的自适应贝叶斯最优框架，明确了不同量子搜索范式的适用区间，并给出了经典与量子搜索之间的最优转换点，理论结果得到数值模拟支持。

Abstract: Unstructured database search is a fundamental problem that requires finding one or more elements satisfying a specific property within a set of candidates. While Grover's algorithm provides a quadratic speedup for finding a marked element when the number of solutions is known, finding all solutions when their number is unknown presents additional challenges. In this paper, we thoroughly analyze this problem by considering all the different regimes of combinations of classical, Grover and generalized fixed-point quantum search instances. We unify these into an adaptive Bayesian approach that maximizes the expected success probability per unit execution time at each searching instance. For a problem with a known number of solutions, much lower than the database size, we prove that our proposed approach is asymptotically optimal within the considered search model. Furthermore, we prove that for any probability distribution characterizing the likely number of solutions, in regimes where the classical verification time is at most 25% of the quantum amplitude amplification time, hybrid classical and Grover approaches are preferred to generalized fixed-point. Outside of this regime, we derive an upper bound on the maximal improvement achievable by fixed-point search compared to the best Grover-based score. Additionally, we determine the optimal transition point between classical sampling and quantum searching using an integral approximation of the probability distribution of the number of solutions. The analytical results are also supported by numerical simulations of the proposed algorithm and by comparisons between the generalized fixed-point and Grover-based search policies.

</details>


### [156] [Value of spatially multiplexed SNSPDs with photon-number-resolving capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06388)
*Fabio Auriemma,Mikkel Ejrnaes*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立组合与贝叶斯框架，量化提升复用探测系统局域光子数分辨能力所带来的信息增益，证明多态SNSPD阵列可显著放松二元阵列对探测器数的二次缩放要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年研究表明单个SNSPD可突破传统开/关探测机制而具备光子数分辨能力，但既有理论多针对二元探测器，缺乏对具有任意可分辨态数目的复用SNSPD系统的统一描述与信息量评估。

Method: 将二元器件的响应张量公式推广，推导具有任意可分辨态数目的复用SNSPD系统的概率响应张量；通过二项损失卷积引入有限效率，并单独评估暗计数影响；据此导出广义N光子探测器保真度、贝叶斯光子数重建及其重建保真度；并以2、3、4态、不同探测器数的复用系统为实例进行计算。

Result: 提高局域光子数分辨能力可系统性地同时提升探测器保真度与贝叶斯光子数重建性能，且显著放松了纯二元复用阵列中探测器数目与可分辨光子数之间所需的二次缩放关系。

Conclusion: 该框架为多态光子数分辨探测器阵列提供了定量的信息价值评估，说明提升单探测器局域分辨态数是比单纯增加探测器数量更高效的设计路径。

Abstract: We develop a combinatorial and Bayesian framework to quantify the information gained by increasing the local photon-number resolution of multiplexed detection systems based on superconducting nanowire single-photon detectors (SNSPDs). Motivated by recent studies that attribute photon-number resolving capabilities to a single SNSPD overcoming the typical on/off detection mechanism, we generalize the existing formulation for the binary devices by deriving the probability response tensor of multiplexed systems based on SNSPDs with an arbitrary number of resolvable states. We include finite efficiency through binomial loss convolution and, separately, assess the impact of dark counts. From this response tensor we derive a generalized N-photon detector fidelity, a Bayesian photon-number reconstruction, and the corresponding reconstruction fidelity. We particularize this formulation to study 2-, 3- and 4-state multiplexed systems with different numbers of detectors and show that increasing local photon-number resolution systematically improves both detector fidelity and Bayesian photon-number reconstruction. This substantially relaxes the quadratic scaling between detector number and resolvable photon-number required by purely binary multiplexed arrays. These results provide a quantitative value-of-information assessment for multi-state photon-number-resolving detector arrays.

</details>


### [157] [When is the Clifford hierarchy generalized semi-Clifford?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06402)
*Rongbiao Thomas Wang,Bobby Zixuan Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究Clifford层级中半Clifford与广义半Clifford结构随层级k和比特数n的变化，给出充要条件、反例构造，并提出更精细的n_c-广义半Clifford结构猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画Clifford层级$\mathcal{C}_k(n)$的结构，明确其何时为半Clifford或广义半Clifford，并推广Zeng-Chen-Chuang的猜想。

Method: 通过分析层级k与比特数n的关系，证明半Clifford的充要条件，构造k,n≥5的反例，并引入n_c-广义半Clifford门的定义与形式刻画。

Result: $\mathcal{C}_k(n)$为半Clifford当且仅当$k\le2$、$n\le2$或$k=3,n\le6$；为广义半Clifford当$k\le4$或$n\le3$，并在$k,n\ge5$时给出反例。$n=4,k\ge5$时$\mathbb{Q}(i)$上的门（模标量）均为广义半Clifford。n_c-广义半Clifford门恰为$\sum_x|\pi(x)\rangle\langle x|\otimes V_x$形式（模两侧Clifford门）。

Conclusion: 猜想$\mathcal{C}_k(n)$为广义半Clifford当且仅当$k\le4$或$n\le4$；并猜想$k\ge3$时每个门具有n_c-广义半Clifford形式且$V_x\in\mathcal{C}_{k-3}(n-n_c)$，细化Zeng-Chen-Chuang猜想。

Abstract: We study semi-Clifford and generalized semi-Clifford structures in the Clifford hierarchy as a function of hierarchy level $k$ and qubit number $n$. We show that $\mathcal{C}_k(n)$ is semi-Clifford if and only if $k\le2$, $n\le2$, or $k=3$ and $n\le6$. We also establish that $\mathcal{C}_k(n)$ is generalized semi-Clifford when $k\le4$ or $n\le3$ and construct counterexamples when $k,n \geq 5$. For $n=4$ and $k\ge5$, we show that every gate defined over $\mathbb{Q}(i)$ up to a scalar is generalized semi-Clifford, reduce the general case to a single level, and propose a strategy to prove our conjecture that $\mathcal{C}_k(n)$ is generalized semi-Clifford if and only if $k\le4$ or $n\le4$. Lastly, we go beyond this structure by introducing $n_c$-generalized semi-Clifford gates, which we show are exactly the gates of the form $\sum_x|π(x)\rangle\langle x|\otimes V_x$ up to Clifford gates on both sides, where $π$ permutes the computational basis states of $n_c$ control qubits. We conjecture that for $k\ge3$ every gate in $\mathcal{C}_k(n)$ has this form for some $n_c\ge1$ with every $V_x\in\mathcal{C}_{k-3}(n-n_c)$, refining the conjecture of Zeng, Chen, and Chuang.

</details>


### [158] [Lindblad equation for the quantum Rabi model in the deep strong coupling regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06417)
*Daniel Holst,Patrick Potts,Peter Samuelsson*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了开放量子Rabi模型在微扰深强耦合区（deep strong coupling）的Lindblad形式量子主方程，并在Schrieffer-Wolff变换框架下结合旋波（secular）与奇异耦合（singular-coupling）近似，通过与Bloch-Redfield方程对比验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放量子Rabi模型在深强耦合区的谱结构接近简并且近似谐振，传统Bloch-Redfield方程在简并/近简并情形下易出现非正定等问题，亟需构造具备完全正定性的Lindblad型主方程来描述该区域的耗散动力学与稳态性质。

Method: 在Schrieffer-Wolff变换后的参考系中，联合使用旋波近似与奇异耦合近似，推导出开放量子Rabi模型的Lindblad形式量子主方程；将该方程与Bloch-Redfield方程进行基准对比，考察若干可观测量的时间演化与稳态特性，并在极限情形下给出解析结果。

Result: 所推导的Lindblad主方程与Bloch-Redfield方程在多个可观测量的时间演化和稳态性质上吻合良好（excellent agreement），并在特定极限情况下获得了可解析处理的结论。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种在深强耦合近简并区可靠描述开放量子Rabi模型耗散动力学的Lindblad主方程方法，验证了其与Bloch-Redfield结果的一致性，并具备在极限情形下解析求解的优势。

Abstract: We derive a quantum master equation in Lindblad form for the open quantum Rabi model in the perturbative deep strong coupling regime, where the spectrum is close to degenerate and harmonic. The Lindblad form is obtained by combining secular and singular-coupling approximations, working in a Schrieffer-Wolff transformed frame. We benchmark our equation against a Bloch-Redfield one, analyzing the time-evolution and steady state properties of several observables and find excellent agreement. Analytical results are obtained in limiting cases.

</details>


### [159] [A Practical Introduction to VQE: Methods, Challenges, and Applications in Physics and Chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06448)
*Jannis Ehrlich,Felix Rupprecht,Konstantin Lamp,Elias Walter,Alejandro D. Somoza,Manuel Enns,Christian Elsässer,Birger Horstmann,Daniel F. Urban*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述变分量子本征求解器（VQE）作为近中期量子硬件的混合量子-经典方法，系统梳理其算法组件、性能影响因素，并为新入门者提供实践指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VQE已成为在近中期量子硬件上解决电子结构与材料科学问题的核心混合量子-经典方法。来自物理、化学和量子信息领域的研究者在使用VQE时，面临问题编码、拟设构造、经典优化和测量策略等众多算法选择，需要结构化的综述帮助做出明智决策。

Method: 本文采用结构化综述方式：对VQE的各个组件（问题编码、拟设构造、经典优化、测量策略）进行系统梳理，并讨论噪声特性、误差缓解技术及硬件相关电路考量等实际影响因素。部分内容基于作者的早期经验。

Result: 提供了VQE组件的结构化概述，识别出对新手尤为重要的方面，并总结了实际工作流程中常见的挑战，辅以物理与量子化学示例加以说明。

Conclusion: 本文旨在为研究基于VQE的量子模拟的研究者提供易于理解的入门指南，支持其在方法与底层硬件持续演进的过程中做出明智决策。

Abstract: The Variational Quantum Eigensolver (VQE) has become a central hybrid quantum-classical method for addressing electronic structure and materials science problems on near-term quantum hardware. As researchers across physics, chemistry, and quantum information engage with VQE, they encounter a wide range of algorithmic choices, including problem encoding, ansatz construction, classical optimization, and measurement strategies to choose from. This article provides a structured overview of these components and discusses practical factors that influence VQE performance in real settings, such as noise characteristics, available error-mitigation techniques, and hardware-dependent circuit considerations. Drawing partly on our early experiences, we highlight aspects that may be especially relevant for newcomers and outline common challenges observed in practical workflows. Our aim is to offer an accessible entry point for researchers approaching VQE-based quantum simulation and to support informed decision-making as the method and underlying hardware continue to evolve, which we support by discussion of examples from physics and quantum chemistry.

</details>


### [160] [A programmable quantum photonic platform integrating coherent emitters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06451)
*Hanna Salamon,Mikkel T. Mikkelsen,Zhe Liu,Peter Lodahl,Severin Krüger,Arne Ludwig,Leonardo Midolo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出并实现了一个可编程量子光子平台，可在低温下同时控制高相干半导体量子点单光子源与可重构光子元件，完成单光子产生、双光子干涉与集成发射器的共振透射/反射测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光量子技术需要将高质量量子光源与可编程光子电路集成以实现片上量子信息处理；半导体量子点是领先的量子光源平台，但其与低温兼容、可编程光子电路的单片集成仍具挑战。

Method: 实现单片集成的可编程量子光子平台，在低温（cryogenic）条件下对芯片上的有源组件进行独立的电学与光学寻址，从而在保持相干非经典光发射的同时实现量子点单光子发射器与可重构光学元件的并行/同时工作。

Result: 展示了多种量子光子功能：纯单光子产生、双光子干涉，以及集成发射器的共振透射与反射测量，证明有源组件可并发运行且相干非经典光发射得以保持。

Conclusion: 该工作标志着从孤立量子光学器件迈向可编程量子光子硬件平台的一步，为将相干量子发射器与大规模光子功能结合提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Photonic quantum technologies require the integration of high-quality quantum light sources with programmable photonic circuitry to enable on-chip quantum information processing. Semiconductor quantum dots have emerged as a leading platform for quantum light generation, yet their monolithic integration with cryogenic-compatible, programmable photonic circuitry remains challenging. Here we demonstrate a programmable quantum photonic platform that enables simultaneous control of highly coherent quantum dot-based single photon emitters and reconfigurable optical elements at cryogenic temperatures. System-level integration enables individual electrical and optical addressing of active components on a single chip, allowing their concurrent operation while preserving coherent non-classical light emission. We demonstrate multiple quantum-photonic functionalities, including pure single-photon generation, two-photon interference, and resonant transmission and reflection measurements of integrated emitters. These results mark a step from isolated quantum-optical devices towards programmable quantum-photonic hardware platforms capable of combining coherent quantum emitters with large-scale photonic functionality.

</details>


### [161] [The right entanglement for perfect nonlocality and zero-error communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06512)
*Laura Mančinska*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过构造 Flagged Magic-Square game，证明了一类非局域博弈的完美策略必然需要且能自检验（self-test）一个特定的非最大纠缠态（Schmidt 系数正比于 (1,√2,√2,√2,√2)），并据此得到经典信道的零误差通信分离：纠缠可在单次使用中传输十条消息，而任何有限维最大纠缠资源至多传输九条。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非局域博弈中的刚性（rigidity）与 self-testing 研究多集中于最大纠缠态；而对于非最大纠缠态的完美策略是否也存在结构性强制机制、以及最大纠缠是否在所有纠缠辅助通信任务中普遍最优，尚缺乏明确反例。本文旨在给出一个具体机制与反例。

Method: 在标准 Magic-Square game 基础上增加一个 "flag" 结果以及每位参与者两个额外问题，构造 Flagged Magic-Square game；该博弈存在使用 Schmidt 系数正比于 (1,√2,√2,√2,√2) 的完美策略。通过直接代数证明，任意维度下的最大纠缠策略获胜概率均与 1 有正距离；同时证明该博弈对该特定非最大纠缠态具有 self-testing 性质。进一步建立通用的 game-to-channel reduction，将博弈模型中的分离转化为经典信道上的单次零误差通信问题，并应用于该博弈。
