Result: 该博弈存在完美策略，所用纠缠态的 Schmidt 系数正比于 (1,√2,√2,√2,√2)；最大纠缠策略的获胜概率在所有维度上一致有界小于 1；博弈能 self-test 该特定非最大纠缠态，从而给出一个证明特定非最大纠缠态的双方可伪心灵感应（pseudo-telepathy）博弈实例。经由 game-to-channel reduction 得到的经典信道中，纠缠允许在单次使用内零误差传输十条消息，而任何有限维最大纠缠资源至多传输九条。

Conclusion: 通过给刚性测量几何添加恰当约束，可强制实现特定 Schmidt 比例，并提供基于其他刚性伪心灵感应博弈构建 self-test 的模块化路径。结果表明，最大纠缠态并非经典噪声信道上零误差通信的普遍最优纠缠资源。

Abstract: We identify a structural mechanism by which perfect play in a nonlocal game requires and self-tests a specific non-maximally entangled state. To this end, we construct a Flagged Magic-Square game by extending the standard Magic-Square game with an additional ``flag'' outcome and two additional questions for each player. The game admits a perfect strategy using a state with Schmidt coefficients proportional to $(1,\sqrt{2},\sqrt{2},\sqrt{2},\sqrt{2})$. We give a direct algebraic proof that maximally entangled strategies have winning probability bounded away from one, independently of dimension. Moreover, the game self-tests this state, providing an example of a two-party pseudo-telepathy game that certifies a specific non-maximally entangled state. Our construction illustrates how adding suitable constraints to a rigid measurement geometry can enforce specific Schmidt ratios and provides a modular route to further self-tests based on other rigid pseudo-telepathy games.
  We establish a general game-to-channel reduction that transfers separations in the game model to one-shot zero-error communication over classical channels. Applied to the Flagged Magic-Square game, it yields a classical channel for which entanglement permits the zero-error transmission of ten messages in a single use, whereas any finite-dimensional maximally entangled resource permits at most nine. Thus, maximally entangled states are not a universally optimal entanglement resource for zero-error communication over classical noisy channels.

</details>


### [162] [Causal memory and structural complexity of a quantum space series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06519)
*Thomas J. Elliott*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文用量子计算力学刻画量子空间序列的因果生成记忆，证明存在等距生成的极小记忆模型，其规范MPS表示给出最小记忆维度与最小记忆熵，后者恰为过去-未来二分下的纠缠熵，从而将纠缠解释为因果生成的不可约记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何衡量一个量子过程比另一个更复杂，是量子复杂性科学的核心问题。经典复杂性科学以因果生成所需记忆来刻画结构，但缺乏面向量子空间序列的完整量子化框架。作者希望建立操作性方案，把量子结构、记忆、熵与纠缠统一起来。

Method: 将经典计算力学推广到量子空间序列（不与生成器回互作用的量子输出序列）。针对平稳、有记忆且允许有限维本原因果模型的量子空间序列，构造可等距生成输出、无需丢弃环境的极小记忆模型，并导出规范矩阵乘积态（MPS）表示。

Result: 规范MPS表示同时确定最小记忆维度与最小记忆熵；最小记忆熵等于过去-未来二分界面的纠缠熵。该类内还证明基本熵率为零、不存在因果不对称性、量子马尔可夫阶为无穷。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了量化内在量子结构的操作框架，揭示因果生成、复杂性、记忆、熵与纠缠之间的深层联系，并赋予纠缠以“因果生成不可约记忆”的操作解释。

Abstract: What makes one quantum process more complex than another? We adopt an operational approach inspired by classical complexity science, which characterises structure through the memory required for causal generation. We develop a fully-quantum extension of computational mechanics for quantum space series: Sequences of quantum outputs that do not return to interact with their generator. For stationary, memoryful quantum space series admitting finite-dimensional, primitive causal models, we show that a memory-minimal model can be chosen to generate the outputs isometrically, without a discarded environment. The resulting canonical matrix product state representation determines both the minimum memory dimension and the minimum memory entropy. The latter is precisely the entanglement entropy across the past-future bipartition, giving entanglement an operational interpretation as irreducible memory for causal generation. Within this class, we further establish a vanishing fundamental entropy rate, the absence of causal asymmetry, and infinite quantum Markov order. These results establish an operational framework for quantifying intrinsic quantum structure, revealing deep connections between causal generation, complexity, memory, entropy, and entanglement.

</details>


### [163] [Classical Simulation of Lossy Gaussian Boson Sampling beyond Square-Root Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06526)
*Youngrong Lim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明用经典光替代有损输入来模拟高斯玻色采样的方法存在精度极限（存活光子数不超过√N），并提出通过近似探测过程而非输入来突破该极限，使平均存活光子数可达N^{2/3}甚至N^{3/4}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光子损耗使高斯玻色采样更易被经典模拟。传统方法将有损输入替换为经典光，但其精度随存活光子数增长而失效，作者希望确定该方法的适用边界并寻找能突破此边界的模拟策略。

Method: 首先证明经典光替换方法的尺度极限：对显式干涉仪，任何具有非负Glauber-Sudarshan P表示的态（无论高斯与否、无论是否关联）在存活光子数超过√N后误差都会增大。随后提出替代方案，即近似探测而非输入：采样器在探测之间精确演化量子态，每次探测后用具有相同协方差的Gaussian态替换条件态。

Result: 经典光替换方法的平均存活光子数在固定压缩和精度下最多随√N增长，这是该方法的极限。新方法对任意干涉仪的总变差误差为O(Nη³)，期望成本为多项式级，平均存活光子数可随N^{2/3}增长；当所有输入模式等量压缩时，通过另一种对构造可达N^{3/4}。

Conclusion: 经典光替换模拟存在√N的固有限度，而近似探测（用同协方差Gaussian态替换条件态）可有效突破该极限，实现更高存活光子数下的精度保持，为高斯玻色采样经典模拟提供了更优方案。

Abstract: Photon loss makes Gaussian boson sampling easier to simulate classically. A standard approach replaces the lossy input by classical light, and it stays accurate while the mean number of surviving photons grows no faster than the square root of the number $N$ of squeezed inputs at fixed squeezing and accuracy. We prove that this scale is a limit of replacement by classical light. For explicit interferometers, no state with a nonnegative Glauber--Sudarshan $P$ representation, Gaussian or not and however correlated, keeps the error in the photon count distribution small beyond this scale. We then surpass this limit by approximating the detection instead of the input. The sampler evolves the state exactly between detections and, after each detection, replaces the conditional state by a Gaussian with the same covariance. For every interferometer its total variation error is $O(Nη^3)$ at polynomial expected cost, where $η$ is the photon survival probability. The mean surviving photon number can therefore grow as $N^{2/3}$ at fixed accuracy. A separate pair construction reaches $N^{3/4}$ when every input mode is equally squeezed.

</details>


### [164] [Closed-Form Arbitrary-Gap Rényi Nonadditivity Above Any Positive Order via Direct Products of Free Groups and High-Girth Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06533)
*Guocheng Zhen,Ranyiliu Chen,Xin Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出了具有实代数元素的量子信道的闭式有限维构造，可在任意正Rényi阶上一致地产生任意大的最小输出Rényi熵可加性违背，并给出具体量子比特数上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最小输出Rényi熵的可加性是否成立是量子信息理论中的重要问题；已有工作（如Shou-Gorshkov 2026）给出了违背构造，但参数规模较大，需更高效的显式构造。

Method: 基于自由群直积与固定域高围长图的有限实现，构造实代数元素的量子信道与Bell输入；对给定p0>0与g>0，取r=⌈2g⌉，用15r个输出量子比特和O(r2^{30r}+r²(1+p0^{-1}))个输入量子比特，使所有p∈[p0,∞]同时满足可加性间隙大于g；并在von Neumann阶进行专门化构造。

Result: 对每个p0、g均构造出单一信道与Bell输入，使全部p∈[p0,∞]的可加性间隙大于g；相比Shou-Gorshkov 2026，输出量子比特数更少，且固定p0下随g增长输入量子比特渐近上界更小；von Neumann阶给出间隙大于1/1024的显式信道，使用37608898个输入量子比特与8个输出量子比特。

Conclusion: 该构造在闭合形式、有限维度与显式参数方面改进了最小输出Rényi熵可加性违背的已知结果。

Abstract: We give a closed-form finite-dimensional construction of quantum channels with real algebraic entries exhibiting arbitrarily large violations of minimum-output Rényi entropy additivity uniformly above any prescribed positive Rényi order, using a different finite realization based on direct products of free groups and fixed-field high-girth graphs. More precisely, for every $p_0>0$ and $g>0$, we construct one channel and one Bell input for which the additivity gap is greater than $g$ simultaneously for all $p\in[p_0,\infty]$. With $r=\lceil2g\rceil$, the construction uses $15r$ output qubits and $O(r2^{30r}+r^2(1+p_0^{-1}))$ input qubits. Compared with the stated parameters of Shou and Gorshkov \cite{ShouGorshkov2026}, our construction uses fewer output qubits for every target gap and has a smaller asymptotic input-qubit bound as the gap grows with the positive cutoff fixed. At the von Neumann order, a separate specialization gives an explicit channel with gap greater than $1/1024$ using $37608898$ input qubits and $8$ output qubits.

</details>


### [165] [The Mermin-Peres Magic Square Game With Three or More Players](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06534)
*Itamar Lorian,J. E. Avron*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 将Mermin-Peres魔方游戏推广到N名玩家（方阵替换为N维超立方体），证明最优经典策略胜率恒为8/9，而N≥3时量子策略胜率降至1/3至3/4之间，量子优势因单配性而丧失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mermin-Peres魔方游戏是展示量子优势（量子策略优于经典策略）的经典范例。本文希望探究当玩家数量从2增加到N时，这种量子优势是否能够保持，从而考察量子非局域性与玩家数量之间的关系。

Method: 将Mermin-Peres魔方游戏推广至N名玩家，把原来的方阵结构替换为N维超立方体；分析该推广游戏的最优经典策略，并将N=2时的量子策略推广至N≥3的情形，计算其获胜概率。

Result: 最优经典策略的获胜概率对所有N均保持8/9不变；对于N≥3，推广后的量子策略获胜概率介于1/3与3/4之间，低于经典策略的8/9。

Conclusion: 对于N≥3的多玩家推广，量子策略不再优于经典策略，量子优势的丧失是量子纠缠单配性（monogamy）的直接后果。

Abstract: We present a generalization of the Mermin-Peres magic square game to $N$ players, in which the square is replaced by an $N$-dimensional hypercube. We show that the winning probability of the optimal classical strategy remains $8/9$ for all $N$, while for $N \ge 3$, a generalization of the quantum strategy for $N = 2$ achieves a winning probability between $1/3$ and $3/4$. This loss of quantum advantage for $N \ge 3$ is a consequence of monogamy.

</details>


### [166] [Symmetry and AI-assisted discovery of magic-state factories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06535)
*Shubham P. Jain,Adam Wills,Shraddha Singh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出对称性与AI辅助搜索方法，通过统一二元矩阵表述和语言模型代理，实现了距离三及以上的魔态蒸馏协议搜索，产出699个工厂类（564个新），含最低开销指数的纯T工厂[[63,11,3]]、[[850,128,6]]及首个γ<1的[[1715,287,6]]。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 魔态蒸馏是容错量子计算的主要资源开销，其成本强烈依赖工厂的失败率（随输入魔态数量增长）。尽管对称性受限方法已使距离二搜索可行，但距离三及以上在中等输入规模下仍难以实现，亟需新的高效搜索方法。

Method: 提出对称性与AI辅助搜索方法：建立统一二元矩阵表述，同时涵盖triorthogonal码搜索与直接电路搜索；证明距离至少三等价于非零且两两不同的syndromes，从而将syndrome选择与兼容输出门搜索解耦；利用群对称性和语言模型代理限制syndrome搜索空间，再辅以确定性求解与独立验证。

Result: 搜索产出699个工厂类，其中564个为新发现，涵盖纯T态工厂及含T、CS、CCZ组合纠缠输出的工厂。纯T工厂[[63,11,3]]（γ=1.589）和[[850,128,6]]（γ=1.057）分别在≤100和≤1000输入协议中达到已知最低开销指数；[[1715,287,6]]（γ=0.998）是已知最小且γ<1的纯T工厂。

Conclusion: 该方法将对称性、AI代理与确定性求解结合，有效拓展了高距离魔态蒸馏协议搜索空间，并提供了上下文目录与搜索简报供社区训练自有代理。成果开始构建面向量子社区的开源魔态蒸馏协议活跃仓库，服务实际容错量子计算需求。

Abstract: Magic-state distillation is a major resource cost in fault-tolerant quantum computing. The cost of a magic-state factory depends strongly on its failure rate, which grows with the number of input magic states. Although symmetry-restricted methods have recently made distance two searches tractable, distance three and above have remained elusive at moderate input counts. We develop symmetry- and AI-assisted methods to search this regime. We present a unified binary-matrix formulation encompassing both triorthogonal-code and direct circuit searches. We show that distance at least three is equivalent to nonzero, pairwise distinct syndromes, separating the choice of syndromes from the search for compatible output gates. We restrict the syndrome search using group symmetry and language-model agents, followed by deterministic solving and independent verification. Our searches yield 699 factory classes, including 564 new ones. These include factories for pure-T states and factories with entangled outputs comprising combinations of T, CS, and CCZ magic states. The pure-T factories [[63, 11, 3]] and [[850, 128, 6]] achieve the lowest overhead exponents we know among protocols with at most 100 and 1000 inputs, respectively, with $γ= 1.589$ and $γ= 1.057$. Our [[1715, 287, 6]] factory, with $γ= 0.998$, is the smallest known pure-T factory with $γ< 1$. We also provide a context directory of search briefs and campaign notes with which readers can train their own agents and tailor the search to their requirements. With these results, we begin constructing an active, open-source repository of magic-state distillation protocols for the quantum community, supplemented by our methods and data, for the practical fault-tolerant quantum computing regime.

</details>


### [167] [Products of unitaries as continuous evolutions at finite precision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06541)
*Michael Jarret*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出离散化连续酉演化与有限维酉乘积双向对应的方法，将离散绝热定理归约为连续定理，误差为O(1/T)，并应用于量子线性系统求解获得O(κ/δ)步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将连续酉演化与离散酉乘积建立双向对应，以便把离散绝热定理归约到成熟的连续绝热理论，避免直接依赖离散绝热定理。

Method: 在合适有限划分上用受调控哈密顿量的截断采样乘积逼近演化；对时钟进行取整把T个单步酉样本的乘积识别为连续演化（差两个半步相位）；利用每步内相消界其与原演化的算符范数距离为O(1/T)；并构造满足比较假设的厄米对数路径。

Result: 在导数有界、谱宽有界、特征值差远离2π非零整数倍且关于T一致的假设下，误差界为O(1/T)；固定公共自由谱弧提供厄米对数路径；对条件数κ的正定量子线性系统，比较与连续绝热理论给出泄漏δ下步数O(κ/δ)的重新标度量比特化行走。

Conclusion: 该对应将连续模拟与连续绝热界转移至相位校正乘积，得到显式加性O(1/T)误差并保留各绝热定理假设，且这些乘积无需离散绝热定理。

Abstract: I present a short approach to discretizing continuous unitary evolution. It gives a correspondence in both directions between continuous unitary evolutions and products of unitaries in finite dimensions, and I use it to reduce discrete adiabatic theorems to continuous ones. At fine steps, holding truncated samples of a regulated Hamiltonian on a suitable finite partition gives an approximating product. Conversely, rounding the clock identifies a product of $T$ unit-step samples with a continuous evolution, up to two half-step phases. I bound its operator-norm distance from the original evolution by $O(1/T)$ through cancellation within each time step. This assumes bounded first and second derivatives, bounded spectral width, and eigenvalue differences bounded away from nonzero multiples of $2π$, uniformly in $T$. For a unitary walk with these derivative bounds, a fixed common free spectral arc supplies a Hermitian logarithm path satisfying the comparison hypotheses. Continuous simulation guarantees then transfer to the phase-corrected product, and continuous adiabatic bounds to the product itself, with an explicit additive $O(1/T)$ error and each adiabatic theorem's hypotheses retained. For positive definite quantum linear systems of condition number $κ$, this comparison and continuous adiabatic theory give a rescaled qubitized walk with $O(κ/δ)$ steps for leakage $δ$, matching the scaling previously obtained through a discrete adiabatic theorem. These products need no discrete adiabatic theorem.

</details>


### [168] [Mid-circuit measurements and feedforward implement every unitary exactly in constant depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06551)
*Chenfeng Cao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 利用带前馈的中途测量，可将精确n量子比特酉合成的最优最坏情况量子深度从Θ(n)降至O(1)，代价是使用O(n4^n·log(n+2))个总量子比特与O(1)轮奇偶前馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注的核心问题是精确n量子比特酉合成所需的最优最坏情况量子深度。传统上该深度为Θ(n)，作者希望探明引入中途测量与前馈后能否突破此下界，并刻画深度与量子比特数之间的权衡。

Method: 方法上，作者构造了确定性电路：使用单量子比特测量、O(1)轮奇偶前馈，以及O(n4^n log(n+2))总量子比特，所有辅助比特初末均为|0>。他们仅对Nehoran与Yuen近似解的矩阵误差进行传送，用一个精确制备的辅助态编码该误差，并通过相干干涉将其作用加回；在算子范数误差2^{-n}下，一步振幅放大即足够。相干扇出电路也达到相同常数深度。

Result: 结果显示：带前馈中途测量可将最坏情况深度降至O(1)，而无前馈的测量与重置仍受电路因果锥界限制；仅用单、双量子比特门时深度为Θ(n)、门数为O(4^n log^2(n+2))；一般目标在深度h下需Ω(4^n/(h+1))个量子比特，即使允许无限经典控制；所提出的深度-宽度权衡在1≤h≤2^n范围内与下界相差O(n^2)因子。

Conclusion: 结论是中途测量与前馈可显著降低精确酉合成的最坏情况量子深度，但需付出额外量子比特开销，并存在深度-宽度下界与近似最优的权衡关系。

Abstract: Mid-circuit measurements with feedforward reduce the optimal worst-case quantum depth for exact $n$-qubit unitary synthesis from $Θ(n)$ to $O(1)$, using arbitrary one- and two-qubit gates and unrestricted ancillary space. Measurements and resets without feedforward remain subject to the circuit causal-cone bound. Our deterministic construction uses single-qubit measurements, $O(1)$ rounds of parity feedforward, and $O(n4^n\log(n+2))$ total qubits, with all ancillas starting and ending in $|0\rangle$. Classical processing is not counted in quantum depth. We teleport only the matrix error of an approximation by Nehoran and Yuen. An exactly prepared ancillary state encodes this error, whose action is added back by coherent interference. At operator-norm error $2^{-n}$, one step of amplitude amplification suffices. Coherent fanout circuits achieve the same constant-depth bound, while circuits using only one- and two-qubit gates attain depth $Θ(n)$ with $O(4^n\log^2(n+2))$ gates. Generic targets require $Ω(4^n/(h+1))$ qubits at depth $h$, even under unrestricted classical control. Our depth-width trade-offs meet this bound within a factor $O(n^2)$ for $1\le h\le2^n$.

</details>


### [169] [CT-bMPS: Approximate Tensor Network Contraction with Boundary MPS on Contraction Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06574)
*Hidetaka Manabe,Feng Pan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出 CT-bMPS，一种用于一般闭合张量网络的可扩展近似收缩方法，将收缩树每条边上的边界张量及其互补环境表示为矩阵乘积态，实现多项式代价的环境感知局部压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用闭合张量网络的近似收缩面临精度与可扩展性难以兼顾的问题：现有通用近似收缩方法在处理更大、更复杂网络时精度和效率不足，且难以在任意收缩序下有效利用环境信息。

Method: CT-bMPS 在收缩树的每条边上，将边界张量及其互补环境同时表示为矩阵乘积态（MPS）；基于约化转移矩阵进行局部压缩，从而在任意收缩树下融合环境信息，并保持时间和内存开销对网络规模与键维数呈多项式增长。

Result: 在 Ising 配分函数、随机张量网络、kicked Ising 动力学及量子纠错解码等模型与应用上进行了基准测试，结果显示在精度和计算效率上优于现有通用近似收缩方法，并可处理更大、更复杂的网络；优化收缩树与环境更新能在不增加键维数的情况下显著提升精度。

Conclusion: CT-bMPS 被确立为一个通用且可扩展的近似框架，具备灵活的收缩序与环境驱动的压缩能力，适用于广泛的应用场景。

Abstract: We introduce contraction tree boundary matrix product states (CT-bMPS), a scalable approximate contraction method for general closed tensor networks. CT-bMPS represents the boundary tensor and its complementary environment at each edge of a contraction tree as matrix product states. This representation allows local compression based on a reduced transition matrix to incorporate environment information along arbitrary contraction trees, while keeping the time and memory costs polynomial in the network size and bond dimension. We benchmark the method on a variety of models and applications, including Ising partition functions, random tensor networks, kicked Ising dynamics, and quantum error correction decoding. These benchmarks demonstrate improved accuracy and computational efficiency over existing general-purpose approximate contraction methods, together with the ability to handle larger and more complex networks. Optimized contraction trees and environment updates substantially improve accuracy without increasing the bond dimension. These results establish CT-bMPS as a general and scalable approximation framework with flexible contraction orders and environment-based compression for a wide range of applications.

</details>


### [170] [Shaping double descent in quantum extreme learning through model design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06581)
*Sabri Meyer,Ege Yilmaz,Aurelien Lucchi,Francesco Tacchino,Francesco Scala*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文分析了相关特征下双下降曲线的显式刻画，并将其推广到量子极限学习机（QELM），给出三个设计旋钮的正则化作用与插值阈值稳定性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双下降现象在独立特征下已被充分理解，但相关特征下其形状由特征协方差谱决定，已有分析多只给出隐式方程，缺乏显式风险曲线刻画；同时量子模型中的双下降行为与设计准则尚不清晰。

Method: 首先针对经典线性回归，利用特征总体协方差的条件数和一个相关强度的谱测度两个量，解析刻画完整风险曲线；然后将该理论应用于量子模型，聚焦量子极限学习机（QELM），分析编码强度、输入重上传和测量采样数三个可调设计旋钮对输出协方差的正则化作用，并推导插值阈值保持稳定的条件。

Result: 给出了经典线性回归在相关特征下双下降风险曲线的显式解析表达；证明QELM中三个设计旋钮均能正则化输出协方差，并可导出插值阈值稳定性条件；发现有限的测量散粒噪声可被利用来保证该稳定性；数值实验验证了各项预测。

Conclusion: 该工作弥补了相关特征下双下降分析仅有隐式方程的空白，并为QELM提供了具体设计原则，使其在插值之外仍能良好泛化。

Abstract: Increasing capacity can induce a machine learning model to generalize worse until it perfectly interpolates the training data, and better once it moves beyond interpolation. This inversion, known as \emph{double descent}, is well understood for independent features. For correlated features, its shape is governed by the spectrum of the feature covariance, but existing analyses typically yield only implicit equations. We first close this gap for classical linear regression, analytically characterizing the entire risk curve through two quantities, namely the condition number of the feature population covariance and a spectral measure of correlation strength. Then, we apply this theory on quantum models, focusing on quantum extreme learning machines (QELMs). We show that three tunable design knobs, \textit{i.e.,} encoding strength, input reuploads, and measurement shots, each regularize the QELM output covariance, and derive conditions under which the interpolation threshold remains stable. Finite measurement shot noise can be leveraged to guarantee this stability. Numerical experiments confirm each prediction, yielding concrete design principles for QELMs that generalize well beyond interpolation.

</details>


### [171] [Information causality does not single out the set of quantum correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06604)
*Marco Pompili,Mirjam Weilenmann*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明信息因果性（IC）原理不能完全刻画量子关联集：存在满足IC但含后量子关联的双体关联集，即使仅用3输入2输出Bell场景亦然。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IC作为物理原理被期望能单方面筛选出量子关联集，但"对任意n的IC是否恰好等价于量子关联"这一基本问题长期悬而未决，本文旨在对其给出否定回答。

Method: 构造满足所有兼容协议IC的关联集，方法是建立适用于广义概率论（GPTs）的IC熵证明；分别在n+1输入2输出的Bell场景下构造关联集，并在3输入2输出场景下构造对所有数据库长度n满足IC且含后量子点的关联集；同时分析其与量子关联集的包含关系。

Result: 对任意有限数据库长度n≥2，存在满足IC（所有兼容协议）且在后量子Bell场景（n+1输入、2输出）中呈现后量子关联的关联集；该关联集与量子关联既非超集也非子集。进一步，在3输入2输出Bell场景下构造出对所有n满足IC且包含后量子点的关联集。

Conclusion: 所有已知形式的IC原理均无法完全刻画双体量子关联集，即便在最小的3输入2输出Bell场景下也如此；这否定了IC单独筛选量子关联的可能性。

Abstract: Information causality (IC) was introduced as a physical principle that imposes constraints on the non-local correlations between two parties (Alice and Bob) in a Bell scenario. Specifically, it states that the information that Bob can gain about an $n$-bit database held by Alice is bounded by the information she communicates to him. To date, the question whether IC for any $n \in \mathbb{N}$ singles out the set of quantum correlations remains open, meaning that it is unknown whether for any number of inputs $m$ and outputs $d$ of the two parties the set of correlations that satisfy IC exactly coincides with the quantum correlations. In this work, we answer this question in the negative. Firstly, we show that for any finite database length $n \geq 2$, there is a bipartite set of correlations that satisfies IC for all compatible protocols and that exhibits post-quantum correlations in the Bell scenario with $n+1$ inputs and $2$ outcomes per party. This result relies on constructing an entropic proof of IC for GPTs that may be of independent interest. We further show that in the Bell scenario with $n+1$ inputs and $2$ outcomes per party, our constructed sets of correlations lead to neither a superset nor a subset of the quantum correlations. Secondly, we construct a set of correlations in the Bell scenario with $3$ inputs and $2$ outputs per party that satisfies IC (for all database sizes $n \in \mathbb{N}$) and that includes post-quantum points. Consequently, all known formulations of information causality fail to fully characterize the bipartite set of quantum correlations, already in the $3$-input, $2$-outcome Bell scenario.

</details>


### [172] [Staying Sober: A Shot-Free Predictor of Logical Error Rates for Concatenated Quantum Error Correcting Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06606)
*Sayam Sethi,Aditi Awasthi,Maxwell Poster,Joshua Viszlai,Jonathan Mark Baker*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种无需采样的预测器，利用稳定子、最小权重逻辑算符及（奇 d 时）权重 d+1 逻辑算符，按拼接层级逐级计算逻辑错误率的首项单项式 c·p^o，复杂度为码参数多项式级且对固定预算关于层级数 ℓ 线性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蒙特卡洛估计 QEC 码逻辑错误率需要 O(p^{-⌈d/2⌉}/ε²) 次采样，在大码距、低错误率下不可行，对码距随层级按 d^ℓ 增长的拼接码更甚。现有 fail fast 方法仅在非拼接码上按权重分离失败样本，且无法刻画拼接码层级间传递的软信息。

Method: 构造预测器，输入码的稳定子、最小权重逻辑算符以及奇 d 码的权重 d+1 逻辑算符，在无采样条件下逐层解析计算 LER 关于 p 的多项式首项单项式；并给出拼接码下以指数时间精确求解首项单项式的递归方法，预测器用以近似该递归。

Result: 证明在预算未耗尽时，对任意非拼接稳定子码、任意逻辑观测量和任意逐量子比特错误率，预测结果精确；对每个评估码在第 1 层与精确枚举一致，对 iceberg 码可精确到三层；用预测器在 48 核心小时内评估并比较 4 种噪声偏置下的 37 种码配置，其中 148 个案例里有 113 个若用蒙特卡洛在 p=10^{-3} 下需超过 10^12 次采样。

Conclusion: 该预测器以多项式级代价绕过采样瓶颈，可扩展到拼接码多层结构，为大规模、低错误率下的 QEC 码逻辑错误率评估与码配置比较提供可行工具。

Abstract: Estimating the logical error rate (LER) of a quantum error-correcting (QEC) code by Monte Carlo sampling takes $O(p^{-\lceil d/2 \rceil}/\varepsilon^2)$ samples at physical error rate $p$, code distance $d$ and relative error $\varepsilon$, which becomes intractable at large distances and low error rates, and especially for concatenated codes, whose distance grows as $d^{\ell}$ with the number of levels $\ell$. The fail fast method reduces this cost by sampling the failures of each weight separately, but it only works for unconcatenated codes, and does not model the soft information that the levels of a concatenated code pass to each other. We propose a predictor that takes the stabilizers, the minimum-weight logical operators and, for odd $d$, the logical operators of weight $d + 1$ of a code, and computes, without sampling, the leading monomial $c\,p^{o}$ of the LER when expressed as a polynomial in $p$, at every level of concatenation, in time polynomial in the code parameters and linear in $\ell$ for fixed budgets. We prove that our prediction is exact for any unconcatenated stabilizer code, for every logical observable and arbitrary per-qubit error rates, whenever no budget is exhausted. Furthermore, for concatenated codes, we construct a recursive approach that computes the leading monomial exactly in exponential time, and our predictor approximates this approach. Our predictions match exact enumeration at level 1 on every code we evaluate, and up to three levels for the iceberg code. Using our predictor, we evaluate and compare $37$ code configurations under four noise biases in $48$ core-hours, where Monte Carlo sampling would need more than $10^{12}$ shots at $p = 10^{-3}$ for $113$ of these $148$ cases.

</details>


### [173] [Quantum codes are quantized codes: Stabilizer codes from quantization over finite fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06607)
*Michael Zurel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出有限域上几何量子化的离散类比，将其应用于特定经典纠错码可得到量子纠错码，从而为编码理论中已知的量子-经典对应关系提供物理解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码理论中已存在量子码与经典码之间的对应关系，但缺乏物理层面的解释。作者希望借助几何量子化的思想为这一数学对应提供物理内涵。

Method: 构造有限域上几何量子化的离散类比，并将其应用于特定类型的经典纠错码。

Result: 对特定经典纠错码应用该离散几何量子化方法，成功得到对应的量子纠错码。

Conclusion: 所提出的离散几何量子化为编码理论中已知的量子-经典对应提供了物理解释。

Abstract: We present a discrete analogue of geometric quantization over finite fields which, when applied to certain classical error-correcting codes, yields quantum error-correcting codes. This provides a physical interpretation for a previously known quantum-classical correspondence in coding theory.

</details>


### [174] [Quantum Krylov Linear Solving Beyond Global Conditioning via Spectral Compression and Directional Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06620)
*Sijia Yu,Yifan Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究时间演化量子Krylov线性求解(QKS)中一个更困难的区域：当趋零特征值仍与解相关且必须保留时，归一化解态复杂度并不必然继承全局条件数的发散，而由"逆放大是否改变解方向"决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局条件数依赖常会大幅高估制备归一化量子线性系统解的难度。现有超越条件数的方法通常只在全局病态方向与目标解相关性有限时有效；本文针对更困难的情形——趋零特征值仍与解相关、必须被保留——展开研究，探寻复杂度能否不继承全局条件数的发散。

Method: 研究时间演化量子Krylov线性求解(QKS)，识别出一个特殊区域：与解相关的谱被压缩到紧凑的约化空间中，病态性集中在与解对齐的"软方向"上，归一化消除了该方向上的发散放大，仅留下有界的横向响应。进而分析归一化解难度的控制因素，并证明该稳定性在有限投影系统误差下持续存在、可传递到实际QKS输出。

Result: (1) 证明与解相关信息可用紧凑且稳定的QKS表示，其子空间维度、演化时间与重构开销在相关特征值趋零时保持有界；(2) 证明归一化解难度由逆放大是否改变解方向决定，而非仅由小特征值决定，即使约化系统任意病态，只要放大与解对齐仍可方向稳定；(3) 证明该稳定性在有限投影误差下持续并传递到实际输出，得到无保留小特征值逆依赖的端到端复杂度界。显式分离族表明，完整逆放大判据可能发散，而相应QKS量保持有界。

Conclusion: 归一化量子线性系统求解的复杂度不应由全局条件数决定；在解相关的病态方向与解对齐时，QKS可在保留小特征值的情况下实现有界复杂度和方向稳定性，即使完整逆放大判据发散，QKS相关量仍可有界。

Abstract: Global condition-number dependence can substantially overestimate the difficulty of preparing normalized quantum linear-system solutions. Many existing beyond-conditioning approaches are favorable when globally ill-conditioned directions have limited relevance to the target solution. Here we study a harder regime for time-evolution quantum Krylov linear solving (QKS), where a vanishing eigenvalue remains solution-relevant and must be retained. We identify a QKS regime in which the solution-relevant spectrum is compressed into a compact reduced space where the ill-conditioning is concentrated along a soft direction aligned with the solution, so that normalization removes the divergent amplification along that direction and leaves only a bounded transverse response. Consequently, the normalized-state complexity need not inherit the divergence of the global condition number. Our contributions are threefold: (1) We show that the solution-relevant information admits a compact and stable QKS representation, with bounded subspace dimension, evolution time, and reconstruction overhead as the relevant eigenvalue vanishes. (2) We show that normalized-state difficulty is governed by whether inverse amplification changes the solution direction, rather than by the small eigenvalue alone; even an arbitrarily ill-conditioned reduced system can remain directionally stable when the amplification is solution-aligned. (3) We prove that this stability persists under finite projected-system errors and transfers to the actual QKS output, yielding an end-to-end complexity bound without inverse dependence on the retained small eigenvalue. An explicit separation family further shows that a full inverse-amplification criterion can diverge while the corresponding QKS quantities remain bounded.

</details>


### [175] [Large Language Model-Guided Discovery of Weight-Five Bivariate Bicycle Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06623)
*Juan Cruz-Benito*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在LLM引导的程序演化工作流下研究权重为5的BB与PBB码，生成1,142个码方案（含1,081个LLM非基线方案），认证多个连通CSS码及其精确距离，并开展代数与连通性结构分析及随机对照比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将此前基于大语言模型的程序演化工作流推广到约束更强的权重为5的双变量自行车码（BB）与扰动双变量自行车码（PBB）族，以扩展LLM引导的码发现范围，并给出可复现的结构与精确距离分析。

Method: 采用LLM引导的程序演化工作流生成候选码，构建包含1,142个不同码方案的目录；对CSS实现进行连通性认证与精确距离验证；进行基无关的连通性分析以识别非连通条目；通过代数分析将连通CSS类划分为3阶、7阶、15阶分圆核层；并设置三组随机搜索对照，按相同的每格与编码维度采样数均匀无放回抽样6,444个CSS方案进行对比。

Result: 目录中含1,142个不同码方案，其中1,081个为LLM生成的非基线方案；认证连通CSS实现[[96,4,10]]、[[140,6,10]]、[[180,4,14]]，并认证7个导入的Lin–Pryadko存档构造；1,142条中有409条被判定为非连通，73.1%的具有精确距离证书的类包含重复连通分量；最强精确连通PBB参数点为[[216,4,10]]，由两个不同的分量类实现；LLM引导活动中936个记录正距离的CSS方案有816个（87.18%）认证为d≥5，而随机对照中为4,672–4,785个（72.50–74.26%）。

Conclusion: LLM引导活动在认证产出率上高于随机对照，而随机对照覆盖更多连通类。该工作将LLM引导发现扩展到约束更强的码族，并对其最强候选给出可复现的结构与精确距离说明。

Abstract: Building on our earlier program-evolution workflow guided by large language models (LLMs), we study weight-five bivariate bicycle (BB) and perturbed bivariate bicycle (PBB) codes. The resulting catalogue contains 1,142 distinct code proposals, including 1,081 nonbaseline proposals attributable to LLM-generated programs. Across the catalogue, we certify connected Calderbank--Shor--Steane (CSS) realizations [[96,4,10]], [[140,6,10]], and [[180,4,14]]. A post-search comparison certifies seven imported Lin--Pryadko archive constructions. For leading parameter triples also represented in that archive, we provide exact distance evidence, explicit bivariate presentations, and verified component reductions. A basis-independent connectivity analysis identifies 409 of the 1,142 catalogue entries as disconnected and shows that 73.1\% of the classes with exact distance certificates contain repeated connected components. Algebraic analysis organizes the connected CSS classes into order-3, order-7, and order-15 cyclotomic-kernel strata. The strongest exact connected PBB parameter point is [[216,4,10]], attained by two distinct component classes. Among the 936 distinct CSS proposals from the LLM-guided campaign with a recorded positive distance, 816 (87.18\%) are certified at $d\geq5$. For comparison, three random-search controls each sample 6,444 CSS code proposals uniformly without replacement, using the same per-lattice and encoded-dimension sample counts as the LLM-guided campaign. In these controls, 4,672--4,785 proposals (72.50--74.26\%) meet the same criterion. The LLM-guided campaign has the higher certified yield, while the random controls cover more connected classes. Together, these results extend LLM-guided discovery to a more constrained code family and provide a reproducible structural and exact-distance account of its strongest candidates.

</details>


### [176] [Destroying and Preserving Measurement Incompatibility over a Quantum Channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06642)
*Eric Chitambar,Yujie Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用zonotope与zonoid的数学框架研究量子信道的"破坏不相容性"与"保持不相容性"，给出qubit unital信道为incompatibility-breaking的充要条件，证明两个破坏不相容性信道并行使用时可发生superactivation，并刻画了保持不相容性的信道形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信道对量子测量不相容性的影响是量子信息中的基本问题。信道可能破坏不相容性（incompatibility-breaking），也可能保持不相容性（incompatibility-preserving）。此前缺乏对这两类信道的完整刻画，尤其缺乏对其在并行使用下行为的理解。本文旨在用zonotope/zonoid的几何框架统一刻画这两类信道，并探究其奇异性质。

Method: 采用zonotope与zonoid的数学框架，该框架可完整刻画二分测量（dichotomic measurements）的相容性，且在qubit情形尤为有效。基于zonoid语言，推导qubit unital信道为incompatibility-breaking的充要条件（对nonunital信道给出充分条件）。通过构造与分析，研究两个incompatibility-breaking信道并行使用时的行为。对incompatibility preservation，分析输入输出维度相等与输出维度更大的情形。

Result: 1) 得到qubit unital信道为incompatibility-breaking的充要条件，并对nonunital信道给出充分条件；2) 证明两个incompatibility-breaking的qubit信道并行使用后不再incompatibility-breaking，即出现superactivation现象；3) 将此结果翻译到量子steering任务：两拷贝two-qubit Werner态可在噪声阈值1/2下被steered，而单拷贝不可能；4) 证明当输入输出维度相等时，信道保持general incompatibility当且仅当它是unitary transformation；输出维度更大时该结论不再成立，并给出一般incompatibility-preserving信道的猜想形式。

Conclusion: zonotope/zonoid框架为刻画量子信道对测量不相容性的影响提供了有力工具。信道对不相容性的破坏性与保持性具有非平凡的并行行为（superactivation），并在量子steering中有直接的可观测后果。保持不相容性的信道在维度匹配时只能是unitary，维度不匹配时需更复杂的刻画，作者提出了猜想形式。

Abstract: A quantum channel is called incompatibility-breaking if it always destroys the incompatibility of quantum measurements. On the other extreme are incompatibility-preserving channels, which never destroy incompatibility. This work studies both types of channels through the mathematical framework of zonotopes and zonoids, an approach that provides a complete characterization of compatibility for dichotomic measurements and is particularly powerful in the qubit setting. Using the language of zonoids, we present necessary and sufficient conditions for when a qubit unital channel is incompatibility-breaking, which are also sufficient for nonunital channels. As one of our main results, we demonstrate the superactivation phenomenon of two incompatibility-breaking qubit channels that are no longer incompatibility-breaking when used in parallel. Translating this result into the task of quantum steering, it says that two copies of a two-qubit Werner state can be steered at noise threshold $1/2$, even though this is impossible using just one copy. For the problem of incompatibility preservation, we prove that a quantum channel with matching input and output dimensions will preserve general incompatibility if and only if it is a unitary transformation. When the output dimension is larger, this result no longer holds, and we offer a conjectured form of a general incompatibility-preserving channel.

</details>


### [177] [Kramers' Theorem Leaves a Topological Footprint in Classical Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06661)
*Chon-Fai Kam,Kai-Wen Wong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 时间反演算符Θ在半整数自旋上满足Θ²=−1，导致每个态与其时间反演态正交，该符号此前仅在量子对象中被读出。本文指出平衡态会丢失该符号，但经典记录可保留它。对于能分辨手性的探测器，一段记录后接另一段记录的时间反演的概率，可度量时间反演的上同调类Θ²=±1。这些概率构成的矩阵以其signature携带该符号，这是一个在等量发射与吸收损耗下存活的拓扑整数。在量子处理器上编程的发射器约10³次测量即可读出该符号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间反演Θ的半整数自旋符号（Θ²=−1）传统上只能从量子对象中读出，经典/平衡态被普遍认为无法保留该符号，作者希望找到一种在经典记录层面可稳健读取该拓扑符号的手段。

Method: 作者利用可分辨手性的探测器，测量“一段记录之后接另一段记录的时间反演”的概率，据此构造概率矩阵，用其signature来刻画时间反演的上同调类Θ²=±1；并在量子处理器上用编程发射器进行实验验证。

Result: 概率矩阵的signature携带Θ²=±1这一符号，且该拓扑整数在等量发射与吸收损耗下依然存活；量子处理器上约10³次shots即可读出。

Conclusion: 时间反演的−1符号不仅是量子特性，也能编码于经典记录的统计结构中被稳健读出，提供了一种对损耗鲁棒、可在量子处理器上高效验证的拓扑判据。

Abstract: Time reversal $Θ$ squares to $-1$ on a half-integer spin, so every state is orthogonal to its time reverse. This sign has been read only from quantum objects. The equilibrium state loses it, but the classical record keeps it. For a detector that resolves handedness, the probabilities that one stretch of record is followed by the time reverse of another measure the cohomology class of time reversal, $Θ^2=\pm1$. Their matrix carries this sign in its signature, a topological integer that survives equal emission and absorption losses. A programmed emitter on quantum processors reads it in about $10^3$ shots.

</details>


### [178] [Quantum Gibbs State Preparation via Relative Decoding: From Fast-Mixing Sources to Broader Target Classes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06667)
*Zhong-Xia Shang,Yufei Wang,Daniel Stilck França*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文基于同态多项式转导（DQI/HDQI 的推广），提出用相对解码把量子 Gibbs 态可制备性保证从源哈密顿量 H_A 转移到目标 H_B，并证明可达逆温由相对距离 d_rel 设定，可远超普通距离 d_ord。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子 Gibbs 态可制备性保证（如快速混合、有能隙相干制备路径）仅适用于受限的哈密顿量族，且每遇到新族通常需重新推导，缺乏可迁移的统一框架。

Method: 将哈密顿量写成 m 个 Pauli 项之和；把乘积正比于恒等式的项子集（其“关系”）视为二元线性码，源为 K_A、目标为 K_B，Pauli 标签矩阵作为奇偶校验矩阵。从 H_A 的规范热场双态（TFD）出发，通过 Bell 变换、可逆标签映射和商码 K_B/K_A 的相干解码器，制备 H_B 的近似 TFD 及 Gibbs 态。解码器只解析源中缺失的目标关系，因此可达逆温由相对距离 d_rel（此类关系中最少项数）决定，而非普通距离 d_ord。

Result: 相对距离 d_rel 可远超 d_ord（后者界定 DQI/HDQI 的均匀精确解码半径）。d_rel 随 m 线性增长且在线性半径处可高效解码时，可认证常数逆温。示例：以已知任意有限温度可制备 TFD 的最近邻自旋链为源，通过稀疏经典奇偶校验矩阵得到有界度、几何非局域、非对易的目标，满足 d_ord=3 而 d_rel=Θ(m)。

Conclusion: 相对解码提供了一种可迁移的可制备性保证机制，给出了一类新的可高效制备 TFD 的哈密顿量族，突破了以往仅限特定族且需逐一推导的限制。

Abstract: Provable guarantees for preparing quantum Gibbs states, such as rapid mixing or gapped coherent preparation paths, are known only for restricted Hamiltonian families and usually must be re-derived for each new family. Building on homomorphic polynomial transduction, a generalization of decoded quantum interferometry (DQI) and Hamiltonian DQI (HDQI), we show how relative decoding transfers such a guarantee from a source Hamiltonian $H_A$ to a target $H_B$. Writing each Hamiltonian as a sum of $m$ Pauli terms, the subsets of terms whose product is proportional to the identity, its relations, form a binary linear code, $K_A$ for the source and $K_B$ for the target, with the Pauli-label matrix as parity-check matrix as in DQI. Starting from the canonical thermofield double (TFD) of $H_A$, a Bell transform, a reversible label map, and a coherent decoder for the quotient code $K_B/K_A$ prepare an approximate TFD of $H_B$, and hence its Gibbs state. Because this decoder only resolves target relations absent from the source, the reachable inverse temperature is set by the relative distance $d_{\rm rel}$, the fewest terms in any such relation. It can far exceed the ordinary distance $d_{\rm ord}$, the fewest terms in any target relation, which bounds the uniform exact decoding radius of DQI and HDQI. We show that linear $d_{\rm rel}$ with efficient decoding at linear radius certifies a constant inverse temperature. As an example, starting from a nearest-neighbor spin chain whose TFD is known to be preparable at every finite temperature, a sparse classical parity-check matrix yields bounded-degree, geometrically nonlocal, noncommuting targets with $d_{\rm ord}=3$ and $d_{\rm rel}=Θ(m)$. To our knowledge, this gives a new class of efficiently preparable TFDs.

</details>


### [179] [Climbing the Fusion Tree: Center Ordering and Decoding Hierarchy in Non-Abelian Mixed States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06692)
*Pablo Sala,Vlad Temkin,Cenke Xu,Daniel Podolsky,Ehud Altman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将非阿贝尔对称群 G 的强-弱自发对称破缺（sw-SSB）形式化为一个解码任务，并通过对偶量子双模型 D(G) 与统计力学模型层级推导其相变结构，结合 S₃ 与 SU(2) 的张量网络计算进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望为强-弱自发对称破缺（sw-SSB）提供一个可操作、可计算的框架，将此前多集中于阿贝尔对称的情形推广到非阿贝尔对称群 G，从而统一理解 sw-SSB 的判据、相变与解码阈值，并为非阿贝尔拓扑码的解码器设计提供理论工具。

Method: 方法核心是把 sw-SSB 表述为解码任务：区分两个相差一对插入不可约表示（irrep）的强对称态，且二者均受强对称退相干破坏。对有限群 G，该任务对偶于在纯荷噪声下区分量子双 D(G) 的逻辑态。作者推导了在一系列逐级增大的嵌套区域上，由融合测量结果进行最优解码的统计力学模型层级；其中测量得到的 irrep 充当淬火无序，并满足类似 Nishimori 的条件。此外借助张量网络计算对 S₃ 与 SU(2) 进行检验。

Result: 由于局域 irrep 无法决定其全局融合方式，强 G×G 对称性仅以非局域方式实现，且在中⼼ Z(G) 下平凡荷的 irrep 可隐藏于噪声生成的 irrep 中，导致强对称自发破缺为 Z(G) 乘以弱对称。非零错误率下唯一的相变是中心的有序化，其阈值随层级上升而不下降；该结论对非幂零群在任意有限层级均成立。张量网络计算表明：S₃ 模型（中心平凡）在第一层级无相变，所有 irrep 的保真度关联可证衰减至非零值；SU(2) 模型呈现与 Nishimori-Ising 普适类一致的 Z₂ 相变，自旋-1/2 irrep 的保真度关联变为长程，且阈值从第一层级到第二层级上升约 16%。

Conclusion: 本文为研究非阿贝尔 sw-SSB 提供了系统框架，并为构造非阿贝尔拓扑码的解码器奠定了理论基础；核心结论是：非阿贝尔情形下强对称破缺到 Z(G)×弱对称，唯一非零错误率相变对应中心有序化，其阈值随解码层级单调不降。

Abstract: We develop an operational approach to strong-to-weak spontaneous symmetry breaking (sw-SSB) of a non-Abelian symmetry group G. We formulate it as the decoding task of distinguishing two strongly symmetric states that differ by a pair of inserted irreps, after both are corrupted by strongly symmetric decoherence. For finite G, this task is dual to distinguishing logical states of the quantum double D(G) under pure-charge noise. We derive a hierarchy of statistical mechanics models describing optimal decoding from the fusion outcomes measured on successively larger nested regions. As for maximum-likelihood decoders of Abelian codes the measured irreps act as quenched disorder obeying a Nishimori-like condition. Strikingly, because local irreps do not determine how they fuse globally, the strong $G\times G$ symmetry is realized only nonlocally, and irreps trivially charged under Z(G) (the center of G) can hide inside noise-generated ones. As a result, the strong symmetry spontaneously breaks down to Z(G), times the weak symmetry. The only transition at nonzero error rate is then an ordering of the center, whose threshold cannot decrease as the hierarchy is ascended. This conclusion holds at any finite level of the hierarchy for non-nilpotent groups. We confirm these predictions with tensor-network calculations for $S_3$ and SU(2). At the first level, the $S_3$ model, whose center is trivial, shows no transition, and the fidelity correlations of all irreps provably decay to non-zero values. The SU(2) model exhibits a $Z_2$ transition consistent with the Nishimori-Ising universality class, where the fidelity correlations of spin-$1/2$ irreps become long-range. The threshold rises from the first to the second level of the hierarchy by $16\%$. Our results provide a systematic framework for studying non-Abelian sw-SSB and for constructing decoders for non Abelian topological codes.

</details>


### [180] [Measurement-free Preparation of Surface-Code States with Digital-Analog Counterdiabatic Drivings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06697)
*Shubham Kumar Balaganchi A. Bhargava,Paolo A. Erdman,Anne-Maria Visuri,Jose D. Martın-Guerrero,Yolanda Vives-Gilabert,Enrique Solano,Narendra N. Hegade*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出数字-模拟方法制备表面码基态流形，利用绝热规范势结构将稳定子局域反绝热项映射为固定角度双比特门与模拟交换演化的数字-模拟模块，在4×4棋盘格稳定子上验证并显著提升短时基态制备，纠缠深度降低一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期待超导量子纠错需要浅电路制备稳定子码态，而逻辑态初始化要求从原生单体/两体控制生成四体稳定子关联，现有方法在深度与测量依赖上存在局限。

Method: 基于Sels-Polkovnikov绝热规范势框架，利用其结构性质将稳定子局域反绝热项映射为数字-模拟块；块由固定角度两比特XY门、单比特旋转和模拟交换相互作用演化构成，生成的 dressed 高阶算子作为变分拟设确定反绝热项；并在n×m稳定子格上推广构造。

Result: 在最高4×4棋盘格 plaquette 稳定子网格（方阵对应旋转表面码，矩形探测扩展性）上，方法生成主导四体反绝热基，短演化时间下相较纯绝热演化显著改善基态制备；数字-模拟合成将纠缠深度降低一个数量级。

Conclusion: 建立了稳定子几何、反绝热规范势结构与数字-模拟控制之间的直接联系，实现了近期待超导架构上无需测量的稳定子码态制备。

Abstract: Preparing stabilizer-code states with shallow circuits is an important primitive for near-term quantum error correction in superconducting circuits, where logical-state initialization requires four-body stabilizer correlations from native one- and two-body controls. We introduce a digital-analog method for the preparation of surface-code ground-state manifolds. The method exploits a structural property of the adiabatic gauge potential, mapping stabilizer-local counterdiabatic terms to digital-analog blocks on the superconducting layout using fixed-angle two-qubit \(XY\) gates, single-qubit rotations, and analog exchange-interaction evolutions. The dressed blocks generate higher-order operators used as the variational ansatz to determine the counterdiabatic terms within the Sels-Polkovnikov adiabatic-gauge-potential framework. We benchmark checkerboard plaquette-stabilizer grids up to \(4\times4\), the square-grid instances correspond to rotated surface codes, while the rectangular cases probe the scaling of the method. For all grids, the proposed method generates the dominant four-body counterdiabatic basis and, at short evolution times substantially improves the ground-state preparation as compared with bare adiabatic evolution. We further generalize the construction to \(n\times m\) stabilizer lattices and show that digital-analog synthesis can reduce the entangling depth by an order of magnitude. Together, these results establish a direct connection between stabilizer geometry, the structure of the counterdiabatic gauge potential, and digital-analog control for measurement-free preparation of stabilizer-code states on near-term superconducting architecture.

</details>


### [181] [Random-Oracle Unitary Synthesis is Impossible](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06700)
*Andrew Huang,Akshar Ramkumar,John Wright*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"平均情形"酉合成问题：给定随机布尔函数（可叠加求值）能否高效实现Haar随机酉。作者证明了超多项式查询下界，说明该模型下酉合成不可能；同时否定回答了Brakerski-Yuen关于ROM-PRU模型中可扩展PRU的问题，并构造了多项式查询下的O(N)-设计，超越此前O(√N)-设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子复杂性中酉合成是核心开放问题：能否在可叠加求值任意经典函数的前提下高效实现任意酉。Brakerski与Yuen（CRYPTO 2026）的工作启发了对该问题的平均情形研究，并提出了ROM-PRU模型下可扩展伪随机酉（PRU）是否可实现的问题。

Method: 提出平均情形酉合成问题：以Haar随机酉为目标，以随机布尔函数为资源；在该模型下进行查询复杂性分析，证明超多项式查询下界。针对ROM-PRU模型分析酉设计的可实现性，并构造具体的酉设计算法。

Result: 证明平均情形酉合成的超多项式查询下界，从而在该模型下酉合成不可行；否定回答了ROM-PRU模型能否实现可扩展PRU的问题：对任意γ>0，无法高效实现Ω(N^{1+γ})酉设计（N=2^n）；正面构造：在poly(n)查询内实现O(N)-设计，优于此前最优的O(√N)-设计。

Conclusion: 在基于随机布尔函数的平均情形模型中，一般酉合成不可行（超多项式下界）。ROM-PRU模型无法实现可扩展PRU及强酉设计，但可高效构造O(N)-设计，改进了先前结果。

Abstract: A major open problem in quantum complexity is the question of unitary synthesis---namely, is it possible to efficiently implement any unitary, given the ability to evaluate any classical function in superposition? Inspired by a recent work by Brakerski and Yuen (CRYPTO 2026), we propose an ``average-case'' unitary synthesis problem, which asks whether it is possible to efficiently implement a Haar random unitary given access to a random Boolean function. We then demonstrate a superpolynomial query lower bound, establishing that unitary synthesis in this model is impossible.
  Along with our main result, we settle in the negative a question Brakerski and Yuen pose, of whether scalable pseudorandom unitaries (PRUs) can be implemented in the ROM-PRU model. In particular, we show that in the ROM-PRU model, no $Ω(N^{1+γ})$ unitary design on $n$ qubits, where $N=2^n$, can be efficiently achieved for any $γ> 0$. On the other hand, we construct an $O(N)$-design in $\text{poly}(n)$ queries in the same model, surpassing the previously best known results which constructed $O(\sqrt{N})$-designs.

</details>


### [182] [Optimal compression with quantum retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06702)
*Shyam Dhamapurkar,Mohit Garg,Manaswi Paraashar,Jaikumar Radhakrishnan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究一种量子数据压缩问题：将Hamming重量不超过n的m位串x压缩为s位串y，使得x的任意比特x_i都能通过对y的标准oracle编码进行至多t次量子查询而无误差地恢复。自适应查询下可实现对数因子最优压缩；非自适应查询下，空间在m上的依赖最优（除对数因子），在n上的依赖至多比最优方案平方级更差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在允许量子查询的模型下，如何对稀疏二进制串进行压缩，并探讨自适应与非自适应查询对压缩效率的影响。

Method: 将x编码为较短串y，并通过对y的标准oracle编码进行量子查询来恢复x的各个比特；分别设计自适应查询方案和非自适应查询方案。

Result: 自适应查询时，压缩可达到对数因子范围内的最优；非自适应查询时，空间对m的依赖除对数因子外最优，对n的依赖至多比最优方案平方级更差。

Conclusion: 量子查询压缩中自适应查询可显著提升压缩效率，非自适应查询虽略逊但仍接近最优。

Abstract: We consider the following data compression problem. Given a string $x \in \{0,1\}^m$ of Hamming weight at most $n$, compress it into a shorter string $y \in \{0,1\}^s$ so that any bit $x_i$ of $x$ can be retrieved without any error using at most $t$ quantum queries to the standard oracle encoding of $y$. If queries are allowed to be adaptive we show how optimal compression up to a logarithmic factor can be achieved. If the queries are required to be made non-adaptively, we show schemes whose space is optimal in its dependence on $m$ except for a logarithmic factor, and is at most quadratically worse when compared to the optimum in its dependence on $n$.

</details>


### [183] [Out-of-control Hamiltonian Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06709)
*Weiyuan Gong,Muzhou Ma,Sitan Chen,Jordan Cotler,Hsin-Yuan Huang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究近期模拟量子模拟器在极简访问模型下的哈密顿量学习：仅允许统一态制备/测量，或仅限计算基制备/测量，证明在这两类受限实验条件下仍可重建多体哈密顿量的全部参数（后者至不可避免的规范自由度）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具备最强可证明保证的哈密顿量学习算法依赖快速、任意的单比特门与任意基测量等量子控制能力，超出近期模拟量子模拟器（如原子、离子平台）的能力范围。因此需要研究在最小访问模型下的哈密顿量学习问题。

Method: 在两种极简访问模型下设计学习协议：(1) 统一态制备与测量：每次实验可将每个量子比特旋转到相同状态、进行短时演化，并在相同基下测量每个量子比特；(2) 计算基态制备与测量：态制备与测量均限制在计算基。针对任意相互作用图上的通用2-局域哈密顿量，以及仅含Pauli X/Z相互作用的最近邻哈密顿量（涵盖当代Rydberg原子平台）在1D和2D矩形晶格上的情形，提出求解大规模结构化多项式系统的新技术来重建参数。

Result: (1) 统一态制备与测量下，对任意相互作用图上的通用2-局域哈密顿量，所有参数均可从该类实验中重建；(2) 计算基态制备与测量下，对1D和2D矩形晶格上仅含Pauli X/Z相互作用的最近邻哈密顿量，所有参数可在不可避免的规范自由度意义下被重建。

Conclusion: 结果表明，即使在最严苛的实验约束下，仍可从量子多体系统的动力学中学习到大量信息；所提出的协议为求解大量参数上的结构化多项式系统引入了新技术。

Abstract: Learning the Hamiltonian of a many-body system from its dynamics is a central task in quantum science, yet the algorithms with the strongest provable guarantees assume some level of quantum control--fast, arbitrary single-qubit gates interleaved with time evolution, and measurements in arbitrary bases--that is beyond the capabilities of near-term analog quantum simulators. Motivated by analog atom- and ion-based platforms, we study Hamiltonian learning under minimal access models.
  Uniform state preparation and measurements: We first consider the setting where in every experiment, one can rotate each qubit to the same state, perform short-time evolution, and measure every qubit in the same basis. Surprisingly, we show that for generic 2-local Hamiltonians on any interaction graph, all of the parameters can be reconstructed from such experiments.
  Computational basis state preparation and measurements: We then consider a similarly constrained setting, but where state preparation and measurement are restricted to the computational basis. For nearest-neighbor Hamiltonians with only Pauli $X/Z$ interactions, a class which captures contemporary Rydberg atom platforms, we show that over 1D and 2D rectangular lattices, all of the parameters can be reconstructed from such experiments up to unavoidable gauges.
  Our protocols introduce new techniques for solving structured polynomial systems over an extensive number of parameters. Taken together, our results suggest that one can learn a great deal from the dynamics of quantum many-body systems even under the most stringent experimental constraints.

</details>


### [184] [Tight Query Lower Bounds for Quantum Sampling, with an Application to Certified Randomness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06721)
*Keshav Bhateja,Mehdi Esmaili,Atul Mantri*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文在量子查询模型下研究高XEB分数对不可信量子设备意味着什么：证明超过理想分数需Ω(N^{1/3})次查询（紧界），并证明在多项式查询、分数接近理想时输出具有n-O(log n)比特光滑最小熵，近乎最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机电路采样是量子计算优势的主要演示，其评估依赖线性交叉熵基准（XEB）。但一个高XEB分数究竟能"认证"不可信量子设备的何种性质尚不明确，本文旨在从理论上刻画高XEB分数的认证含义。

Method: 在量子查询模型中分析诚实采样器的理想分数作为有效设备与认证随机性的Tsirelson型上界。通过两类进展度量（progress measure）证明下界：对Fourier采样，度量是纯化随机函数中消去成对元素相干振幅的范数，结合多项式方法与压缩oracle技术；对Haar随机态，度量是Dirichlet正交多项式展开中平均阶数的平方根，由一次观测效应的精确恒等式控制。

Result: 对N个结果的Haar与Fourier oracle系综，以常数幅度超过理想分数需要Ω(N^{1/3})次查询，且该界是紧的，从而证明了Haar随机态的先前猜想并推广到Fourier采样。对Fourier采样及经规范态制备oracle访问的Haar随机态，多项式查询且分数在理想值o(1)以内的n比特输出具有n-O(log n)比特光滑最小熵，即使对手与设备纠缠并事后获知oracle也成立，近乎最优。推论：碰撞分布的查询复杂度为Θ(N^{1/3})，且该算法的相干版本可近似制备振幅均匀态的量子上Hadamard积。

Conclusion: 高XEB分数可作为有效量子设备的Tsirelson型上界：超出理想分数需付出Ω(N^{1/3})查询代价，而接近理想分数的高分则蕴含近乎最优的认证随机性（n-O(log n)比特光滑最小熵）。研究为XEB的认证能力提供了严格的理论基础。

Abstract: Random circuit sampling experiments, the leading demonstrations of quantum computational advantage, are tested with the linear cross-entropy benchmark (XEB), which scores outcomes by their ideal probabilities. We study what a high XEB score certifies about an untrusted quantum device. In the quantum query model, we show that the honest sampler's ideal score acts as a Tsirelson bound for efficient devices and certified randomness. First, exceeding the ideal score by a constant requires $Ω(N^{1/3})$ queries for the Haar and Fourier oracle ensembles with $N$ outcomes, and this bound is tight; this proves an earlier conjecture for Haar-random states and extends it to Fourier sampling. Second, for Fourier sampling and for Haar-random states accessed through the canonical state-preparation oracle, the $n$-bit output of a device that makes polynomially many queries and scores within $o(1)$ of the ideal score has $n-O(\log n)$ bits of smooth min-entropy, even against an adversary who is entangled with the device and later learns the oracle; this is nearly optimal. As a corollary, sampling the collision distribution, which is proportional to the square of the ideal distribution, has query complexity $Θ(N^{1/3})$, and a coherent version of the same algorithm approximately prepares quantum Hadamard products of states with uniform amplitude magnitudes. Both lower bounds rest on progress measures that bound the excess over the ideal score and increase only slightly with each query. For Fourier sampling the measure is the norm of the coherent amplitudes for removing pairs in a purification of the random function, combining the polynomial method with the compressed oracle technique; for Haar-random states it is the square root of the average level in an expansion in Dirichlet orthogonal polynomials, controlled by an exact identity for the effect of one observation.

</details>


### [185] [Certifying Measurement Incompatibility under Bounded Classical Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06722)
*Oxana Shaya*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入"并集最大泄漏"(union maximal leakage),用于量化用经典共用解释复现观测概率所需的信息量,并据此刻画经典通信下的测量不相容性、Bell非局域性与私密随机性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测量不相容性是通信优势、态分辨以及Bell非局域性(用于设备无关QKD与私密随机数)的必要条件;但经典通信会影响接收方输出,故在认证测量不相容性时必须将其纳入考量,需要一个能刻画"经典通信+相容测量"复现观测所需信息量的量化工具。

Method: 提出并集最大泄漏,由观测概率通过线性规划计算。若接收方独立测量选择前有至多k比特消息,则各消息对应的相容测量给出并集最大泄漏不超过k,该界在任意与输入无关的非信号共享资源下成立。进一步给出该量与最小经典通信量的等价刻画、与Bell非局域性的等价条件(固定共享量子态与接收方测量下,违规当且仅当存在某消息值使接收方测量相对该态上的两输出发送方测量呈Bell非局域),并对二元接收结果给出条件熵界的显式私密随机性证明与可组合安全提取。

Result: 并集最大泄漏可由线性规划计算;存在k比特上界且在非信号共享资源下稳健;量子情形下违规蕴含初始共享态纠缠;并集最大泄漏等于用相容测量加任意非信号辅助复现观测所需的最小经典通信;该量精确刻画Bell非局域性出现条件;二元结果时违规可认证对发送方私密的随机性(即便接收方测量依赖消息且发送方持量子边信息),并给出显式条件熵界与含设备记忆的序列协议中的可组合安全随机性提取。

Conclusion: 并集最大泄漏为经典通信辅助下的测量不相容性认证提供了统一、可计算的框架,将通信量、纠缠、Bell非局域性与私密随机性联系起来,并支持设备无关场景下的安全随机性提取。

Abstract: Measurement incompatibility enables quantum advantages in communication and state discrimination. It is also necessary for Bell nonlocality, which is used in device-independent protocols for quantum key distribution and private randomness generation. Classical communication can influence the receiver's outcome and must therefore be accounted for when certifying measurement incompatibility. We introduce union maximal leakage, which quantifies the information needed for a common classical explanation of all observed probabilities and is computed from them by a linear program. If a message of at most $k$ bits precedes the receiver's independent measurement choice, compatible measurements for each message give union maximal leakage at most $k$. This bound holds with any input-independent nonsignalling shared resource. In quantum theory, a violation also implies that the initially shared state is entangled. Conversely, union maximal leakage determines the minimum classical communication needed to reproduce the observations with compatible measurements and arbitrary nonsignalling assistance. For a fixed shared quantum state and receiver measurements, a violation is possible exactly when, for some message value, the receiver's measurements are Bell-nonlocal against two-outcome sender measurements on that state. For binary receiver outcomes, we show that a violation also certifies randomness private from the sender, even when the receiver's measurement depends on the sender's message and the sender retains quantum side information. We quantify this privacy through an explicit conditional entropy bound and establish composably secure randomness extraction in sequential protocols with device memory.

</details>


### [186] [Approximate cloning of structured pure states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06723)
*Yaroslav Herasymenko,Junqiao Lin,Philip Verduyn Lunel,Dmitry Grinko*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究结构化量子态族近似克隆的样本复杂度，构造费米子与玻色子高斯态及相位态的最优或多项式复杂度克隆信道，并给出统一表示论框架与复杂度线性标度的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子不可克隆定理指出一般量子信息无法被复制：即使已有 N 份未知态，任何产生 N+1 份拷贝的信道都会在某些输入上产生非零迹距离误差。对任意纯态，达到误差 ε 所需样本量 N ≃ dim H/ε，对高维系统天文般巨大。因此本文关心：若输入被承诺属于某类有结构的状态族，克隆复杂度能否大幅降低。

Method: 考察多体物理与量子信息中的若干基本状态族：n 模费米子高斯态与 Slater 态、n 模玻色子高斯态、qudit 相位态。对费米子构造最优克隆信道；对玻色子高斯态构造显式克隆信道，证明多项式复杂度且不限制态能量，但一般不最优。费米子与玻色子结果源自统一的表示论框架，推广 Werner 及 Chiribella-Yang 的工作，并给出克隆信道的酉实现。通过高 N 下 frame potential 的受控鞍点计算，解释多类状态族克隆复杂度随族流形维度线性增长。对相位态，框架不适用，独立构造具有最优克隆保真度的信道。

Result: 费米子情形构造出最优克隆信道，将复杂度从随 n 指数增长降为多项式增长；玻色子高斯态情形给出显式克隆信道并证明多项式复杂度，且无需对态能量施加约束，但该信道一般不最优；统一表示论框架同时给出信道及其酉实现；鞍点计算解释了克隆复杂度与族流形维度成线性关系，并同样解释层析复杂度的标度；对相位态独立构造了最优克隆保真度信道。

Conclusion: 结构化状态族的近似 N→N+1 克隆可以显著优于一般纯态的 dim H/ε 复杂度。费米子高斯态可达到最优且多项式复杂度，玻色子高斯态可实现多项式复杂度克隆，相位态可独立达到最优保真度。统一表示论框架与 frame potential 鞍点分析揭示了复杂度与状态族流形维度的线性关系，并与层析复杂度标度一致，补充了 stabilizer 态克隆的近期结果。

Abstract: The no-cloning theorem is a cornerstone result in quantum mechanics that forbids copying of general quantum information. More quantitatively, even given $N$ copies of an unknown state, any channel producing an $(N+1)$-copy state incurs a nonzero trace-distance error on some inputs. Turning this around, one can ask about the sample complexity of approximate $N\rightarrow N+1$ cloning: for a desired small error $ε$, what $N$ suffices? For arbitrary pure states on a Hilbert space $H$, the answer is $N\simeq \dim H/ε$ - astronomically large for most $H$ of interest. What if the input is promised to lie in a structured family? We examine a range of families fundamental to many-body physics and quantum information: $n$-mode fermionic Gaussian and Slater states, $n$-mode bosonic Gaussian states, and qudit phase states. For fermions, we construct optimal cloning channels, reducing the complexity from exponential to polynomial in $n$. For bosonic Gaussian states, we construct an explicit cloner that certifies polynomial complexity, without any constraint on the energy of the state; this channel, however, is not optimal in general. The fermionic and bosonic results follow from a single representation-theoretic framework, which generalizes previous work by Werner and by Chiribella and Yang; it also yields the cloners' unitary implementation. Furthermore, we explain why for many families the cloning complexity is linear in the dimension of the family manifold, via a controlled saddle-point evaluation of the frame potential at high $N$. The same calculation accounts for the analogous scaling of tomography complexity. The above framework does not cover phase states; for these we give an independent construction of a channel with optimal cloning fidelity. Our work complements recent results on cloning stabilizer states.

</details>


### [187] [Random Order in Quantum Streaming: Replenishment and Robust Lower Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06727)
*Nadezhda Voronova*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究随机序对量子流算法中量子内存作用的影响，提出"补充(replenishment)"概念：后续经典输入可恢复被先前查询消耗的量子态效用。构造基于Hidden Matching的人工问题，在均匀随机序下存在多对数空间的一遍量子算法，但在随机序下经典及"先更新后查询"量子模型下均需多项式空间。通过强化Multiple Hidden Matching的可消耗性下界，证明无预先纠缠时任何支持r个独立匹配请求的量子编码需Ω(r)量子比特；并扩展Kallaugher三角计数算法与Assadi-Sundaresan噪声Gap Cycle框架至量子流，得到随机边序下Ω(n)空间下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典直觉认为随机序通常降低问题难度，但量子内存一旦被查询消耗便不可复用。本文关注随机序能否通过后续输入重新"补充"已消耗的量子态，从而改变量子内存的角色，并厘清随机序在量子流模型中的能力边界。

Method: 1) 构造基于Hidden Matching的人工问题，含重复坐标数据与在线匹配请求，对比均匀随机序量子算法、"先更新后查询"量子模型及随机序经典模型；2) 强化Gilboa-Jain-McClean对Multiple Hidden Matching的可消耗性界，证明无预先纠缠下支持r个独立请求需Ω(r) qubits，顺序请求时线性界延伸至r=Θ(√N)；3) 改造Kallaugher三角计数算法以适应边均匀重复的均匀随机流，重建量子草图并重采样经典估计量；4) 将Assadi-Sundaresan鲁棒Noisy Gap Cycle框架扩展至量子流，由Block Hidden XOR量子通信下界导出随机边序下Ω(n)空间下界。

Result: 人工问题在均匀随机序下存在多对数空间一遍量子算法，但随机序经典与"先更新后查询"量子模型下无条件需多项式空间；Multiple Hidden Matching下界：无纠缠时r≤N^{1/2-δ}需Ω(r) qubits，顺序请求线性界至r=Θ(√N)；改造后的三角计数算法在合适参数区间改进期望空间界；量子流下Block Hidden XOR通信下界给出大环随机边序Ω(n)空间下界，并影响若干图问题。

Conclusion: 随机序可通过"补充"机制使小量子表示持续有效，从而赋予量子流算法超越经典及先更新后查询量子模型的优势；但其他任务仍存在本质性空间需求，随机序并不能普遍消除量子内存的消耗约束。

Abstract: How can random order change the role of quantum memory in streaming? Later classical input can restore the usefulness of a quantum state consumed by earlier queries. We call this replenishment.
  We construct an artificial problem based on Hidden Matching, with repeated coordinate data and online matching requests. It admits a one-pass quantum algorithm using polylogarithmic space in uniformly random order, but unconditionally requires polynomial space both classically in random order and quantumly when all updates precede the requests.
  To prove the quantum lower bound, we strengthen the consumability bounds of Gilboa, Jain, and McClean for Multiple Hidden Matching. Without prior entanglement, any quantum encoding supporting $r$ independent matching requests requires $Ω(r)$ qubits for $r\le N^{1/2-δ}$ and every fixed $δ\in(0,1/2)$, even with simultaneous revelation and arbitrary joint decoding. For sequential requests, the linear bound extends through $r=Θ(\sqrt N)$.
  We also adapt Kallaugher's triangle-counting algorithm to uniformly random streams in which every edge is repeated equally often. Rebuilding the quantum sketch and resampling the classical estimator improve its expected-space bound in suitable parameter regimes.
  Finally, we extend the robust Noisy Gap Cycle framework of Assadi and Sundaresan to quantum streaming. A quantum communication lower bound for Block Hidden XOR yields an $Ω(n)$ space lower bound in random edge order for large enough cycles, with consequences for several graph problems.
  Thus random order can enable replenishment of small quantum representations, while substantial space requirements persist for other tasks.

</details>


### [188] [Qubit CSS codes with Universal Transversal Gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06730)
*Arpit Dua,Adam Holmes,Michael J. Gullans,Victor V. Albert*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明：若允许在不同量子比特划分上分别使用深度一（depth-one）少比特门层，则这些层可以共同生成纠错码上的通用逻辑门集合，从而绕过Eastin-Knill定理对单一划分的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Eastin-Knill定理表明，任何能探测单量子比特错误的编码都不存在通用的横截（transversal）逻辑门集合，这通常意味着需要魔态蒸馏、码切换、规范固定、晶格手术等复杂且开销巨大的通用量子计算构造。作者希望避免这些额外机制，直接在编码层面实现通用逻辑门集合。

Method: 作者利用Eastin-Knill定理仅约束单一量子比特划分这一点：对不同的量子比特划分分别施加深度一的少比特门层，每个逻辑门生成元由一个深度一层后接同一稳定子码的纠错组成。进一步，作者提出通过将纠缠电路施加到较低距离的“种子”码（其允许一个深度一非Clifford门）和一个合适的“核（kernel）”码上，来搜索这类编码。

Result: 作者构造了47个具有深度一生成集合的编码，这些编码每层至少容忍一个故障门，其中包含[[37,1,7]]码和[[59,1,9]]码，二者的校验权重均不超过八。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，通过在不同量子比特划分上组合深度一少比特门层，可实现容错的通用逻辑门集合，且无需魔态蒸馏、码切换、规范固定、晶格手术等 gadget，为通用容错量子计算提供了一条更简洁的编码级路径。

Abstract: The Eastin-Knill theorem rules out a universal set of transversal logical gates on any code that detects single-qubit errors. The theorem constrains one partition of the qubits at a time. We show that depth-one layers of few-qubit gates, each respecting its own partition, can together generate a universal logical gate set on an error-correcting code. Each logical gate generator is then a single depth-one layer followed by error correction of the same stabilizer code, with no magic-state distillation, code switching, gauge fixing, lattice surgery, or other gadgets. We show that such codes can be found by applying an entangling circuit to a lower-distance "seed" code admitting a depth-one non-Clifford gate and an appropriate "kernel" code. We construct 47 codes with depth-one generating sets that tolerate at least one faulty gate per layer, including a [[37,1,7]] and a [[59,1,9]] code, both of check weight at most eight.

</details>


### [189] [Streaming Regev's Quantum Factoring Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06733)
*Katherine Van Kirk,Craig Gidney*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文解决了Regev因式分解算法难以实现"量子比特回收"的问题，通过叠加掩码与Fibonacci幂相位估计两项改进，将其空间开销降至 n+M(n)+O(√n)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Regev因式分解算法的一大实际障碍是空间占用过高，且此前被认为无法与"量子比特回收"（即对相位估计中的控制比特进行流式处理而非全部同时存储）兼容。已有工作（Ragavan与Vaikuntanathan）利用Fibonacci累加器将量子比特数从O(n^{1.5})降至11.32n，但仍需大量编码转换与反计算。作者旨在进一步降低空间并实现比特流式化。

Method: 在Ragavan与Vaikuntanathan的构造基础上做两项关键改进：一是采用叠加掩码防止无关信息进入累加器，从而免去反计算；二是用酉操作的Fibonacci幂（而非2的幂）执行相位估计，避免原构造所需的各种编码转换。改进后相位估计比特在电路中仅被使用一次，因而可被流式处理。

Result: 将Regev算法的空间占用降至 n+M(n)+O(√n)，其中M(n)为原地乘法所需空间。例如采用Luo等人（2026）的原地乘法器，Regev算法可用4n+O(√n)个量子比特分解n位整数。

Conclusion: 该构造仍是Regev算法迄今最低空间的实例化方案，虽仍不及Shor算法主流实现所需的n/2+εn量子比特，但表明Regev算法远未达到完全优化。

Abstract: A major practical obstacle to Regev's factoring algorithm is its large space usage. In particular, the algorithm was believed to be incompatible with "qubit recycling," which streams the control qubits in phase estimation rather than storing them all simultaneously. We show how to fix this streaming problem. We build on the construction of Ragavan and Vaikuntanathan, which used Fibonacci accumulators to reduce the qubit count from $O(n^{1.5})$ to $11.32n$. We make two key improvements. First, we use superposition masking to prevent unwanted information from entering the accumulators, removing the need for uncomputation. Second, we show how to perform phase estimation using Fibonacci powers of a unitary operation rather than powers of two. This avoids various encoding conversions required by the construction of Ragavan and Vaikuntanathan. After these improvements, the phase-estimation qubits are used only once in the circuit, allowing them to be streamed.
  Our improvements reduce the space usage of Regev's factoring algorithm to $n+M(n)+O(\sqrt{n})$, where $M(n)$ is the space required for an in-place multiplication. For example, using the in-place multiplier of Luo et al. (2026), Regev's algorithm can factor an $n$-bit integer using $4n+O(\sqrt{n})$ qubits. Although our construction still falls short of the $n/2+εn$ qubits sufficient for leading implementations of Shor's algorithm, it is the lowest-space instantiation of Regev's algorithm to date and suggests that Regev's algorithm is still far from fully optimized.

</details>


### [190] [Characterizing Photonic Partial Distinguishability Through Gram Matrix Tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06736)
*Marcin Kotowski,Marco Robbio,Oliver Reardon-Smith,Benoit Seron,Leonardo Novo,Ernesto F. Galvão,Michał Oszmaniec*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"Gram矩阵层析"方法，用于刻画独立制备光子的内部自由度，通过可测量的Bargmann不变量重构Gram矩阵，并提供误差界与采样复杂度保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分可区分性会影响多光子干涉，这是Boson Sampling等大规模光子实验及多光子态量子技术应用的核心问题。然而，导致该行为的内部自由度难以刻画：在很多情形下无法直接访问，甚至其维度或物理本质事先未知。

Method: 针对独立制备、内部态为纯态或近纯态的光子，引入Gram矩阵层析。对纯态，Gram矩阵包含预测对内部自由度不敏感实验统计所需的全部信息。从二次数量级的可测量Bargmann不变量（编码重叠模长与集体相位）中，在物理无关的相位选择意义下重构该矩阵。采用两种互补手段获取这些量：定向Fourier干涉仪，以及在单个随机干涉仪中测量的两体与三体光子数关联。

Result: 推导了显式误差界，可控制任意后续对内部态不敏感实验中预测分布与实际结果分布之间的全变差距离；这些保证考虑了有限采样与偏离纯态的情形。协议的总采样复杂度与内部Hilbert空间维度无关，在内部态重叠的温和假设下为多项式级。

Conclusion: 该协议基于标准干涉测量，将部分可区分光子的刻画转化为稳健且具有操作意义的表征方法。

Abstract: Partial distinguishability affects multiphoton interference, which is central to large-scale photonic experiments such as Boson Sampling and to applications of multiphoton states across quantum technologies. Characterizing the internal degrees of freedom responsible for this behavior is challenging: in many scenarios they cannot be accessed directly and even their dimension or physical nature may be unknown \emph{a priori}. Here we introduce Gram-matrix tomography for independently prepared photons with pure or nearly pure internal states. For pure states, the Gram matrix contains all information required to predict the statistics of experiments that are insensitive to the internal degrees of freedom. We reconstruct this matrix, up to physically irrelevant phase choices, from a quadratic number of measurable Bargmann invariants (quantities encoding overlap magnitudes and collective phases). We access these quantities using two complementary approaches: targeted Fourier interferometers and two- and three-body photon-number correlations measured in a single random interferometer. We derive explicit error bounds that control the total-variation distance between predicted and actual outcome distributions for any subsequent experiment that is insensitive to the internal states. These guarantees account for finite sampling and deviations from purity. Crucially, the total sample complexity of the protocol is independent of the internal Hilbert-space dimension and polynomial under mild assumptions on overlaps of internal states. Our protocol uses standard interferometric measurements and turns them into a robust and operationally grounded characterization of partially distinguishable photons.

</details>


### [191] [Anti-symmetrization is cheap and useful](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06737)
*Adam Bene Watts,John Bostanci,Matthias C. Caro,Daniel Grier,Sihan Liu,Gaurav Mahajan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究未知混合量子态的反对称化计算任务，提出两种算法：一种使用 $\widetilde{O}(r/\lambda_{\min})$ 份拷贝的流式算法，另一种使用 $O(r/\lambda_{\min})$ 份拷贝的非流式算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反对称化是量子信息处理中的基础计算任务。研究如何在有限拷贝数下高效实现反对称化，并应用于量子密码学（单向态生成器的归约）与量子采样（弱Schur采样、酉Schur采样、纯度放大）等下游任务。

Method: 给定秩为 $r$ 的qudit混合态 $\rho=\sum_i \lambda_i|\varphi_i\rangle\langle\varphi_i|$ 的iid拷贝。第一种算法采用流式处理，仅使用受控SWAP和常数层单比特门，消耗 $\widetilde{O}(r/\lambda_{\min})$ 份拷贝；第二种非流式算法借助Schur-Weyl对偶工具实现，消耗 $O(r/\lambda_{\min})$ 份拷贝。

Result: 流式算法在流式环境下仅需极小工作内存即可运行，并给出从带隙混合态单向态生成器到纯态单向态生成器的归约。非流式算法在拷贝数 $n$ 与局部维度 $d$ 的某些区间内优于现有方法，应用于弱Schur采样、酉Schur采样及qudit态的最优纯度放大。

Conclusion: 反对称化问题可通过简单量子操作以接近最优的拷贝复杂度实现，并在量子密码学与量子采样等任务中带来性能提升。

Abstract: We consider the computational task of anti-symmetrization of an unknown mixed quantum state: Given iid copies of a rank-$r$ qudit state $ρ=\sum_{i=1}^r λ_i |\varphi_i\rangle\!\langle\varphi_i|$, prepare a copy of the state proportional to $Π_{\mathrm{anti}}^r (|\varphi_1\rangle\otimes \ldots\otimes|\varphi_r\rangle)$, with $Π_{\mathrm{anti}}^r$ the anti-symmetric subspace projector. We first give a simple algorithm for anti-symmetrization that uses $\widetilde{O}(r / λ_{\min})$ copies of $ρ$, where $λ_{\min}$ is the minimum non-zero eigenvalue of $ρ$. This algorithm works even in the streaming setting with small working memory, and it uses only simple quantum operations like controlled-SWAPs and constantly many layers of single-qubit gates. As an application of this streaming anti-symmetrization procedure, we give a reduction from gapped mixed-state one-way state generators to pure-state one-way state generators. Then, using tools from Schur-Weyl duality, we give and analyze a second (non-streaming) anti-symmetrization procedure that uses $O(r / λ_{\min})$ copies of $ρ$, which then serves as a building block in new algorithms for weak Schur sampling and unitary Schur sampling, outperforming existing approaches in some regimes of number of copies $n$ and local dimension $d$, as well as in an algorithm for optimal purity amplification for qudit states.

</details>


### [192] [Free entropy and quantum minimum description length](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06741)
*Patrick Hayden,Alexander Maloney,Jinzhao Wang,Yuxiang Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文定义了有限分辨率下的有限维物理自由熵，并证明其（相差显式常数）直接刻画了在已知特征值、未知特征基条件下编程多份量子态所需的最小量子内存，即量子最小描述长度，这是Schumacher压缩的一种变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Voiculescu的自由熵是Shannon熵的非交换类比，用于计数逼近算子的矩阵微观态，但它此前在量子理论中没有物理意义。本文旨在赋予自由熵物理意义，将其与量子信息中的资源量化问题联系起来。

Method: 作者定义了有限分辨率下、有限维度的物理自由熵，并分析了在量子态特征值已知但特征基未知的情形下，编程多份该状态所需的最小量子内存。该任务被表述为Schumacher压缩的一种变体，其最优代价被称为量子最小描述长度。

Result: 研究表明，物理自由熵在相差一个显式常数的意义下，直接刻画了该量子最小描述长度，从而为自由熵建立了明确的物理（量子信息）解释。

Conclusion: 本文为自由熵提供了量子信息论意义，并简要讨论了自由熵与各类量子熵之间的区别及其在量子物理中的潜在应用。

Abstract: In free probability, Voiculescu's free entropy counts matrix microstates that approximate an operator. It is a non-commutative analog of the Shannon entropy, distinct from any quantum entropy of a density operator, but it has had no physical meaning in quantum theory. We define a finite-dimensional physical free entropy at finite resolution and show that it directly characterizes, up to an explicit constant, the minimal quantum memory needed to program many copies of a state with known eigenvalues and unknown eigenbasis. This task is a variant of Schumacher compression, and we call its optimal cost the quantum minimum description length. We briefly discuss how free entropy differs from quantum entropies and its potential applications in quantum physics.

</details>


### [193] [Quantum minimum description of density matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06742)
*Patrick Hayden,Alexander Maloney,Jinzhao Wang,Yuxiang Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究Schumacher压缩的一个变体：在已知谱、未知特征基、且不保留纯化的情况下，压缩大量密度矩阵副本所需的最小存储代价。对固定维度和互异正特征值，给出了代价及其加性常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Schumacher压缩假设特征基已知，但实际问题中特征基常未知。本文关注在未知特征基、不保留纯化的约束下，压缩密度矩阵副本所需的最小存储代价这一变体问题。

Method: 可达性：将Werner克隆映射推广到GL(d,C)不可约表示，并用最高权差与行间隙控制有限迹距离界。逆命题：在谱间隙假设下，使用不可约群轨道上的Koashi-Imoto不可压缩性的定量形式。此外用Lean给出证明证书。

Result: 对固定维度与互异正特征值，获得了该压缩任务的代价及其加性常数，并建立了与通用无损编码开销之间的关系。

Conclusion: 刻画了未知特征基下密度矩阵压缩的量子最小描述长度及其加性常数，并揭示其与通用无损编码开销和自由熵的联系。

Abstract: We study a variant of Schumacher compression. The task asks for the minimal memory cost for compressing many copies of a density matrix with known spectrum and unknown eigenbasis, without preserving its purification. For fixed dimension and distinct positive eigenvalues, we obtain the cost through its additive constant. Achievability uses a generalization of Werner's cloning map to $\mathrm{GL}(d,\mathbb C)$ irreducible representations, with finite trace-distance bounds controlled by highest-weight differences and row gaps. The matching converse follows from a quantitative form of Koashi--Imoto incompressibility for irreducible group orbits under a spectral-gap assumption. We also identify the relation between this quantum minimum description length and universal lossless coding overhead, and its connection to free entropy is explained in a companion letter. We provide a Lean certificate for our proofs.

</details>


### [194] [Efficient Logic with Ultra-High-Rate Quantum Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06749)
*Nishad Maskara,Rohan Mehta,Zhiyang He,Varun Menon,J. Pablo Bonilla Ataides,Mikhail D. Lukin,Hengyun Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对超高码率量子LDPC码，提出基于群对称性的并行逻辑运算方法，通过轨道划分、并行手术与链映射卷积实现低时空开销的容错逻辑测量，并给出一系列距离保持的门构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超高码率qLDPC码虽能显著降低容错量子计算的量子比特开销，但缺乏高效、并行的逻辑运算方案，逻辑门实现往往带来巨大的时空开销，制约其实际可用性。

Method: 利用码的群对称性将逻辑量子比特划分为轨道；采用并行手术方法实现群不变逻辑Pauli乘积测量集合，使其具有高度可编程性（只要每个测量在整个轨道上重复，即可任意耦合逻辑轨道）；提出“链映射卷积（chain-map convolution）”技术，借助群结构高效提升协议的空間与时间距离。

Result: 为[[256, 68, 12]]、[[576, 148, 18]]配对划分码以及[[1152, 580, 12]]（码率1/2）码构造了保距并行测量gadget，仅需约2.5倍空间开销和3–4轮综合提取；同一方法还支持将培育魔态并行注入高码率码；通过子群限制扩展可编程性，揭示了逻辑控制能力与容错所需时空资源之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 结构化并行性是降低qLDPC逻辑运算时空开销的关键，所提方法为设计低开销容错qLDPC逻辑操作提供了新途径，有助于构建更高效的容错量子体系结构。

Abstract: Quantum low-density parity-check (qLDPC) codes offer a promising route to reducing the qubit overhead of fault tolerance, with recent ultra-high-rate constructions achieving encoding rates above one half. In this work, we develop fast and parallel logical computation methods for ultra-high-rate codes. We first utilize the group symmetries of the codes to partition the logical qubits into orbits, and then apply parallel surgery methods to implement group-invariant collections of logical Pauli-product measurements. These measurements are highly programmable, as we may arbitrarily couple logical orbits as long as each measurement is repeated across the whole orbit(s). To reduce the space and time overheads, we develop a technique called chain-map convolution, which uses the group structure to boost both the spatial and temporal distances of our protocol in a highly efficient manner. As case studies, we construct distance-preserving parallel measurement gadgets for the [[256, 68, 12]], [[576, 148, 18]] pair-partition codes, and a [[1152, 580, 12]] rate-$1/2$ code, with only around $2.5\times$ space overhead and 3-4 rounds of syndrome extraction each. The same methods also enable parallel injection of cultivated magic states into high-rate codes. Finally, we extend programmability through subgroup restrictions, revealing a tradeoff between logical control and the spacetime resources required for fault tolerance and highlighting the crucial role structured parallelism plays in reducing overhead. These results provide new methods for designing qLDPC logical operations with low spacetime overhead, opening a route toward more efficient fault-tolerant quantum architectures.

</details>


### [195] [Discovering New Problems for Decoded Quantum Interferometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06753)
*Rhik Mazumder,Pradeep Niroula*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了判定规则（soundness rules），通过码参数、译码保证和经典基线来筛选适合解码量子干涉（DQI）的候选优化问题；证明在计数满足约束的标准目标下DQI相对Prange补全的优势存在严格上界，并据此通过AI智能体搜索找到两个新候选应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DQI利用经典纠错码求解优化问题，但难以判断哪些问题能带来量子优势，缺乏可操作的筛选准则。作者希望建立一套判定规则，在候选问题层面上预先排除无法超越经典基线的场景。

Method: 提出基于码参数、译码保证与经典基线的soundness rules；针对计数满足约束的目标，在最坏情况Hamming译码下推导DQI相对Prange补全的优势上界；给出实值目标下的性能律（目标函数同时决定解质量与译码器需纠正的错误）；并借助AI智能体搜索，依据上述规则寻找候选应用。

Result: 证明最坏情况Hamming译码下DQI优势最多超出Prange补全$(\sqrt{2}-1)/2$，最优多项式交集（OPI）渐近达到该边际上限，故该设定下无候选可超过OPI；发现两个候选应用：基于McEliece型公钥的交替最大一致问题，以及由离散对数构造的背包型乘法多项式交集；对二者均证明了译码保证并给出与Prange重启的有限长度比较，在较大实例上DQI的期望质量超过固定预算内经典求解器所能达到的水平。

Conclusion: DQI的量子优势并非普适，其上限可由码参数与经典基线严格约束；结合实值目标性能律与AI辅助搜索，可系统性地定位有前景的应用场景，如交替最大一致与乘法多项式交集。

Abstract: Decoded quantum interferometry (DQI) uses classical error correction to solve optimization problems, but it is hard to tell which problems could give it a quantum advantage. We develop soundness rules that screen candidate problems by their code parameters, decoding guarantees and classical baselines. For the standard objective of counting satisfied constraints, we prove that under worst-case Hamming decoding DQI's guarantee can exceed Prange completion by at most $(\sqrt{2}-1)/2$. Optimal polynomial intersection (OPI) attains this margin cap asymptotically, so no candidate in this setting can exceed OPI's margin. We also derive a performance law for real-valued objectives: the objective fixes both the solution quality and which errors the decoder must correct. An AI-agent search guided by these rules found two candidate applications. Alternant max-agreement on McEliece-type public keys tests what a secret decoder is worth to DQI against classical solvers that see only the public instance. Multiplicative polynomial intersection, a knapsack-type problem built from discrete logarithms, pairs a cosine objective with a public decoder for signed errors. For both we prove decoding guarantees and finite-length comparisons with Prange restarts, and on the larger instances we tested, DQI's expected quality exceeds what our classical solvers reach within fixed budgets.

</details>


### [196] [Finite-bath projected ensembles in dual-unitary circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06754)
*Rahul Arvind,Nicholas Hunter-Jones*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究对偶幺正线路投影系综中的涌现随机性，在三个逆多项式假设下证明了多项式规模的热浴即可形成近似k-设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作证明当热浴尺寸趋于无穷时，双幺正砖墙线路经部分测量诱导的投影系综构成量子态设计，但有限热浴尺寸下的收敛速率尚未被严格建立。

Method: 考虑可解双幺正砖墙线路演化后对部分子系统测量，在未测子系统上得到投影系综；引入三个逆多项式假设——局域置换混合、回退运动上界、测量边界处收缩，并构建该系综涌现随机性的微观输运解释，将偏离局域置换算子的部分视为缺陷，与量子行走理论建立联系。

Result: 在上述假设下填补了收敛速率的空白，证明多项式尺寸的热浴足以形成近似k-设计。

Conclusion: 投影系综中的涌现随机性可通过对偶幺正线路的微观输运图像理解，缺陷与量子行走的类比为有限尺寸下的设计形成提供了严格刻画。

Abstract: We study the emergent randomness in projected ensembles generated by dual-unitary circuits. Specifically, we consider evolution by solvable dual-unitary brickwork circuits followed by measurements on part of the system, which induce an ensemble of states on the unmeasured subsystem. Previous work has established that this projected ensemble of states forms a quantum state design in the limit where the bath size is large, but the rate of convergence at finite bath size has not been rigorously established. Under three inverse-polynomial assumptions, local permutation mixing, a bound on recurrent backward motion, and contraction at the measured boundary, we fill this gap and prove that a bath of polynomial size suffices to form approximate $k$-designs.
