In establishing our results, we develop a microscopic transport interpretation of emergent randomness in the projected ensemble, in which deviations from local permutation operators appear as defects, and make connections with the theory of quantum walks.

</details>


### [197] [The Grothendieck Game and Self-Testing Continuous Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06755)
*Alexander Kulpe,Giulio Malavolta,Simon Schmidt,Michael Walter*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了 Grothendieck 博弈是 Majorana 可观测量（以及连续 pin 群的自旋表示）的鲁棒自检验，从而首次实现了对 Majorana 算子生成的连续测量族的鲁棒自检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自检验能够仅凭观测到的关联就认证量子态与测量，而无需知道产生它们的设备描述。此前尚缺乏对由 Majorana 算子生成的完整连续测量族的鲁棒自检验。

Method: 利用 Grothendieck 博弈——一种两名玩家分别接收球面方向并返回二元答案的非局域博弈——证明其是 Majorana 可观测量的自检验；并将该认证推广至 Majorana 可观测量的乘积。

Result: 任何接近最优的博弈表现都能认证整个连续参数化的量子测量族，进一步获得对连续 pin 群自旋表示的鲁棒自检验。

Conclusion: Grothendieck 博弈可作为 Majorana 可观测量及其乘积的鲁棒自检验工具，为连续测量族及连续 pin 群自旋表示提供了认证方案。

Abstract: Self-testing certifies quantum states and measurements from observed correlations alone, without requiring a description of the devices that produce them. In this work we establish, to the best of our knowledge, the first robust self-test of the full continuous family of measurements generated by Majorana operators. We achieve this by showing that the Grothendieck game, a two-player nonlocal game in which the players receive directions on a sphere and return binary answers, is a self-test of Majorana observables. That is, any near-optimal performance certifies an entire continuously parameterized family of quantum measurements. We further extend this certification to products of Majorana observables, obtaining a robust self-test of the spin representation of the continuous pin group.

</details>


### [198] [Nonadditivity in fermionic quantum Shannon theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06758)
*Farzin Salek,Amir-Reza Negari,Graeme Smith,Jens Eisert,Debbie Leung*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作在奇偶超选择与正则反对易关系下研究费米子系统的集体态制备与信道编码，精确求出二模高斯态的纠缠形成、信道最小输出熵与Holevo信息，并证明四类可加性问题在费米子情形下均不成立，同时给出经典、零误差经典、量子与零误差量子容量及强逆定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shor与Hastings提出的四类可加性问题在玻色/一般量子信道中已被研究，但费米子系统受奇偶超选择与反对易代数约束，其物理可允许的分解与输入集合受限。本文旨在建立这些可加性问题的费米子对应版本，并给出精确解，刻画奇偶超选择如何改变纠缠与信道容量的行为。

Method: 以二模高斯态τ为对象，在所有满足奇偶超选择的物理纯态分解上优化纠缠形成；对由τ关联的高斯信道，在所有物理信道输入上优化最小输出熵与Holevo信息；给出适用于任意Rényi阶α∈(0,∞]的形成与最小输出熵公式；并结合有限块长界与强逆分析，推导经典、零误差经典、量子与零误差量子容量。

Result: 单副本纠缠形成使正则化减半；τ⊗m的物理纠缠形成精确等于⌈m/2⌉log2，导致两份与一份形成值相同，违反可加性与强超可加性。关联高斯信道的最小输出熵与Holevo信息在任意块长下精确求出且均不可加。物理上单次使用不传输经典信息，两次使用可完美传输1比特；无辅助经典容量与零误差经典容量均为每模1/2比特，且有强逆：高于容量时解码成功概率趋于零。无辅助量子与零误差量子容量均为每模1/2逻辑量子比特，纠缠保真度在高于容量时趋于零。噪声高斯族在满秩下给出一份与两份形成值并证明严格不可加；τ在局域保奇偶测量下的Bell违反首次出现于三份。

Conclusion: 奇偶超选择使费米子纠缠与信道容量呈现显著的非可加性：形成值不随副本线性增长，正则化表现为半值，信道信息量亦不可加。优化由高斯系综与经典码达到，说明在物理约束下高斯/经典策略最优。经典与量子容量均为每模半比特/半逻辑量子比特，并伴随强逆定理，给出了费米子对应四类可加性问题的完整精确解答。

Abstract: We study collective state preparation and channel encoding in fermionic systems under parity superselection and composition governed by the canonical anticommutation relations. For a two-mode Gaussian state $τ$, we obtain the exact entanglement of formation, $E_{\mathrm F}^{\mathrm{phys}}(τ^{\otimes m})=\lceil m/2\rceil\log2$ for every $m\geq1$. Two copies have the same formation value as one, violating additivity and strong superadditivity, while regularization halves the single-copy value. For an associated Gaussian channel, we determine the minimum output entropy and Holevo information at every blocklength and prove nonadditivity of both. Together, these results give exact answers to the fermionic counterparts of the four additivity questions associated with Shor and Hastings. One physical use carries no classical information, whereas two uses transmit one bit perfectly. The unassisted classical and zero-error classical capacities both equal one half bit per mode. A sharp finite-block bound gives a strong converse: the decoding success probability vanishes at every rate above capacity. These optimizations cover all physical pure-state decompositions and channel inputs, with Gaussian ensembles and classical codes attaining the optima. The formation and minimum output entropy formulas hold for every Rényi order $α\in(0,\infty]$. We also determine the unassisted quantum and zero-error quantum capacities, both equal to one half logical qubit per mode, and prove a strong converse: entanglement fidelity vanishes at every rate above capacity. For a noisy Gaussian family, we determine the one- and two-copy formation values and prove strict nonadditivity at full rank. For $τ$, Bell violation under local parity-preserving measurements first appears at three copies.

</details>


### [199] [A classical oracle separation between QMA and QMA(2)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06760)
*Sabee Grewal,Saachi Mutreja,Kabir Tomer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个经典oracle，使得相对于该oracle有 QMA ≠ QMA(2)，其分离基于图上带符号边约束定义的可分离哈密顿量问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前已知 QMA 与 QMA(2) 在 oracle 下的关系尚不明确，作者希望给出相对于经典 oracle 的分离结果，澄清只允许可分离见证者是否会削弱验证能力。

Method: 定义一个可分离哈密顿量问题：由图上带符号的边约束给出。YES 实例存在零能量的乘积态；NO 实例利用反对称子空间构造，其零能量态是纠缠态，但不存在低能量的乘积态。QMA 下界使用压缩 oracle 技术，追踪量子计算在少量查询 YES 实例后所获得的信息，关键是将部分 oracle 记录的叠加映射到 NO 族，而不显著改变验证者的行为。

Result: 构造出经典 oracle，使得在此 oracle 下 QMA ≠ QMA(2)。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了 QMA 与 QMA(2) 相对于经典 oracle 的分离，表明通过可分离见证者限制验证者会带来本质差异，并为 oracle 下多见证者量子验证复杂性类的研究提供了新工具。

Abstract: We exhibit a classical oracle relative to which QMA \neq QMA(2). The separation is based on a separable Hamiltonian problem defined by signed edge constraints on a graph. The YES instances have zero-energy product states, while the NO instances, constructed using the antisymmetric subspace, have entangled zero-energy states but no low-energy product states. For the QMA lower bound, we use compressed-oracle techniques to track the information learned by a quantum computation making few queries to a YES instance. The key step is to map the resulting superpositions of partial oracle records to the NO family without significantly changing the verifier's behavior.

</details>


### [200] [Hypercontractivity and entropy decay for infinite-dimensional quantum Gibbs samplers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06764)
*Simon Becker,Cambyse Rouzé,Robert Salzmann*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文为一大类无穷维量子 Gibbs 采样器建立了相对熵的指数衰减速率，核心技术工具是超收缩估计与对数 Sobolev 不等式的微扰稳定性理论，并在 Bose-Hubbard、量子 Coulomb 气体等模型上给出指数熵衰减结果，同时构造了具有正谱隙但无有限时间�L^2到L^p�界（p>2）的周期充电模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子 Gibbs 采样器的收敛速率（相对熵或量子信道混合时间）是量子信息与统计物理交叉领域的核心问题。已有工作多局限于有限维或特���模型，对无穷维多模系统、含相互作用（如跳跃、斥力密度相互作用）的采样器缺乏统一且定量化的指数熵衰减理论，需要一个能覆盖广泛模型的稳定性框架。

Method: 提出超收缩估计与对数 Sobolev 不等式的微扰稳定性理论作为关键技术手段；通过适当选取耗散耦合，将无穷维量子 Gibbs 采样器化归为可应用该稳定性理论的形式。

Result: 对任意跳跃项与斥力密度相互作用的全多模 Bose-Hubbard 采样器，证明其熵指数衰减；对谐阱中的量子 Coulomb 气体与分子系统，证明其相互作用在任意固定有限秩低能截断下均具有指数熵衰减；并构造一个周期充电模型，其谱隙为正但不满足任何 p>2 的有限时间�L^2到L^p�有界性。

Conclusion: 该工作将超收缩与对数 Sobolev 不等式的稳定性方法推广至无穷维量子采样器，获得广泛模型类的指数相对熵衰减；同时揭示谱隙为正不足以保证有限时间超收缩性，说明更强条件对该类系统是必要的。

Abstract: We derive rates of exponential relative-entropy decay for a broad class of infinite-dimensional quantum Gibbs samplers. A key technical ingredient is a perturbative stability theory for hypercontractive estimates and logarithmic Sobolev inequalities. For suitably chosen dissipative couplings, we obtain exponential entropy decay for full multimode Bose-Hubbard samplers with arbitrary hopping and repulsive density interactions. For harmonically confined quantum Coulomb gases and molecular systems, we prove exponential entropy decay for every fixed finite-rank low-energy truncation of the interaction. Finally, we exhibit a periodic charging model with a positive spectral gap but no finite-time $L^2$-to-$L^p$ bound for any $p>2$.

</details>


### [201] [Fermions and the Tsirelson Problem in Real Quantum Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06767)
*Timothee Hoffreumon,Mischa P. Woods*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明实量子理论(RQT)在两类推广下仍与普通量子理论(QT)实验不可区分：允许分离测量用交换可观测量(而非张量积)表示，以及引入超选择规则(如费米子信息论)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者此前已证明RQT与QT实验不可区分。本文探究这种不可区分性在两种自然推广下是否成立：一是将分离测量由张量积可观测量推广为可交换可观测量；二是引入超选择规则，如费米子信息论中的情形。

Method: 首先在RQT中解决Tsirelson问题，证明交换测量与张量积测量生成相同的有限维关联；由于标准复数证明无法直接移植到实数域，需先在RQT中建立等价性。随后构建超选择理论的局域实量子表述，将RQT框架扩展至实数域上的超选择表示。

Result: (1) RQT中Tsirelson问题得到肯定回答：交换与张量积测量产生相同的有限维关联；(2) 建立了超选择理论的局域实量子表述，其费米子版本与费米子信息论在实验上不可区分，且该构造可推广至所有超选择量子理论。

Conclusion: RQT与QT的不可区分性在交换可观测量表示和超选择规则两种推广下均得以保持，表明实量子理论在更广泛的物理框架下仍具实验等价性。

Abstract: In a previous work, we showed that real quantum theory (RQT) cannot be experimentally distinguished from ordinary quantum theory (QT). In this paper, we investigate whether this indistinguishability survives two natural extensions of the setting: allowing separated measurements to be represented by commuting rather than tensor-product observables, and imposing superselection rules, as in fermionic information theory. We show that it does in both cases.
  Our first result is a positive answer to the Tsirelson problem in RQT: commuting and tensor-product measurements generate the same finite-dimensional correlations. This is surprisingly not a trivial consequence of the standard complex proof, as it does not carry over to the reals. The proof obstruction becomes particularly relevant in the presence of superselection rules, which is why we first establish the equivalence in RQT before extending the framework to superselected RQT.
  Our second result is a local, real quantum formulation of superselected theories. We extend the RQT framework to accommodate a representation of superselection over the reals and show, in particular, that it admits a fermionic version whose predictions are experimentally indistinguishable from those of fermionic information theory. More generally, the construction extends to all superselected QTs.

</details>


### [202] [Complexity separations for optimal matchgate-Clifford synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06768)
*Berta Casas,Diego García-Martín,Yuxuan Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究matchgate-Clifford群中的最优精确合成问题，证明其复杂度依赖门集的连通图结构：路径图和完全图下可O(n²)经典求解并给出最优编译器，树形图下判定问题为NP完全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: matchgate-Clifford群在随机费米子协议和matchgate合成中具有重要作用，其伴随作用同构于2n个Majorana模的单位行列式带号置换群。作者希望在给定目标酉算子和门集下，输出使用最少操作数的n量子比特电路，即研究该群中的最优精确合成问题，并揭示其复杂度与门集的关系。

Method: 研究由不同连通图的Majorana编织（braids）构成的门集。对路径图和完全图，证明问题可在O(n²)时间内经典求解，并给出显式的门最优编译器；对树形连通图，证明最优合成问题的判定版本为NP完全。

Result: 对路径图和完全图，问题具有O(n²)经典可解性并提供门最优编译器；当连通图为树时，最优合成判定问题NP完全。在n=80量子比特的链上，与Qiskit和Tket对比，总门数分别获得×2.57和×2.28的常数因子改进。

Conclusion: matchgate-Clifford群中精确合成问题的复杂度强烈依赖门集的连通图结构：部分图结构下可高效最优求解，树形结构下则NP完全；所提最优编译器在实际基准上优于Qiskit和Tket。

Abstract: Clifford and matchgate circuits are canonical families of classically simulable quantum circuits. Their intersection, the matchgate-Clifford group, plays an important role in randomized fermionic protocols and in matchgate synthesis. Its adjoint action is isomorphic to the group of unit-determinant signed permutations of $2n$ Majorana modes, and we study optimal exact synthesis in this group. That is, given a target unitary and a gate set, output an $n$-qubit circuit implementing the target using the fewest operations. We show that the complexity of this problem strongly depends on the gate set. In particular, we study gate sets consisting of Majorana braids with different connectivity graphs. For path and complete graphs, we prove that the problem is classically solvable in $\mathcal{O}\left(n^2\right)$ time, and we provide explicit gate-optimal compilers. In addition, we prove that when the connectivity graph is a tree, the decision version of the optimal synthesis problem becomes NP-complete. Finally, we benchmark our optimal compiler on chains of up to $n=80$ qubits against those of \texttt{Qiskit} and \texttt{Tket}, obtaining circuits with constant factor improvements $\times2.57$ and $\times2.28$ in the total number of gates, respectively.

</details>


### [203] [Fast convergence of propagation algorithms for open quantum systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06769)
*Giorgio Facelli,Hamza Fawzi,Omar Fawzi,Cambyse Rouzé,Sam Slezak,Daniel Stilck França*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立传播算法快速收敛的一般条件，统一处理自旋与费米子体系，并将结果应用于噪声量子动力学模拟和Gibbs态局域可观测量计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子多体系统中局域可观测量期望值的经典模拟效率依赖于传播算法的收敛速度。已有工作给出的可模拟时间尺度（如log(1/u)）仍有提升空间，且缺乏统一处理噪声演化、费米子系统与Gibbs态的一般性收敛框架。

Method: 提出适用于自旋和费米子体系的传播算法快速收敛的一般充分条件，核心机制是演化算子的收缩性（contractivity）对局域相互作用所生成算符增长的控制；将该框架分别应用于任意相互作用图上局域哈密顿量生成的噪声时间演化，以及以Gibbs态为稳态的伪Lindbladian。

Result: 对任意相互作用图上的噪声演化，证明当噪声强度λ相对相互作用图度数足够大时动力学可高效模拟；一般情形下给出由相互作用强度u、噪声强度λ和相互作用结构决定的高效时间上界t_max。无噪声极限λ=0时，可模拟时间尺度由log(1/u)扩展至t_max~1/u，与Zhao、Marvian和Tong的最新结果一致。对Gibbs态，证明高温下传播算法快速收敛，弱相互作用体系（强度u）可适用至逆温度β~log(1/u)。

Conclusion: 收缩性是控制局域相互作用增长、实现高效经典模拟的一般机制。该框架统一了噪声动力学与Gibbs态两类问题，改进了已知的时间尺度上界，并为自旋与费米子体系提供了普适的收敛判据。

Abstract: We establish general conditions for the rapid convergence of propagation algorithms for computing expectation values of local observables in quantum many-body systems, covering both spin and fermionic systems. Our results apply to evolutions that are sufficiently contractive, providing a general mechanism by which contractivity controls the growth generated by local interactions and enables efficient classical simulation. As a first application, we consider noisy time evolution generated by local Hamiltonians on arbitrary interaction graphs. We show that the dynamics can be efficiently simulated when the noise strength $λ$ is sufficiently large compared to the degree of the interaction graph. More generally, for systems with an interaction strength $u$, our bounds determine a time horizon $t_{\text{max}}$ as a function of the interaction strength $u$, the noise strength $λ$, and the interaction structure below which the algorithm is efficient. In the noiseless limit, $λ=0$, our analysis extends the efficiently simulable time scale from $\log(1/u)$ proved in Facelli, Fawzi, and Fawzi (2026) to $t_{\text{max}} \sim 1/u$ matching the recent improvement by Zhao, Marvian and Tong (2026). As a second application, we apply the same framework to the computation of local observables in Gibbs states of local Hamiltonians. We consider a pseudo-Lindbladian whose steady state is the Gibbs state and show that, at sufficiently high temperature, its propagation algorithm converges rapidly. In particular, for weakly-interacting systems with interaction strength $u$, our result applies up to inverse temperatures $β\sim\log(1/u)$.

</details>


### [204] [Engineering logical gates with irrep surface codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06775)
*Yabo Li,Zijian Song,Vieri Mattei,Naren Manjunath,Apoorv Tiwari,Tyler D. Ellison*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"irrep surface codes"（ISC），一类二维拓扑量子纠错码，可横向实现任意满足有限生成且不可约条件的逻辑门集合，并配套发展格点手术协议以实现与常规surface code计算之间的信息转移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子计算通常依赖固定的逻辑门原语集合，实现其他所需操作需要消耗大量资源。Eastin-Knill定理限制了横向逻辑门集合的有限性，因此需要系统性地设计编码以支持特定目标逻辑门。

Method: 构造ISC二维拓扑码，通过要求目标门集合满足两个条件来设计横向逻辑门：(i) 生成有限群（受Eastin-Knill定理约束，模全局相位）；(ii) 不可约，即任何与门集合对易的算子必须正比于恒等算子。该框架可容纳任意有限对角门群和任意可逆经典门，因为均可嵌入更大的不可约有限门群。同时发展格点手术协议，实现surface code与满足两类充分条件的ISC之间的编码信息转移。

Result: ISC可横向实现任意有限对角门群、任意可逆经典门，以及超出Clifford层级任何有限层级的逻辑门；格点手术协议实现了surface code与ISC之间的信息转移。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了为目标逻辑操作定制拓扑码并将其集成到常规计算中的系统性方法。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum computation typically relies on a fixed set of logical primitives, while realizing other desired operations can require substantial resources. We introduce irrep surface codes (ISCs), two-dimensional topological quantum error-correcting codes engineered to transversally implement a prescribed set of logical gates. The engineered gates only need to satisfy two conditions: (i) they generate a finite group, which, up to global phases, is forced by the Eastin-Knill theorem, and (ii) they are irreducible, meaning that any operator commuting with the set of gates must be proportional to the identity. This accommodates arbitrary finite groups of diagonal gates and arbitrary reversible classical gates, since each can be embedded into a larger irreducible finite group of gates. It also enables logical gates outside every finite level of the Clifford hierarchy. To incorporate these operations into surface-code computations, we further develop lattice-surgery protocols to transfer the encoded information between surface codes and ISCs obeying either of two sufficient conditions. Together, these results establish a systematic method for tailoring topological codes to desired logical operations and integrating them into conventional computations.

</details>


### [205] [Informed Trading model with quantum walks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06779)
*H. W. Shawn Liew,Patrick Rebentrost*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将Kyle模型扩展至资产未来价值服从经典随机游走与连续时间量子行走两种情形，解析近似并数值求解风险中性做市商与知情交易者之间的市场均衡。经典均衡中知情交易者采用线性策略、做市商定价呈logistic曲线，利润随时间次线性增长；量子均衡中交易者策略呈多簇结构、定价规则出现多重跳跃，但知情交易者利润随时间线性增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Kyle模型仅针对单资产、既定信息结构的市场刻画知情交易者的价格冲击。作者希望通过改变资产未来价值的随机过程设定（经典随机游走 vs. 连续时间量子行走），考察不同信息/价格演化机制如何影响知情交易者与做市商之间的均衡结构及交易者获利的时间标度。

Method: 将标的资产未来价值分别建模为经典随机游走和连续时间量子行走；在该设定下同时解析近似与数值求解风险中性做市商与知情交易者之间的均衡；比较两种情形下知情交易者的交易策略、做市商的定价规则以及利润随时间的增长行为。

Result: 经典均衡：知情交易者策略为线性，做市商定价规则近似logistic曲线，知情交易者利润随时间t次线性增长。量子均衡：知情交易者策略呈现多个簇，定价规则对应出现多重跳跃；均衡非线性程度提高，但知情交易者利润随时间t线性增长。

Conclusion: 资产未来价值的随机过程设定会显著改变Kyle型市场均衡的结构与利润标度：经典随机游走产生线性策略、logistic定价与次线性利润；量子行走导致簇状策略、跳跃式定价与线性利润，表明量子式价格演化可在更复杂均衡下带来更强的时间线性获利能力。

Abstract: The seminal Kyle model offers a framework to understand the price impact of an informed trader in a market model for a single asset. We study variants in which the asset's future value is modeled by a classical random walk and by a continuous-time quantum walk, respectively. With these assumptions, we analytically approximate and numerically identify the market equilibrium between risk-neutral market maker and informed trader. In the classical equilibrium, the informed trader possesses a linear strategy, whereas the market maker's pricing rule resembles a logistic curve. The informed trader's profit is also identified to grow sub-linearly with time t. In the quantum equilibrium, the informed trader's strategy forms multiple clusters and the pricing rule exhibits multiple jumps correspondingly. Despite the increasing non-linearity of the equilibrium, the informed trader's profit grows linearly with time t.

</details>


### [206] [Pretty good shadow tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06781)
*Sabee Grewal,Angelos Pelecanos,Jack Spilecki,Ewin Tang,John Wright*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个使用 O(log(m)log(m/δ)/ε²) 份量子态副本的影子层析（shadow tomography）算法，通过平均情况分析下的 pretty good measurement 与 minimax 原理（非构造性地）导出最坏情况保证，并指出该方法固有的障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 影子层析问题中，经典对应问题的复杂度约为 O(log(m)/ε²)，而量子问题存在额外的 log(m) 因子差距。作者希望缩小这一差距，并理解该差距是否可由现有技术消除。

Method: 在平均情况设定下：从已知先验中随机抽取量子态，对 O(log(m)/ε²) 份副本施加 pretty good measurement，得到对先验中绝大多数态在所有可观测量上的常数误差保证；再对该保证进行 boosting，得到平均情况影子层析算法；最后利用 minimax 原理，非构造性地推出存在具有相同最坏情况保证的测量方案。

Result: 算法使用 O(log(m)log(m/δ)/ε²) 份输入态副本，对所有 i∈[m] 输出 tr(O_i ρ) 的估计，误差在 ±ε 以内，失败概率不超过 δ。同时识别出该方法的固有障碍：pretty good measurement 能放大关于先验随机性的成功概率，但不能放大关于测量随机性的成功概率。

Conclusion: 所提算法达到 O(log(m)log(m/δ)/ε²) 的副本复杂度。作者认为上述障碍是该方法的本质限制，它解释了量子问题复杂度与经典对应问题之间残留的 log(m) 因子差距。

Abstract: We give a shadow tomography algorithm that uses $O(\log(m) \log(m/δ) / \varepsilon^2)$ copies of the input state. Specifically, given a quantum state $ρ$ and observables $O_1,\ldots,O_m$, the algorithm outputs estimates of $\mathrm{tr}(O_iρ)$ for all $i\in[m]$ that are all accurate to within $\pm\varepsilon$, except with probability at most $δ$. The key idea is to work in the average-case setting: for a random state drawn from a known prior, a pretty good measurement on $O(\log(m)/\varepsilon^2)$ copies gives a constant-error guarantee on all but a small fraction of the prior. Boosting this guarantee gives an average-case shadow tomography algorithm, and the minimax principle then (non-constructively) implies the existence of a measurement with the same worst-case guarantee.
  We also identify an obstruction to further improvement with this approach. While pretty good measurements amplify success over the randomness of the prior, they do not amplify success over the randomness of measurement. This issue appears to be inherent, and gives rise to the remaining $\log(m)$ factor separating the complexity of the quantum problem from its classical counterpart.

</details>


### [207] [Quantum-optimized phase closure for robust interferometric imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06786)
*Zhenning Liu,Emil T. Khabiboulline,Lorcan O. Conlon,Brittany McClinton,Aqil Sajjad,Saikat Guha,Jayadev Rajagopal,Alexey V. Gorshkov,Daniel Gottesman,Stephen T. Ridgway*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明利用多光子联合测量可更精确、更省光子地测量closure phase，并在大阵列下以三光子联合测量方案实现光子代价随阵列规模近线性增长的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地面干涉阵列中，望远镜相关的大气相位涨落会破坏成像相位信息；closure phase通过闭合三角形基线相位求和来抵消这类误差，因此提升其测量效率、精度与鲁棒性是光学干涉成像的关键问题。作者希望借助量子信息处理技术，在有限光子条件下改进closure phase测量。

Method: 作者首先从理论上确定，在未知的望远镜相关相位噪声下，closure phase信息至少需要同一大气时间相位稳定窗口内的三个光子才能保留；随后研究联合三光子测量在三望远镜最小场景中的优势。针对大阵列，作者以全局均方误差为准则估计所有bispectra，并利用visibility的物理约束分析总信息量增长；据此构造最多使用三光子联合测量、光子代价为Õ(m)的高效协议，并与顺序单光子协议的Ω(m²)光子需求对比。最后用多参数量子度量学分析高精度区域中的集体测量优势。

Result: 理论结果表明，联合三光子测量已在最小三望远镜设置中优于单光子测量；m望远镜阵列虽有Θ(m²)个独立三角形bispectra，但在固定精度下总信息量仅近似随m线性增长。所构造协议使用最多三光子联合测量，光子代价为Õ(m)，而任意顺序单光子协议需要Ω(m²)光子。当每个时间稳定窗口内探测光子数随阵列规模线性增长时，所需曝光时间可保持与m无关，至多相差对数因子。高精度区域中，集体测量也表现出类似优势。

Conclusion: 量子信息处理技术可显著提升光学干涉测量的灵敏度与鲁棒性；多光子联合测量为closure phase及bispectra估计提供了在光子效率和阵列扩展性上均优于传统单光子顺序方案的新途径。

Abstract: Closure phase enables imaging with ground-based interferometric arrays by canceling telescope-dependent phase errors due to atmospheric fluctuations. It can be obtained by measuring fringe phases on individual baselines forming a triangle and summing them. We show that closure phases can be measured more accurately with fewer photons by performing joint measurements on multiple photons. We first establish that at least three photons from the same atmospheric temporal phase-stability window are required for closure-phase information to survive the unknown telescope-dependent phase noise, and show that joint three-photon measurements already provide an advantage over single-photon measurements in the minimal three-telescope setting. We then turn to large arrays and consider estimating all bispectra---complex quantities whose phases are the closure phases---under a global mean-square error criterion. Although an $m$-telescope array contains $Θ(m^2)$ independent triangle bispectra, we show that physical constraints on the visibilities make the total information content grow only nearly linearly with $m$ at fixed accuracy. Exploiting this structure, we construct an efficient protocol using at most three-photon joint measurements with photon cost $\tilde{O}(m)$, whereas any sequential single-photon protocol requires $Ω(m^2)$ photons. When the number of detected photons per temporal stability window scales linearly with the array size, this allows the required exposure time to remain independent of $m$ up to logarithmic factors. We further analyze the complementary high-precision regime using multiparameter quantum metrology and find a similar advantage for collective measurements. Overall, our results show that quantum information processing techniques can substantially improve the sensitivity and robustness of optical interferometry.

</details>


### [208] [Computational entanglement distillation beyond pure states: efficient protocols and fundamental limitations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06787)
*Takeru Utsumi,Jacopo Rizzo,Ryuji Takagi,Lorenzo Leone*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究有限资源下的混合态纠缠蒸馏，证明Rényi相干信息在样本高效与计算高效两种约束下均刻画了最坏情形最优速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统纠缠信息论依赖任意多输入副本与无界复杂度的操作，而现实中资源有限；现有有限资源纠缠理论仅对纯态的理想情形有较充分探索，混合态纠缠蒸馏的最优速率刻画仍缺失。

Method: 在两种资源约束下分析混合态纠缠蒸馏：(1) 样本高效机制，允许无界计算能力；(2) 计算高效前沿，协议须多项式时间。构造满足多项式秩约束的通用协议，并构造反例态（可蒸馏纠缠近乎广泛，但任何通用样本高效协议速率不超过其Rényi相干信息）证明最优性；进一步将同界推广至输入态已知的高效协议。

Result: 在样本高效机制中实现该速率且无需预知输入态（在多项式秩约束下），并证明其为通用协议最优；在计算高效前沿，即使输入态已知，任何高效协议仍受同一界限制，且在额外边际秩多项式约束下另一通用协议可高效达到该速率。

Conclusion: Rényi相干信息获得新的操作意义：既刻画有限副本下通用纠缠蒸馏的最坏情形速率，也刻画即便已知输入态时高效蒸馏的最坏情形速率。

Abstract: The information-theoretic approach to entanglement typically relies on arbitrarily many input copies and unboundedly complex operations; however, the available resources are constrained in reality. This motivates the study of entanglement theory with limited resources. One of its central questions is which quantities characterize entanglement manipulations, and so far, only the idealized case of pure states has been well explored. In this work, we address entanglement distillation from mixed states and identify the Rényi coherent information as the quantity determining the optimal rate in the worst case under two distinct resource constraints. We first characterize the sample-efficient regime, in which unbounded computational power is allowed. Our universal protocol achieves this rate without knowledge of the input state under certain polynomial rank constraints. This rate is optimal among universal protocols: we construct states whose distillable entanglement is nearly extensive, yet from which no universal sample-efficient protocol attains a rate above their Rényi coherent information. We then push this characterization to the computationally efficient frontier: the same bound holds for any efficient protocol even when the input state is known, and another universal protocol achieves this rate efficiently under additional polynomial constraints on the marginal ranks. Overall, our results give the Rényi coherent information a novel operational meaning in two computational settings: universal distillation with limited copies and efficient distillation even with knowledge of the input state.

</details>


### [209] [Characterizing Quantum Advantage for Generalizations of the Boolean Hidden Matching Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06789)
*Mark Bun,Joao F. Doriguello,John Kallaugher,Nadezhda Voronova*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究 $f$-Boolean Hidden Partition 问题的一路通信复杂度，用 $f$ 的符号次数 $d$ 刻画其经典与量子通信界，证明了 Doriguello 和 Montanaro (TQC 2020) 的猜想，并完全刻画了具有多项式量子优势的函数类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题推广了 Boolean Hidden Matching 与 Hidden Hypermatching（即 $f$ 为奇偶函数的情形）。此前工作仅对 $d \le 1$ 的经典协议和 $d \le 2$ 的量子协议给出对数上界，并对部分结构化大符号次数函数给出多项式下界，缺少对一般 $f$ 的完整刻画，因此需要建立以符号次数 $d$ 为参数的统一通信界。

Method: 通过引入 $f$ 的符号次数 $d$ 作为复杂度参数，分析 $f$-Boolean Hidden Partition 问题在一路通信模型下的随机经典与量子通信复杂度，建立经典情形 $Θ(n^{1-1/d})$ 的紧界，并给出量子情形的上下界。

Result: 对任意符号次数 $d \ge 2$ 的函数 $f$，其随机经典一路通信复杂度为 $Θ(n^{1-1/d})$；量子一路通信复杂度介于 $Ω(n^{1-2/d})$ 与 $O(n^{1-1/\lceil d/2 \rceil} \log n)$ 之间。由此完全刻画了具有多项式量子优势的函数恰为 $d \ge 2$ 者，且 $d=2$ 情形给出新的无限族指数分离。

Conclusion: 符号次数 $d$ 决定了 $f$-Boolean Hidden Partition 的经典与量子一路通信复杂度阶，$d \ge 2$ 是存在多项式量子优势的充要条件，验证并推广了 Doriguello–Montanaro 的猜想。

Abstract: We study the one-way communication complexity of the $f$-Boolean Hidden Partition problem. Here, Alice is given an $n$-bit string and Bob is given $Ω(n)$ disjoint blocks of its indices together with a string of labels. Under the promise that the evaluations of $f$ on these blocks either agree with all of the labels or disagree with all of them, Bob must determine which is the case using a single message from Alice. This problem generalizes the Boolean Hidden Matching and Hidden Hypermatching problems, which capture the special case where $f$ is the parity function.
  We establish classical and quantum communication bounds for $f$-Boolean Hidden Partition in terms of the sign degree $d$ of $f$, proving a conjecture of Doriguello and Montanaro (TQC 2020). Their prior work gave logarithmic communication upper bounds for classical protocols when $d \le 1$ and quantum protocols when $d \le 2$, as well as polynomial lower bounds for certain structured functions with larger sign degree. We show that for every $f$ of sign degree $d \ge 2$, the randomized classical communication complexity of this problem is $Θ(n^{1-1/d})$ while its quantum communication complexity lies between $Ω(n^{1-2/d})$ and $\bO{n^{1-1/\lceil d/2 \rceil} \log n}$. This completely characterizes the functions $f$ admitting polynomial quantum advantage as those for which $d \ge 2$, with a new infinite family of exponential separations given by the case $d = 2$.

</details>


### [210] [Local universality of non-local quantum resources in rotationally invariant scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06791)
*Silas R. Beane,Roland C. Farrell*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将散射相位移动导出的纠缠功率推广至任意自旋J的闭合形式，并证明多种非局域量子资源（纠缠、非局域magic）的生成能力在领头阶仅取决于与最近非纠缠门的投影Hilbert-Schmidt距离平方。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弹性散射可在两出射粒子自旋间产生纠缠与非局域magic，其产生量由作用在双自旋-J联合Hilbert空间上的S矩阵（幺正量子门）编码。既有工作仅针对J=1/2、1、3/2的旋转不变S矩阵，用散射相位移动给出了纠缠功率，缺乏对任意J的统一描述，也未厘清不同量子资源生成能力之间的共性。

Method: 对旋转不变的S矩阵，推导任意自旋J下纠缠功率关于散射相位移动的闭合形式解；进而分析一大类非局域量子资源（含纠缠功率与非局域magic功率）的领头阶行为，将其与到最近非纠缠门的投影Hilbert-Schmidt距离平方相联系，考察其对相互作用细节的依赖性。

Result: 得到适用所有J的纠缠功率闭合形式表达式。证明广泛类别的非局域量子资源在领头阶仅取决于到最近非纠缠门的投影Hilbert-Schmidt距离平方；该结论同时适用于纠缠功率与非局域magic功率，不同资源的区别仅体现在整体归一化因子上。

Conclusion: 散射的领头资源生成能力具有普适的几何起源，即仅由S矩阵到最近非纠缠门的Hilbert-Schmidt距离决定，而与相互作用细节无关；不同非局域资源共享同一几何标度，仅归一化不同。

Abstract: Elastic scattering can generate entanglement and non-local magic between the spins of the two outgoing particles. The amount of entanglement and magic produced is encoded in the S-matrix; a unitary quantum gate that acts on the joint Hilbert space of the two spin-J particles. Previous work derived the entanglement power in terms of the scattering phase shifts for rotationally invariant S-matrices with J=1/2,1,3/2. In this paper, a closed form solution is derived for all J. We further prove that a broad class of non-local quantum resources depend to leading order only on the squared projective Hilbert-Schmidt distance from the nearest non-entangling gate. This holds for the entanglement power and non-local magic power, and the choice of resource simply changes the overall normalization. This universality establishes a common geometric origin for the leading resource-generating capacity of scattering, independent of the details of the interaction.

</details>


### [211] [Error Mitigation From a Single Unmodified Error-Corrected Circuit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06792)
*Maor Shutman,Dorit Aharonov,Itai Arad,Yosi Atia,Eyal Bairey,Zvika Brakerski,Barak A. Katzir,Netanel H. Lindner,Omri Golan,Ilya Gurwich*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出SOLEM框架，完全在后期处理中利用综合征数据进行逻辑错误缓解，无需修改纠错电路，运行时开销比外部LEM指数级降低，且与SALEM相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有综合征感知的逻辑错误缓解（SALEM）虽比外部LEM在运行时开销上呈指数级降低，但通常需要基于综合征实时处理的自适应电路操作，实现复杂。作者希望消除对电路修改与实时处理的需求，同时保留综合征信息的优势。

Method: SOLEM用自然产生的、以综合征为条件的逻辑错误信道替代刻意的电路修改，将综合征数据完全用于后期处理。在此框架下开发了无偏估计量与启发式估计量，包括离线的逻辑版概率错误消除（PEC）与零噪声外推（ZNE），以及一个全局回归估计量。

Result: SOLEM具备三项优势：1）后期处理可用比实时更强的解码器处理综合征；2）同一测量数据可构造多个估计量并用经验统计误差等指标优化；3）逻辑错误表征仅在后期处理进行，表征数据可在电路执行期间采集，降低对硬件漂移的敏感性。其运行时开销比外部LEM指数级降低，与相应SALEM估计量相当。

Conclusion: SOLEM通过将综合征信息完全用于后期处理，在无需修改纠错电路的前提下实现逻辑错误缓解，兼具实现简单、可优化性强与低运行时开销的优点，为量子纠错中的错误缓解提供了更实用的途径。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that syndrome information obtained during error-corrected quantum computation can be utilized for logical error mitigation (LEM), yielding exponential reductions in runtime overhead compared to external'' LEM methods, which do not make use of syndrome data. However, these syndrome-aware LEM methods (SALEM) generally require adaptive circuit operations based on real-time processing of syndromes. Here, we introduce syndrome-based offline logical error mitigation (SOLEM), a framework that utilizes syndrome data entirely in post-processing, without any modification to the given error-corrected circuit. In this sense, SOLEM is built into'' error-corrected circuits, and is in fact simpler to implement than external LEM protocols. The basic idea of SOLEM is to use naturally occurring syndrome-conditioned logical error channels in place of deliberate circuit modifications. We develop unbiased and heuristic estimators within this framework, including offline logical versions of probabilistic error cancellation (PEC) and zero-noise extrapolation (ZNE), as well as a global-regression estimator. SOLEM's fully offline nature leads to three significant advantages. First, SOLEM can post-process syndromes with a more powerful decoder than the one used in real time. Second, multiple estimators can be constructed from the same measured data and optimized using empirical statistical errors and related performance metrics. Third, logical error characterization is needed only in post-processing, allowing characterization data to be collected during circuit execution and reducing sensitivity to hardware drift. Remarkably, SOLEM estimators can achieve these advantages while attaining a runtime overhead which is exponentially lower than that of external LEM and is comparable to that of corresponding SALEM estimators.

</details>


### [212] [Testing bosonic and fermionic Gaussian states with a constant number of copies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06794)
*Jop Briët,Davi Castro-Silva,Michael Walter,Freek Witteveen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究玻色子与费米子高斯量子态的检验问题，证明常数个副本即可判定给定状态是否为高斯态或与任何高斯态的重叠有界，改进了此前随模式数（系统规模）增长的副本需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯态在量子物理与量子信息处理中居核心地位，而判定一个量子态是否为高斯态（或与所有高斯态重叠很小）是基础性任务。此前的检验方案需要随模式数（系统规模）增长的副本数，开销大，因此作者希望降低副本复杂度至常数级别。

Method: 基于同态多份高斯态副本的对称性，并通过对高斯态集合的距离进行界定——利用梯度流以及先前在球面上 Kac 随机游走研究中得到的谱界来估计到高斯态集合的距离。

Result: 证明常数个状态副本就足以完成检验，即副本数不再依赖模式数（系统规模），显著优于此前随模式数增长的方案。

Conclusion: 高斯态检验的样本复杂度可降至常数，其关键在于高斯态副本的对称性与基于梯度流和谱界的距离估计方法，这对量子态检验与量子信息处理具有理论与实用价值。

Abstract: Bosonic and fermionic Gaussian quantum states are families of quantum states that play a central role in quantum physics and quantum information processing. We study the problem of testing whether a given state is either Gaussian, or has bounded overlap with any Gaussian state. Previous analyses of this testing problem led to tests that require a number of copies of the state that scales with the number of modes (the system size). In this work we show that a constant number of copies of the state suffices. We derive these results based on the symmetries of identical copies of Gaussian states, and by bounding the distance to the set of Gaussian states through a gradient flow and a spectral bound studied previously for the Kac random walk on a sphere.

</details>


### [213] [From Quantum Tunneling to Classical Optimization through Instantons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06797)
*Jiayu Shen,Dylan Herman,Guneykan Ozgul,Akshay Seshadri,Junhyung Lyle Kim,Shree Hari Sureshbabu,Shouvanik Chakrabarti,Niraj Kumar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过瞬子分析证明，由路径积分蒙特卡洛启发的经典优化算法PISGD可继承量子隧穿机制，其逃离局部极小值的逃逸时间按反弹作用量而非势垒高度指数缩放，并数值验证了对可分离与不可分离势能均成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 优化的常见瓶颈是解被势垒困于局部极小值，经典噪声驱动优化器（如SGD）的逃逸时间随势垒高度指数增长。作者希望探究量子隧穿机制能否被引入并改善经典优化，尤其关注该机制是否必须依赖真正的量子动力学。

Method: 采用基于瞬子（instanton）的分析方法，考察从路径积分蒙特卡洛（PIMC）实例化而来的路径积分随机梯度下降（PISGD）。将Kramers逃逸率理论推广到路径空间，推导逃逸时间与反弹作用量（等于两势阱间Agmon距离的两倍）的关系，并对可分离与非可分离势函数进行数值实现验证。

Result: PISGD在经典非凸全局优化中获得了与量子隧穿相同的指数缩放规律：逃逸时间按反弹作用量而非势垒高度指数增长。数值实验在可分离和非可分离势上均确认了该缩放关系。当势垒高而薄时，该方法可比经典方法更快逃离局部极小值。但PISGD仅保留非相干隧穿，缺少多条隧穿路径间的相干性。

Conclusion: PISGD作为经典算法也能继承非相干量子隧穿机制，从而在特定势垒几何下优于经典噪声优化器。然而由于缺乏相干性，本文结论并不能排除量子隧穿类算法在整体上可能相对经典算法具有量子优势。

Abstract: We use an instanton-based analysis to show that Path Integral Stochastic Gradient Descent (PISGD), a classical optimization algorithm instantiated from path integral Monte Carlo (PIMC), can inherit the quantum tunneling mechanism. Quantum tunneling is the phenomenon in which a quantum particle passes through a potential barrier that a classical trajectory cannot cross in the same way. This mechanism can contribute to the performance of quantum optimization algorithms, since a common bottleneck in optimization is that the solution becomes trapped in a local minimum, separated from better solutions by a potential barrier. Classical noise-driven optimizers, such as stochastic gradient descent (SGD), have escape times that scale exponentially with the potential barrier height. For quantum tunneling, the escape time is instead exponential in the bounce action, which equals twice the Agmon distance between the two basins. When the barrier is tall but thin, quantum tunneling can allow escaping local minima faster than such classical methods. Remarkably, this scaling is not exclusive to algorithms with genuine quantum dynamics. For PISGD applied to classical nonconvex global optimization, we adapt Kramers' escape-rate theory to path space and obtain the same exponential scaling as quantum tunneling, in terms of the bounce action rather than the barrier height. We confirm this scaling numerically by implementing PISGD for both separable and nonseparable potentials. That said, PISGD retains only incoherent tunneling, since coherence among multiple tunneling paths is absent. Our analysis therefore does not rule out a potential quantum advantage for quantum-tunneling-based algorithms over classical ones in general.

</details>


### [214] [Bell nonlocality does not imply violation of nonlocal games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06799)
*Ricardo Faleiro,Lucas E. A. Porto,Marco Túlio Quintino,Flavien Hirsch*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明，在通常定义（确定性判定谓词）下，非局域博弈无法作为贝尔非局域性的完备见证：存在不违反任何此类博弈的非局域行为，且量子力学可产生这种行为，该现象在满足 $X,Y\geq2$、$A,B\geq4$ 的所有两体场景中存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非局域博弈为推理贝尔非局域性提供了概念上清晰的框架，被广泛用作非局域性的判据与见证。然而其原始且最常用的定义（带确定性谓词）是否足以刻画所有非局域行为，此前并不清楚，本文旨在检验该框架作为非局域性完备见证的能力。

Method: 从非局域博弈的原始定义出发，即采用确定性判定谓词（deterministic predicate），在一般两体场景 $(X,Y,A,B)$ 中进行构造性证明，论证存在某些非局域行为不违反任何此类非局域博弈，并进一步构造量子策略以产生这些“博弈经典”（game-classical）的非局域行为。

Result: 证明了存在非局域行为不违反任何确定性谓词的非局域博弈；进一步证明量子力学可产生这类行为；该现象在每一个满足 $X,Y\geq2$ 且 $A,B\geq4$ 的两体场景中均存在。

Conclusion: 以确定性谓词定义的非局域博弈并非贝尔非局域性的完备见证，存在量子可实现的非局域行为无法被其捕获。这表明需要更一般的判定结构（如非确定性谓词或其他见证形式）来完整刻画非局域性。

Abstract: Nonlocal games offer a conceptually clear way to reason about Bell nonlocality. However, we show that as originally defined, and most commonly used, i.e. with a deterministic predicate, they cannot be complete witnesses of nonlocality. Concretely, we prove the existence of nonlocal behaviours which do not violate any such nonlocal game. Furthermore, we also show that quantum mechanics can produce these game-classical nonlocal behaviours. We prove that this phenomenon exists in every bipartite scenario $(X,Y,A,B)$ with $X,Y\geq2$ and $A,B\geq4$.

</details>


### [215] [Tight bounds for hybrid quantum-classical query algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06803)
*Andris Ambainis,András Gilyén,Martins Kokainis*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究查询模型下的混合量子-经典算法，证明相位估计、振幅估计、搜索、AND-OR树等问题的紧确上下界，并给出通用的下界证明框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合量子-经典算法（量子子程序间测量并丢弃量子比特）此前缺乏系统的下界证明方法，相关研究多为零散、特例化处理，缺少统一框架。

Method: 在查询模型中定义混合算法（每个量子子程序至多做q次oracle查询，子程序间全部测量并丢弃）；通过分析测量记录的概率分布并构造区分性进度度量来证明下界，AND-OR情形则结合经典与量子两种进度度量。

Result: 证明多个问题的紧确界：相位估计Θ(1/(qε²))；振幅估计Θ(p(1-p)/(qε²))；搜索Θ(N/q)；二层AND-OR树Θ(nm/q)，均对q从1到无限制量子复杂度Q(f)成立；并给出区分两分布的混合下界，紧到对数因子。

Conclusion: 建立了混合量子-经典算法的通用下界证明方法，并得到多个基础问题的匹配上下界。

Abstract: We study hybrid quantum-classical algorithms in the query model. The computation consists of quantum subroutines that make at most $q$ oracle queries; between subroutines, all qubits are measured and discarded. We prove matching upper and lower bounds for several problems in this model:
  (i) (Unbiased) Phase estimation up to a precision $ε$ requires $Θ(\frac{1}{q ε^2})$ queries;
  (ii) (Unbiased) Amplitude estimation up to a precision $ε$ requires $Θ(\frac{p(1-p)}{q ε^2})$ queries;
  (iii) Search among $N$ items requires $Θ(\frac{N}{q})$ queries;
  (iv) Two level AND-OR tree (AND of $m$ ORs, with $n$ inputs to each OR) requires $Θ(\frac{nm}{q})$ queries.
  All of these bounds are optimal up to a constant factor, for all $q$ from 1, corresponding to the classical complexity, to $Q(f)$, the unrestricted quantum query complexity of the respective problem. We also derive a hybrid lower bound for distinguishing two distributions specified by a state-preparation unitary; this bound is tight up to a logarithmic factor.
  Besides specific lower bounds, an important contribution is developing methods for proving lower bounds on hybrid quantum algorithms (which have been very ad-hoc up to now). The first two bounds follow from a common framework: we analyze probability distributions over measurement transcripts and bound a progress measure that captures their distinguishability. The AND-OR bound requires a more delicate argument, combining two progress measures that track the information available to the algorithm classically and in quantum form, respectively.

</details>


### [216] [Polynomial-time classical algorithms for mean-field models up to the glass transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06807)
*Alexander Schmidhuber,Alexander Zlokapa*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明经典算法可在多项式时间内估计SYK模型在所有常数温度下的局部可观测量，并将技术推广至经典自旋玻璃问题，同时发展出严格的量子腔方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SYK模型是强相互作用费米子系统，具有高度量子特性：高斯态远离热态，表示热态需大多项式规模量子电路。虽然近期有工作证明经典算法可在准多项式时间内估计足够高温下的局部热期望，但所有常数温度下的多项式时间算法仍是空白；经典自旋玻璃在其相变点前的热期望计算也属未解问题。

Method: 作者发展了一套完全严格的量子腔方法（quantum cavity method），并将其应用于SYK模型和经典自旋玻璃系统，以分析热期望的可计算性。

Result: 经典算法可在多项式时间内估计SYK模型在所有常数温度下的局部可观测量；该技术可直接推广至经典系统，解决了经典自旋玻璃在其相变点前计算热期望的开放问题；并给出了从任意常数温度Gibbs态学习SYK哈密顿量的多项式时间与样本复杂度的量子算法。

Conclusion: 量子腔方法为强相互作用量子系统的经典模拟提供了严格工具，鉴于经典腔方法在优化、采样、推断与学习中的成功，该方法有望在更多问题上获得独立应用。

Abstract: The Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev model is a strongly interacting fermionic system that has been well-studied in condensed matter and high energy physics. It is highly quantum: Gaussian states are far from the thermal state (Hastings and O'Donnell, STOC'22) and representing the thermal state requires large polynomial-size quantum circuits (Anschuetz et al., QIP'25). Very recently, it was nonetheless proven that classical algorithms can estimate local thermal expectations at sufficiently high temperature in quasipolynomial time (Zlokapa, FOCS'26). We show that classical algorithms can in fact estimate local observables at all constant temperatures in polynomial time. Our techniques also extend straightforwardly to classical systems: we resolve an open question about computing thermal expectations of a classical spin glass up to its phase transition (Bencs et al., STOC'26). Our proof develops a fully rigorous quantum cavity method. Due to the success of the classical cavity method in optimization, sampling, inference and learning, we expect the quantum cavity method to find further applications of independent interest. As an example, we give a quantum algorithm that learns SYK Hamiltonians from the Gibbs state at any constant temperature with polynomial time and sample complexity.

</details>


### [217] [Quantum 1-PCA with Pauli Measurements in Nearly Linear Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06808)
*Dutch Hansen,Jerry Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子1-PCA问题：给定未知n量子比特混合态的副本，使用非自适应单量子比特测量恢复其主特征向量的经典描述，并在存在态模型误差情况下达到与纯态情形相近的复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作（Grewal等）在纯态假设（η=0）下实现了接近最优的速率，但实际态往往存在模型误差/混合性。本文旨在去除纯态假设，研究在态存在偏差（η=max(1-λ, ε)>0）时能否保持相同量级的样本与时间复杂性。

Method: 算法核心是递归构造低维子空间，使其近似保留目标特征向量；全部测量非自适应选择，并在单量子比特Pauli基下执行。为实现对Hilbert空间维度近乎线性的运行时间，提出一种结构化Pauli采样方案，可对指数多个投影Pauli矩阵进行快速批量计算。

Result: 对具有最高特征值λ、谱隙至少Δ的n量子比特态，算法以高概率恢复主特征向量至保真度≥1-ε：副本复杂度为 Õ(2^n·η²/(Δ³ε³))，时间复杂度为 Õ((2^n/(Δε))·poly(η/(Δε)))，其中η=max(1-λ, ε)。当谱隙为常数且ε=Ω(η)时，复杂度降为 Õ(2^n/ε)。

Conclusion: 本文证明在存在态模型误差（混合态）情况下，量子1-PCA可达到与纯态情形相同的样本与时间速率（至多相差polylog因子），将Grewal等的保证推广到η>0的一般情形。

Abstract: We consider the problem of quantum 1-PCA: given copies of an unknown $n$-qubit mixed state, recover a classical description of its leading eigenvector. Our goal is to do so using non-adaptive and single-qubit measurements. For an $n$-qubit state with top eigenvalue $λ$ and spectral gap at least $Δ> 0$, we give an algorithm that recovers the leading eigenvector to fidelity at least $1 - \varepsilon$ with high probability using $\tilde{O}\left( {2^n \cdot η^2} / {Δ^3 \varepsilon^3}\right)$ copies and $\tilde{O}((2^n/Δ\varepsilon) \cdot \operatorname{poly}(η/Δ\varepsilon))$ time, where $η= \max (1 - λ, \varepsilon)$. All of our measurements are non-adaptively chosen, and performed in single-qubit Pauli bases. When the spectral gap is constant and the desired accuracy is comparable to the noise level, i.e. $\varepsilon = Ω(η)$, our runtime and copy complexity become $\tilde{O} (2^n / \varepsilon)$. This generalizes the guarantees of Grewal et al. [arXiv:2601.04444], who achieved similar rates, but under the assumption $η= 0$, i.e., that the state was pure. Our results show that the same rates hold in the presence of state misspecification, up to polylogarithmic factors. From a technical perspective, our algorithm works by recursively constructing low-dimensional subspaces that approximately preserve the target eigenvector. To achieve nearly linear runtime dependence on the dimension of the Hilbert space, we develop a novel structured Pauli sampling scheme that enables fast batched computation of exponentially many projected Pauli matrices.

</details>


### [218] [Finding Gaussian Structure in Bosonic States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06810)
*Alvan Arulandu,Sitan Chen,Ziyun Chen,Jerry Li,Eric Ma*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究纯玻色高斯态的不可知层析（agnostic tomography）：给定任意n模玻色态ρ的若干副本，输出一个纯高斯态，使其与ρ的非保真度至多为 opt+ε（opt为任意纯高斯态可达的最小非保真度）。作者在高保真与低保真两种情形下给出高效协议，并证明了相应下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高斯态层析工作多假设目标态本身就是高斯态，而实际中目标态可能任意且仅"接近"高斯。本文希望在无此假设的不可知设定下，仍然高效地学到最优的纯高斯近似，并刻画其复杂度上界与下界，同时为容错的高斯性检验提供首个真正容错的协议。

Method: 协议遵循统一范式：先用一般高斯测量结合经典鲁棒统计技术迭代获得较好的热启动（warm start）估计；再利用非高斯测量，通过凸与非凸优化对热启动进行精化。非高斯测量被证明是达到所获强不可知保证的必要手段。

Result: 当 opt 低于某普遍常数时，协议运行时间与副本复杂度关于 n、1/ε 强多项式，并含 log log E（E为最近纯高斯态能量）；对任意 opt，协议使用 (n+1)^{poly(1/ε)} poly(1+log log E) 的副本与运行时间。作为推论，得到首个真正容错的高斯性检验协议，可区分 opt>c+ε 与 opt<c-ε（任意阈值 c∈(0,1)）。此外证明除非 NP⊆BQP，否则 poly(n,1/ε) 运行时间不可能。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了纯玻色高斯态不可知层析的高效可行性与复杂度下界，证明非高斯测量不可或缺，且其保证严格优于对经典高斯进行鲁棒估计所能达到的极限，并给出首个容错高斯性检验协议。

Abstract: We study agnostic tomography of pure bosonic Gaussian states: given copies of an arbitrary $n$-mode bosonic state $ρ$, the goal is to output a pure Gaussian state whose infidelity with $ρ$ is at most $\mathrm{opt} + ε$, where $\mathrm{opt}$ is the minimum infidelity achievable by any pure Gaussian state.
  We give efficient protocols achieving this in both the high and low fidelity regimes. When $\mathrm{opt}$ is below some universal constant, our protocol has runtime and copy complexity which is strongly polynomial in $n, 1/ε$ and $\log \log E$, where $E$ is the energy of the closest pure Gaussian state. For arbitrary $\mathrm{opt}$, our protocol uses $(n+1)^{\mathrm{poly}(1/ε)} \mathrm{poly}\left(1+\log\log(E)\right)$ copies and runtime. As a corollary, we obtain the first truly tolerant Gaussianity testing protocol for distinguishing whether $\mathrm{opt} > c + ε$ or $\mathrm{opt} < c - ε$, for any threshold $c\in(0,1)$. We also prove $\mathrm{poly}(n,1/ε)$ runtime is impossible, unless $\mathrm{NP}\subseteq\mathrm{BQP}$.
  Our protocols follow a shared paradigm: first, we iteratively use general Gaussian measurements combined with techniques from classical robust statistics to obtain a good warm start estimate, then we leverage non-Gaussian measurements to refine this warm start using convex and non-convex optimization methods. Interestingly, we prove that non-Gaussian measurements are necessary to match the strong agnostic guarantees we obtain, and in fact these guarantees are provably superior to what is possible for robustly estimating classical Gaussians.

</details>


### [219] [Polynomial-time simulation of non-Clifford quantum error correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06811)
*Serban Cercelescu,Mark Koch,Arthur Pesah*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 非Clifford量子纠错电路的所有中间态均可表示为三阶相位多项式态，等价于新引入的DCP稳定子形式下的稳定子态，从而给出精确多项式时间模拟算法与开源模拟器merlin。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非Clifford量子纠错电路的精确模拟通常计算代价昂贵，而现有方法难以刻画其内部中间态结构，限制了模拟规模与对魔法态的理解。

Method: 证明一大类非Clifford纠错电路（含魔法态蒸馏/培育、码切换与规范固定、带伴随提取的横向非Clifford门、对角魔法态注入）在随机Pauli噪声下的每个中间态都是三阶相位多项式态；引入对角Clifford与Pauli（DCP）稳定子形式，证明三阶相位多项式态恰为该形式下的稳定子态；刻画其表示并实现多项式时间的表示更新。

Result: 得到该类电路的精确多项式时间模拟算法及分析其内部态与魔法态的框架；开源实现merlin在蒸馏、培育与码切换电路上优于现有非Clifford模拟器，Bravyi-Haah蒸馏中随逻辑输出数增长的运行时与内存扩展性更优，并可模拟超出其他测试模拟器能力的码切换电路。

Conclusion: 三阶相位多项式态与DCP稳定子形式为一大类非Clifford纠错电路提供了统一且可高效模拟的数学框架，扩展了可精确模拟的量子纠错与魔法态处理范围。

Abstract: We show that every intermediate state of a broad class of non-Clifford quantum error-correction circuits is a third-order phase-polynomial state, even under stochastic Pauli noise. The class includes magic state distillation and cultivation, code switching and gauge fixing, transversal non-Clifford gates with syndrome extraction, and injection of diagonal magic states. Third-order phase-polynomial states strictly generalize stabilizer states, and we prove that they are exactly the stabilizer states of the diagonal-Clifford-and-Pauli (DCP) stabilizer formalism that we introduce. We characterize these states and show that their representations can be updated in polynomial time. This yields an exact polynomial-time simulation algorithm for these circuits and, more broadly, a framework for reasoning about their internal states and about magic states in general. We provide an open-source implementation, \texttt{merlin}, and benchmark it against existing non-Clifford simulators on distillation, cultivation, and code switching circuits. The benchmarks demonstrate improved runtime and memory scaling as the number of logical outputs grows in Bravyi-Haah distillation, and simulation of a code switching circuit beyond the reach of all other tested simulators.

</details>


### [220] [Protecting Quantum Computers against Untrusted Users](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06812)
*Shiv Akshar Yadavalli,Joel Rajakumar,Alexander Schuckert,Michael J. Gullans*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出受限计算模型1/2BQP_1：量子服务器仅在执行后揭示随机计算基输入，并只输出一个比特。作者猜想经典客户端经多项式次自适应查询仍无法高效分解RSA模数，且该模型涵盖DQC1的应用；安全性依赖于分解的经典困难性与有理重构的电路下界猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公开可用的容错量子计算机需在保留科学实用性（如多时间关联、OTOC、配分函数等DQC1应用）的同时限制其密码分析能力，避免被用于分解RSA等破解任务。

Method: 定义受限模型1/2BQP_1：量子服务器提供随机计算基输入（执行后才揭示）与一个指定输出比特，允许任意电路与系统规模。通过两步论证安全性：其一证明单比特读出使Shor、Ekera-Hastad、Kitaev、Regev等标准分解算法的量子阶段可在围绕单次算术预言机调用的广义族内经典模拟；其二分析相干实现有理重构以释放因子比特的绕道，指出随机输入阻碍该攻击。并提出基于量子自旋动力学经典预测检验的候选方案以区分1/2BQP_1与DQC1。

Result: 1/2BQP_1涵盖DQC1及其无限温度多时间关联、OTOC和归一化配分函数等应用；在所述广义算法族内，单比特读出使标准分解算法的量子阶段可经典模拟；相干有理重构攻击受随机输入阻碍，因有理重构长期难以并行化到对数深度经典电路；由此给出1/2BQP_1与DQC1分离的候选方案。

Conclusion: 该协议的安全性不仅建立在分解问题的经典困难性上，还依赖于关于有理重构的电路下界猜想。

Abstract: Publicly accessible fault-tolerant quantum computers must preserve scientific utility while limiting cryptanalytic power. We propose the restricted model of computation, 1/2BQP_1: a quantum server provides random computational-basis inputs, revealed only after execution, and one designated output bit. This interface permits arbitrary circuits and system sizes. We conjecture that a classical client making polynomially many adaptive requests cannot efficiently factor RSA moduli. The model subsumes the well-known one-clean-qubit model DQC1, retaining its applications to infinite-temperature multi-time correlations, out-of-time-order correlators, and suitably normalized partition functions. We propose a candidate for separating 1/2BQP_1 from DQC1 based on testing classical predictions of quantum spin dynamics. We argue security in two steps. First, we show that one-bit readout makes the quantum stages of standard factoring algorithms classically simulable, even when their preparation and postprocessing are redesigned within a broad family around a single arithmetic-oracle call. This covers the quantum stages of Shor, Ekera-Hastad, Kitaev, and Regev factoring constructions. Second, we examine the workaround of coherently implementing rational reconstruction to release a factor bit. Random inputs obstruct this straightforward attack: known techniques accommodate logarithmic-depth classical circuits, but rational reconstruction has resisted such parallelization for decades. Therefore, we show that security of our protocol is built on not only classical hardness for factoring, but also conjectured circuit lowerbounds for rational reconstruction.

</details>


### [221] [CV-QAOA: Efficient Low-Depth Quantum Optimization of Continuous Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06815)
*Sriram Bharadwaj,Di Luo,Leo Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究连续变量量子近似优化算法（CV-QAOA）用于高维连续优化，证明其在严格凸二次函数、旋转双井（RDW）及新提出的旋转方井（RSW）问题上具有严格的量子查询复杂度优势，并指出RDW的可去量子化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展早期CV-QAOA方案（引入变分优化初态），在高深度极限下恢复量子哈密顿下降（QHD）的收敛保证，并系统刻画CV-QAOA相对经典算法在连续优化中的量子优势边界。

Method: 提出带变分优化初态的CV-QAOA，并在高深度极限下与QHD收敛性挂钩；对三类代价函数进行查询复杂度分析：严格凸二次函数、RDW函数、新提出的RSW函数；对RDW给出1步CV-QAOA算法及其去量子化与梯度对齐线搜索分析；对RSW用CV-QAOA模拟绝热演化，并与经典算法查询下界对比；最后在deflected corrugated spring与Easom函数上做数值模拟。

Result: (1) d步CV-QAOA用2d次量子查询最小化任意d维严格凸二次函数；(2) 1步CV-QAOA仅用2次量子查询求解含2^d个局部极小值的RDW，而此前QHD需Õ(d^3)次查询；1步CV-QAOA可被有效去量子化，梯度对齐线搜索以O(d)次查询成功，接近信息论下界Ω(d/log d)；(3) 对RSW，CV-QAOA模拟的绝热演化以d^{o(1)}次查询达到全局最小值，而任何学习隐藏旋转的经典算法需Ω(d^2/log d)次查询，并给出Θ(d^2 log d)次查询的经典算法几乎匹配该下界。

Conclusion: CV-QAOA在特定连续优化族上可实现指数级量子查询加速，但优势依赖于问题结构（如RDW可去量子化）；RSW通过抑制有效局部梯度信息刻画了超越可去量子化范围的量子优势。数值实验表明CV-QAOA在更一般问题上具有良好性能。

Abstract: We study a Continuous-Variable Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (CV-QAOA) for high-dimensional continuous optimization. Our formulation extends an earlier CV-QAOA proposal with a variationally optimized initial state and recovers the convergence guarantees of Quantum Hamiltonian Descent (QHD) in the high-depth limit. We prove rigorous performance guarantees of CV-QAOA on several families of cost functions. First, we show $d$-step CV-QAOA minimizes any $d$-dimensional strictly convex quadratic function with $2d$ quantum queries to the cost function. We then analyze a family of nonconvex "Rotated Double Well" (RDW) functions with $2^d$ local minima introduced by arXiv:2311.00811. While prior work showed QHD reaches its global minimum with $\tilde O(d^3)$ queries, we prove that 1-step CV-QAOA solves RDW with just two quantum queries. Although general-purpose classical solvers need superpolynomial time for RDW and structure-awareness can reduce the cost to polynomial time, we show that the 1-step CV-QAOA protocol can be efficiently dequantized, and that a gradient-aligned line search succeeds with $O(d)$ queries, nearly matching the information-theoretic $Ω(d/\log d)$ query lower bound. To move beyond the dequantizable regime, we introduce a ``Rotated Square Well'' (RSW) problem, whose globally flat landscape suppresses useful local gradient information. For this family, we show that an adiabatic evolution simulated by CV-QAOA can reach the global minimum using $d^{o(1)}$ queries. On the other hand, any classical algorithm that learn the hidden rotation in RSW provably requires $Ω(d^2/\log d)$ queries, a bound we nearly match with an explicit $Θ(d^2\log d)$-query classical algorithm.Numerical simulations on deflected corrugated spring and Easom functions illustrate the promising performance of CV-QAOA on more general problems.

</details>


### [222] [The complete phase diagram of detailed-balanced Gibbs samplers for the mean-field Heisenberg model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06818)
*Levente Raikovich,József Mák,Csaba Czabán,András Gilyén*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究了平均场Heisenberg模型中KMS-细致平衡Gibbs采样器Lindbladians的谱隙，证明其谱隙与临界温度关系符合相图：无序相为常数、临界点为1/√n、有序相为1/n。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前Basso等人(2026)仅对GNS-细致平衡Davies生成元刻画了该相图，且在临界温度处的谱隙标度仅得到部分解决，本文希望补全这一缺口并推广到更广泛的Lindbladian族。

Method: 针对n个自旋的平均场Heisenberg模型，分析新发现的KMS-细致平衡Gibbs采样器Lindbladians族，通过谱隙分析推导其温度依赖标度。

Result: 这些Lindbladians族的谱隙呈现相同相图：临界温度以上（无序相）谱隙为常数，临界温度处标度为1/√n，临界温度以下（有序相）标度为1/n。

Conclusion: 结果推广并完善了Basso等人的工作，表明KMS-细致平衡Gibbs采样器Lindbladians与GNS-细致平衡Davies生成元共享同一谱隙相图，并完整解决了临界温度处的谱隙标度问题。

Abstract: We study the spectral gap of recently discovered families of KMS-detailed-balanced Gibbs-sampler Lindbladians for the mean-field Heisenberg model on $n$ spins. We show that the spectral gaps of these natural families of Lindbladians admit the same phase diagram: the gap is constant above the critical temperature (i.e., in the disordered phase), scales as $1/\sqrt{n}$ at the critical temperature, and as $1/n$ below the critical temperature (i.e., in the ordered phase). Our result builds on and extends the recent work of Basso et al.~(2026) which characterized this phase diagram for the GNS-detailed-balanced Davies generator, but only partially resolved the gap scaling at the critical temperature.

</details>


### [223] [Improved bounds on stabilizer extent and Clifford rank](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06819)
*Pulkit Sinha,Benjamin Lovitz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 证明稳定子秩≤k的纯态其稳定子外延≤2^{O(√(k log(k+1)))}，并给出Clifford秩-k算子的平方Clifford系数范数类似界，解决相关定量猜想并导出多项下界与断层扫描算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳定子秩、Clifford秩及相应范数、外延之间的定量关系此前不清楚，尤其(Mehraban-Tamasbi, STOC 2025)提出的定量猜想未解；同时张量幂的近似稳定子秩、AND函数的二次相位表示、伪随机态排除及稳定子秩态断层扫描的复杂度均受限于缺乏此类范数—秩不等式。

Method: 建立稳定子外延与稳定子秩之间的上界：对任意稳定子秩≤k的纯态，证明其稳定子外延≤2^{O(√(k log(k+1)))}；并将该结果推广为Clifford秩-k算子的平方Clifford系数范数的类似上界。由此推出稳定子保真度下界2^{-O(√(k log(k+1)))}。

Result: 解决了Mehraban-Tamasbi (STOC 2025)的定量猜想；证明任意非稳定子量子比特态张量幂的近似稳定子秩下界Ω(n^2/log n)，将T-态张量幂的最佳已知下界推广到任意非稳定子态（含magic态）；由Clifford秩—范数不等式得到n位AND函数用二次相位精确表示的Ω(n^2/log n)下界（改进此前的线性界）；排除近似稳定子秩与Clifford秩为O((log n)^2/log log n)的伪随机态与酉系综，比Kalra-Sinha (Quantum 2026)改进log n；给出稳定子秩≤k态的断层扫描算法，时间与副本复杂度为poly(n)2^{O(√(k log(k+1)))}，指数近乎开方改进。

Conclusion: 稳定子外延（及Clifford系数范数）与秩之间仅需次指数（√(k log k)型）代价即可相互控制，这一范数—秩不等式是统一工具：既解决保真度定量猜想，又推广张量幂近似稳定子秩下界到任意非稳定子态，改进AND函数二次相位表示下界与伪随机态排除阈值，并给出近乎开方改进的断层扫描算法。

Abstract: We prove that every pure state of stabilizer rank at most $k$ has stabilizer extent at most $2^{O(\sqrt{k\log(k+1)})}$, and establish the analogous bound for the squared Clifford coefficient norm of Clifford-rank-$k$ operators. This implies stabilizer fidelity at least $2^{-O(\sqrt{k\log(k+1)})}$, resolving the quantitative conjecture of (Mehraban-Tamasbi, STOC, 2025), and proves an $Ω(n^2/\log n)$ lower bound for the approximate stabilizer rank of tensor powers of any non-stabilizer qubit state. The latter result generalizes the best-known lower bound for tensor powers of $T$-states (Mehraban-Tamasbi, STOC, 2024) to arbitrary non-stabilizer qubit states, including magic states.
  As a consequence of the Clifford rank--norm inequality, we obtain an $Ω(n^2/\log n)$ lower bound for exact representations of $n$-bit AND function by quadratic phases, improving the previous best-known linear bound. Further consequences rule out pseudorandom state and unitary ensembles with approximate stabilizer and Clifford rank $O((\log n)^2/\log\log n)$, respectively, a $\log n$ improvement over prior work (Kalra-Sinha, Quantum, 2026). We also obtain tomography algorithms for states of stabilizer rank at most $k$, with $poly(n)2^{O(\sqrt{k\log(k+1)})}$ time and copy complexity, a nearly square-root improvement in the exponent over the best-known algorithm.

</details>


### [224] [Designing Group-Valued Codes with Full Regular Low-Weight Bases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06820)
*Jong Yeon Lee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出群值量子纠错码的设计原则，构造具有低权完全正规正则逻辑基的码，引入"框架宽度"f 作为新码参数，给出高码率 LP 与 PP 码实例，并提出高效距离认证方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高码率、高距离码的已报道正则基可能比码距重数倍，且最小权逻辑算子可能因根本性障碍无法构成完全正则基，因此需要新的设计原则以获得低权逻辑算子，从而降低容错量子计算的资源开销。

Method: 提出群值量子纠错码的设计原则：通过平移一组种子算子生成低权完全正规正则逻辑基；引入框架宽度 f 作为与距离 d、校验权 w 并列的码参数。识别结构性障碍（奇阶群上全填充二元单项式 CSS 校验的全正则性强制距离为二；2的幂阶群上，将每个群元素替换为1后两个校验矩阵均满行秩可保证全正则性）。用显式高码率 lifted-product (LP) 与 pair-partition (PP) 码验证原则，并提出结合对称性约简与稳定子剪枝的穷举距离认证方法。

Result: 给出 [[1088,128,22]] LP 码（f=22）与 [[1024,256,24]] PP 码（f≤27），均在 w=10；在 w=11 时 PP 构造可达 d=25、f≤27，或 d=24 且已证明最优 f=25，体现了参数间权衡以及距离与框架宽度的严格分离；并用所提方法高效确定了一千多量子比特 LP 实例的精确距离。

Conclusion: 框架宽度 f 作为附加码参数，与距离 d、校验权 w 及显式逻辑基一起，可为资源估计与容错逻辑操作的编译提供依据；所提设计原则与距离认证方法可用于构造和验证高码率、低权逻辑算子的量子纠错码。

Abstract: Efficient fault-tolerant quantum computing architectures benefit from low-weight logical operators. However, reported canonical bases for high-rate, high-distance codes can be several times heavier than the code distance, and fundamental obstructions can prevent minimum-weight logical operators from forming a complete canonical basis. To address this issue, we develop design principles for group-valued quantum error-correcting codes with low-weight full regular canonical logical bases, generated by translating a set of seed operators. We introduce frame width $f$, the smallest weight cutoff that permits a complete canonical logical basis, as an additional code parameter alongside distance $d$ and check weight $w$. Cataloguing these parameters together with explicit logical bases can inform resource estimates and the compilation of fault-tolerant logical operations.
  First, we identify a structural obstruction: for fully populated binary monomial CSS checks over a group of odd order, full regularity forces distance two, while for groups of power-of-two order, full row rank of both check matrices after replacing every group element by $1$ guarantees full regularity. Second, we demonstrate our principles through explicit high-rate lifted-product (LP) and pair-partition (PP) codes: an $[[1088,128,22]]$ LP code with $f=22$ and an $[[1024,256,24]]$ PP code with $f \leq 27$, both at $w=10$. At $w=11$, our PP constructions attain either $d=25$ with $f \leq 27$ or $d=24$ with the proved optimum $f=25$, demonstrating both a tradeoff among these parameters and a strict separation between distance and frame width. Finally, we propose a method combining symmetry reduction and stabilizer-based pruning for exhaustive distance certification, and use it to efficiently establish exact distances for LP examples with more than a thousand qubits.

</details>


### [225] [Natural proofs for quantum state preparation lower bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06826)
*Christine Li,Natalie Parham*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 建立量子态制备下界的自然证明屏障，类比Razborov-Rudich结果，在标准密码假设下证明任何"自然"性质都无法对固定层级的Magic Hierarchy给出超多项式态制备下界，并指出多项现有下界技术均属自然性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释为何难以证明更强的量子态制备下界，并将经典复杂性理论中的自然证明屏障推广到量子态制备下界领域。

Method: 定义量子态的"自然"性质：对足够大比例的Haar随机态成立，且在给定态全部振幅时可高效测试；在标准密码假设下分析此类性质的能力上限。

Result: 证明不存在自然性质能对固定层级Magic Hierarchy给出超多项式态制备下界；并表明基于近似次数、非局域哈密顿量唯一基态、互信息等现有下界技术均属自然性质。

Conclusion: 量子态制备下界存在自然证明屏障，多个已知技术受此屏障限制，说明现有方法难以突破超多项式下界。

Abstract: We identify a barrier that helps explain why proving stronger quantum state-preparation lower bounds has been so difficult. In particular, we establish a quantum analogue of the Razborov-Rudich natural proofs barrier for state-preparation lower bounds. We call a property of quantum states \emph{natural} if it holds for a sufficiently large fraction of Haar-random states and can be efficiently tested when given all of the state's amplitudes. Under a standard cryptographic assumption, we show that no natural property can prove superpolynomial state-preparation lower bounds even against a fixed level of the Magic Hierarchy. We show that several existing state-preparation lower-bound techniques are natural in our sense, including arguments based on approximate degree, not being a unique ground state of a local Hamiltonian, and mutual information.

</details>


### [226] [The practical cost of magic state cultivation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06828)
*Rohan Mehta,Varun Menon,Hengyun Zhou,Mikhail D. Lukin,J. Pablo Bonilla Ataides*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出 Caliper——一种仅利用非破坏性中途测量综合征信息的开边界后选择方法，用于魔态培养的资源基准测试，揭示了实际中途后选择对培养协议性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有魔态培养（magic state cultivation）的资源估计假设可以在最终逃逸阶段对输出魔态进行无噪声横向测量以支持后选择，但此类测量会破坏魔态，在实际计算中不可用。因此需要研究在仅依赖中途综合征信息约束下的后选择方法及其对资源与性能的影响。

Method: 提出 Caliper 开边界后选择方法，仅使用非破坏性的中途综合征信息进行后选择，并与在该约束下运行的现有方法进行对比基准测试。

Result: 在某些区间，Caliper 唯一地恢复了先前估计的资源效率；在其他情况下则观察到权衡：在先前工作报告的时空成本下，可达逻辑错误率高出数个数量级；要恢复所报告的逻辑错误率，需要显著更大的码和更长的逃逸阶段。

Conclusion: 涉及中途后选择的实际因素会显著影响魔态培养协议的设计与性能，凸显了对实际可行后选择方案进行资源评估的必要性。

Abstract: Magic state cultivation is a promising method for preparing high-fidelity non-Clifford resource states - a central component of fault-tolerant quantum computing - with low spacetime overhead. In benchmarking cultivation, existing resource estimates assume access to noiseless transversal measurement of the output magic state to allow for postselection during the final escape stage of the cultivation process. However, such measurements would destroy the magic state and are therefore unavailable during computation. We develop an open-boundary postselection method, Caliper, that uses only nondestructive mid-circuit syndrome information and substantially outperforms existing methods operating under these constraints. In some regimes, our postselection method uniquely recovers the resource efficiency of prior estimates. However, in other cases, we observe a tradeoff. At spacetime costs reported in previous works, the achievable logical error rates can be orders of magnitude higher, and recovering the reported logical error rate requires substantially larger codes and longer escape stages. Our results highlight how practical considerations involving mid-circuit postselection can impact the design and performance of magic state cultivation protocols.

</details>


### [227] [Energy-constrained two-way capacities of the pure-loss bosonic channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06832)
*Stefano Pirandola*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文在无条件下、平均发送光子数约束下，确定了纯损耗玻色信道的双向量子、纠缠分发、私密和密钥容量，四者相等且为 g(N)-g((1-η)N)，解决了长期存在的有限能量容量问题，并给出强逆阈值与指数强逆结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯损耗玻色信道的容量此前多在无约束（无限能量）条件下研究，而实际通信总受平均光子数约束。2009年提出的反向相干信息速率是否为有限能量下的最优速率长期未决，因此需要建立统一的有限能量容量框架。

Method: 核心工具是"扇区隐形传态模拟"(sector teleportation simulation)，用以将输入能量约束转移到共享纠缠资源上，从而在针对通用自适应协议的逆命题论证中保持该约束。同时分析并行协议在硬性总光子数截止下的行为。

Result: 对于透射率η和平均光子数N，四种容量一致，均为 g(N)-g((1-η)N)，其中g为热态熵；强逆阈值对所有N>0均为无约束值 -log_2(1-η)；对带硬性总光子数截止的并行协议，同一有限能量容量公式满足指数强逆。

Conclusion: 研究确立了自适应玻色通信的统一有限能量框架，并揭示能量约束如何从根本上改变可达速率与逆命题界。

Abstract: We determine the two-way quantum, entanglement-distribution, private, and secret-key capacities of the pure-loss bosonic channel under an unconditional mean transmitted-photon-number constraint. For transmissivity $η$ and mean photon number $N$, all four capacities coincide and are given by $g(N)-g((1-η)N)$, where $g$ is the entropy of a thermal mode. This resolves the longstanding finite-energy capacity problem by establishing the optimality of the reverse-coherent-information rate introduced in 2009. Our central tool is sector teleportation simulation, which we develop specifically to transfer the input-energy constraint to the shared entanglement resource, thereby preserving the constraint throughout converse arguments for general adaptive protocols. For every $N>0$, we further show that the corresponding strong-converse thresholds coincide with the unconstrained value $-\log_2(1-η)$. For parallel protocols with a hard total-photon-number cutoff, the same finite-energy capacity formula instead satisfies an exponential strong converse. Together, these results establish a unified finite-energy framework for adaptive bosonic communication and reveal how energy constraints fundamentally reshape both attainable rates and converse bounds.

</details>


### [228] [Distributional Quantum Query Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06835)
*Shalev Ben-David,M. H. Ebtehaj*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文为分布量子查询复杂度下的组合、直接和与直接积问题给出了下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有量子查询复杂度的联合计算性质（组合定理、直接和定理、直接积定理）均只适用于最坏情况，无法处理输入服从固定分布（如乘积分布 μ^k）的情形；Yao 型极小极大定理虽保证存在困难分布，但不保证其为乘积分布。

Method: 证明组合、直接和、直接积问题的分布联合计算下界；引入（a）γ_2 范数的“乘性”变体，用于代替乘性对手法证明直接积定理；（b）一种新的“无 Shaltiel”量子查询复杂度度量，用以刻画分布量子查询复杂度的组合行为。

Result: 建立了组合、直接和与直接积问题的分布联合计算下界，并证明新的无 Shaltiel 度量满足良好性质且刻画分布量子查询复杂度的组合行为。

Conclusion: 将量子查询复杂度的联合计算性质从最坏情况推广到分布情形，并提供了新的分析工具。

Abstract: Quantum query complexity enjoys a variety of pleasing joint computation properties: for example, a composition theorem asserting $Q(f\circ g)=Θ(Q(f)Q(g))$ for all Boolean functions $f$ and $g$; a direct sum theorem asserting that computing $k$ copies of a function (or search problem) costs $Ω(k)$ times as much as the cost of computing one copy; and a direct product theorem asserting that for Boolean functions, even succeeding at the direct sum problem with exponentially small probability still requires $Ω(k)$ times the cost of computing one copy to bounded error.
  However, all of these results are strictly for worst-case quantum query complexity. For example, if we have a fixed distribution $μ$ over inputs, the direct sum theorem says nothing about the quantum query complexity of computing $k$ copies of $f$ when the input comes from the product distribution $μ^k$ instead of being worst-case. (Note that while a standard Yao-type minimax theorem guarantees a hard distribution for the direct sum problem, there's no guarantee that this hard distribution is a product distribution.) A similar problem occurs for the direct product theorem and the composition theorem: none of these results respect distributions.
  In this work, we give distributional joint computation lower bounds for the composition, direct sum, and direct product problems. Along the way, we introduce some new tools for handling quantum query lower bounds, including (a) a new ``multiplicative'' variant of the $γ_2$ norm (which we use in place of the multiplicative adversary method for proving the direct product theorem), and (b) a new ``Shaltiel-free'' measure of quantum query complexity, which we show characterizes the composition behavior of distributional quantum query complexity and satisfies pleasing properties.

</details>


### [229] [Semi-Cliffordness of the Clifford hierarchy for a single qudit in composite dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06836)
*Yifei Qi,Rahul Sarkar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明单个qudit上的Clifford层级门为semi-Clifford当且仅当维度d是无平方因子数，并在复合维度下引入四类门的区分，同时证明第三层门在任意维度下均为广义semi-Clifford。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信息论中一个重要问题是判定Clifford层级中的每个门是否为semi-Clifford，因为这类门可通过门隐形传态实现资源高效实现。已有结果仅限于素数维度，需要推广到一般维度以刻画层级门的结构。

Method: 将素数维度的结果推广到任意维度：在复合维度下，ℤ_d²是辛模而非向量空间，据此区分四类门——semi-Clifford (SC)、置换对角类 (N)、Lagrangian semi-Clifford (LSC) 和广义 semi-Clifford (GSC)；分析平方因子条件下这些类之间的等价关系。

Result: 证明单个qudit维度d上的每个层级门为semi-Clifford当且仅当d为无平方因子数；在无平方因子维度下 SC=LSC 且 N=GSC，而当d含平方因子时这些等价可能失效（Lagrangian子模可能非自由）；并证明任意维度下单qudit Clifford层级的第三层门都是广义semi-Clifford。

Conclusion: 该工作将semi-Clifford性判定从素数维度完整推广到一般维度，揭示了无平方因子性作为关键代数条件，并刻画了复合维度下门类型的精细分类结构。

Abstract: An important question in quantum information theory asks whether every gate in the Clifford hierarchy is semi-Clifford or not, as such gates admit resource-efficient implementations via gate teleportation. Extending recent results for prime dimensions~\cite{silva_clifford_2025}, we prove that every hierarchy gate on a single qudit of dimension \(d\) is semi-Clifford, if and only if \(d\) is square-free. In composite dimensions, \(\mathbb{Z}_d^2\) is a symplectic module rather than a vector space, motivating a distinction between four types of gates. Semi-Clifford gates ($\mathcal{SC}$) are the ones that become diagonal under left and right multiplication by Clifford gates, while a broader class $\mathcal{N}$ admits a permutation diagonal form, under left and right multiplication by Cliffords. Lagrangian semi-Clifford gates ($\mathcal{LSC}$) conjugate some maximal abelian Pauli subgroup to another, whereas generalized semi-Clifford gates ($\mathcal{GSC}$) map some maximal abelian Pauli subalgebra to another under conjugation. In square-free dimensions, we have \(\mathcal{SC}=\mathcal{LSC}\) and \(\mathcal{N}=\mathcal{GSC}\), but when \(d\) is not square-free, these equivalences can fail, as Lagrangian submodules can become non-free. We also show that every third-level gate of the one-qudit Clifford hierarchy is a generalized semi-Clifford in arbitrary dimensions.

</details>


### [230] [A Spectrum-Based Converse for Quantum State Discrimination and Its Applications to Classical-Quantum Channel Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06840)
*Tam{á}s Havas,Hsuan-Yin Lin,Eirik Rosnes*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究有限码长经典-量子信道编码中平均译码错误概率的逆界，提出基于多重量子假设检验的谱型逆界，并在量子去极化信道与振幅阻尼信道上验证其紧致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限码长经典-量子信道编码的逆界（converse bound）是刻画可达速率与错误概率权衡的关键工具。已有逆界往往依赖较复杂的量，亟需仅由先验概率与量子态谱决定的、更易计算且在低噪声下更紧的逆界。

Method: 首先给出多重量子假设检验错误概率基于成对迹距离（pairwise trace distance）的下界，并导出相应的保真度（fidelity）界。在此基础上提出仅依赖先验概率与各量子态谱的谱型逆界，并针对乘积态输出码给出由输出态特征值乘积构成的显式形式。

Result: 谱型逆界在适当条件下对量子去极化信道保持紧致；对量子振幅阻尼信道上使用|+⟩与|-⟩输入态的二元码给出显式逆界，并在大码长区域讨论其正态近似。所有数值算例中，该谱型逆界在低噪声水平下均比其它逆界更紧。

Conclusion: 谱型逆界为有限码长经典-量子信道编码提供了一种计算可行且紧致的性能界限，尤其在低噪声场景优于已有逆界，可用于指导实际信道编码设计。

Abstract: We investigate converse bounds on the average decoding error probability in finite-blocklength classical-quantum channel coding. We first present a lower bound for multiple quantum hypothesis testing in terms of pairwise trace distances and derive a corresponding fidelity bound. We then obtain a spectrum-based converse that depends only on the a priori probabilities and spectra of the states. We show that the converse bound remains tight for the quantum depolarizing channel under suitable conditions. For codes with product-state outputs, this converse takes an explicit form involving products of output-state eigenvalues. We apply it to binary codes over the quantum amplitude damping channel using the input states $\vert+\rangle$ and $\vert-\rangle$. For this setting, we also discuss a normal approximation to the spectrum-based converse in the large blocklength regime. In all numerical examples considered, the spectrum-based converse is tighter than the other converse bounds at low noise levels.

</details>


### [231] [Quantum information processing under maximal distance measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06842)
*Bartosz Regula,Ludovico Lami,Mario Berta,Marco Tomamichel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 将经典距离度量推广为"最大扩展"后，信道编码与资源蒸馏的任务性能不受影响，但信道模拟与资源稀释的速率由基于Belavkin-Staszewski相对熵的函数给出，且原有的可逆性结论（如反向Shannon定理）在该度量下失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信息中常用的迹距离、基于保真度的纯化距离，是经典距离度量"最小"的量子扩展；但扩展方式并不唯一，存在一整套扩展谱。作者希望考察：若改用经典距离的"最大扩展"（对操作性任务施加更严格的误差要求），量子信息处理任务会受到怎样的影响。

Method: 以经典距离度量的最大扩展作为误差度量，对若干典型量子信息任务（信道编码、量子资源蒸馏、信道模拟、量子资源稀释）的速率与性能进行渐近分析，并与标准度量的情形对比，进而刻画新度量下速率的熵刻画。

Result: 部分任务（信道编码、量子资源蒸馏）的性能完全不受度量改变的影响；而信道模拟与量子资源稀释的速率不再由标准Umegaki量子相对熵的函数给出，而是由基于Belavkin-Staszewski相对熵的函数刻画。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了Belavkin-Staszewski相对熵与操作性任务之间的联系，并表明量子信息中若干已知的可逆性结论——包括反向Shannon定理以及量子资源变换的渐近可逆性——在最大距离度量下不再成立。

Abstract: The measures of distance frequently used in quantum information, such as trace distance or fidelity-based purified distance, are the smallest extensions of the corresponding distance measures in classical information theory. However, this way of extending classical distances is not unique, and a whole spectrum of other extensions can be defined. Here we study the question of how quantum information processing tasks are affected by a change of the error metrics to the maximal extensions of the classical distance measures, imposing more demanding error requirements on operational tasks. We find that the performance of some tasks, such as channel coding or quantum resource distillation, is not affected whatsoever by this change. However, for other tasks such as channel simulation and quantum resource dilution, we show that the rates are now given not by functions of the standard (Umegaki) quantum relative entropy, but ones based on the Belavkin-Staszewski relative entropy. Our results connect the Belavkin-Staszewski relative entropy with operational tasks and reveal that known reversibility results in quantum information, including the reverse Shannon theorem and the asymptotic reversibility of quantum resource transformations, no longer hold under maximal distance measures.

</details>


### [232] [Rewire and reset are all you need: Minimal resources for manipulating non-Abelions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06845)
*Chiu Fan Bowen Lo,Rohith Sajith,Anasuya Lyons,Ashvin Vishwanath,Ruben Verresen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文确定了在一般有限群的量子双重模型中实现任意子编织与融合所需的最小电路资源：编织可通过无辅助比特的顺序幺正电路完成，而输入无关的融合虽不存在纯幺正协议，但仅需一个量子比特重置即可实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编织与融合任意子是拓扑量子计算的基础，但现有电路方案通常依赖辅助比特（ancilla）和测量。作者希望确定在一般有限群量子双重模型中执行这些任意子操作所需的最小电路资源，并厘清辅助资源是否本质必需。

Method: 提出通过顺序幺正电路、无需辅助比特地编织任意子，直观上等价于对任意子所在底层图进行形变。更一般地，引入"图重连"（graph rewiring）这一图示演算，为每个双量子比特门赋予几何解释，并追踪其对稳定子的作用。针对融合，证明了一个一般性不可行定理，排除在不引入辅助比特情况下对未知任意子类型进行纯幺正融合的可能；随后用量子比特重置实现此类"输入无关"融合。

Result: 编织可由无辅助比特的顺序幺正电路实现；纯幺正协议无法完成输入无关的任意子融合（no-go定理）；仅使用一个qudit重置即可实现输入无关融合。结论：与既有观念相反，非阿贝尔任意子的编织与融合均不需要辅助比特资源。

Conclusion: 重新界定了任意子操作的资源需求，表明重置是被低估的纠错原语；所提融合协议可避免辅助比特与测量带来的时空开销，对纠错实验具有实际指导意义。

Abstract: Braiding and fusing anyons are fundamental to topological quantum computation, yet existing circuit protocols often employ ancillas and measurements. We determine the minimal circuit resources for performing such anyon operations in quantum double models of generic finite groups. Our approach braids anyons through a sequential unitary circuit without ancillas. Intuitively, this is obtained by deforming the underlying graph on which the anyons live. More generally, we introduce graph rewiring, a diagrammatic calculus that assigns a geometric interpretation to individual two-qudit gates and tracks their action on stabilizers. For fusion, we prove a general no-go theorem excluding purely unitary protocols for fusing unknown anyon types without introducing ancillas. Remarkably, we show such "input-agnostic" fusion is enabled by simply using a qudit reset. Hence, contrary to folklore, ancilla resources are not needed to braid or fuse non-Abelian anyons (non-Abelions). This work has implications in experimental efforts in error correction: our fusion protocol highlights reset as an underutilized primitive for error correction without the spacetime-overhead of ancillas and measurements.

</details>


### [233] [Boundary-accessible work in a collision-charged topological quantum battery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06849)
*Osman Orçun Keskin,Özgür E. Müstecaplıoğlu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究SSH型二聚化自旋链量子电池通过单一边界端口对外做功的能力。利用自由费米子精确映射，给出了所有粒子数守恒二次提取协议下载荷功的上界；仅当电池的某个单粒子模式占据数超过半数时载荷才能获得可提取功。共振充电可将拓扑边缘模式填充至阈值以上，单次边界脉冲即可达到界值的98.6%，而平庸相下载荷保持被动。拓扑提供局域访问通道，谱匹配与对称性决定能量能否转化为可用的局域功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子电池的ergotropy定义了任意全局幺正操作可提取的功，但实际器件通常只通过单个局域端口交换能量。单端口能提取多少存储功、拓扑边缘模式是否有助益尚不明确，这是本文的核心动机。

Method: 构建SSH型二聚化自旋链量子电池，用激发的二能级辅助系统经重复碰撞在一端充电，最初为空的二能级载荷在同一位置放电。通过精确自由费米子映射，推导所有粒子数守恒二次提取协议下的载荷功上界，并分析拓扑相与平庸相、键无序和格点无序的影响。

Result: 载荷只有在电池某单粒子模式占据数超过1/2时才能获得可提取功；共振充电把拓扑边缘模式填充到阈值以上，单次边界脉冲可交付界值允许的最大载荷功的98.6%。相同六次碰撞控制下平庸相载荷保持被动。在最大化交付功的协议中，循环功率在有限碰撞次数处达到峰值，而交付功在扫描范围内持续增加。跃迁无序（保持手性对称）下优势仍然存在，格点无序破坏手性对称并导致一定比例载荷保持被动。

Conclusion: 拓扑为单端口能量提取提供局域访问通道，而能否把存储能量转化为可用的局域功取决于谱匹配与对称性；SSH边缘模式可作为高效的局域放电通道。

Abstract: Quantum batteries store energy in nonequilibrium quantum states. The ergotropy sets the work extractable by an arbitrary global unitary, but a device usually exchanges energy through a single local port. It remains unclear how much stored work such a port can deliver, and whether topological edge modes help. We study a dimerized spin chain of Su-Schrieffer-Heeger (SSH) type. Excited two-level ancillas charge it at one end by repeated collisions, and an initially empty two-level load discharges it at the same site.
