An exact free-fermion mapping yields a bound on the load work for all number-conserving quadratic extraction protocols. The load gains extractable work only if a single-particle mode of the battery holds more than half an excitation. Resonant charging fills the topological edge mode above this threshold. A single boundary pulse then delivers 98.6% of the maximal load work allowed by the bound for representative parameters. Under the same six-collision controls, the trivial-phase load remains passive. Within the protocol that selects the load pulse for maximum delivered work, cycle power peaks at a finite collision number while delivered work continues to increase over the scanned range. Over the tested range, the advantage survives hopping disorder, which preserves chiral symmetry. On-site disorder breaks this symmetry and leaves a finite fraction of loads passive. Topology thus supplies a localized access channel, while spectral matching and symmetry decide whether stored energy becomes usable local work.

</details>


<div id='physics.class-ph'></div>

# physics.class-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [234] [The dual-array RopeComb: a guide-balanced transmission with synthesisable rising mechanical advantage for impedance-matched launch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04320)
*Rami N. Mahdi*

Main category: physics.class-ph

TL;DR: 提出RopeComb机构：用离散等半径滑轮与绳索折返合成分段变化的机械增益曲线（G=0→100+），实现无离合器、无阻尼器的平稳启动与近匀速输出力，数值验证其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将慢速大驱动力高效传递给轻质负载并保持近恒定输出力，需要机械增益随行程呈陡峭凸型上升的传动。传统连续轮廓机构（变半径卷筒、凸轮）在大尺度下受直径跨度、惯量与轮缘爆裂限制，难以工程实现。

Method: RopeComb以若干等半径滑轮将张力构件多次折返，形成交错跨段，合成离散分段增益曲线；每段折返在首次接触时比值归零，实现10 m/s下的无冲击啮合。因阵列比有闭式解，可用有界最小二乘拟合匀速力目标。为承受大滑架反力并降低倾覆力矩，采用两个独立阵列夹持中央导轨，驱动等比分段级（n1+n2=k），按落绳数比 n2:n1 配置各阵列比值与构件数。

Result: 在100:1至10,000:1质量比范围内，用8–13根张力构件即可将输出力拟合至匀速目标误差0.6%–1.5%。在k=7、1000:1的仿真发射中，双阵列布置抵消单阵列97.7%的反力与95%的倾覆力矩，并把柔性峰均载荷力从1.40降至1.13。所有结果均为数值结果。

Conclusion: RopeComb以离散等半径滑轮折返替代连续变半径轮廓，绕开直径跨度、惯量与轮缘强度瓶颈，实现高增益比、无冲击啮合与近匀速力输出；双阵列构型可显著抑制反力与倾覆力矩。当前证据限于数值仿真，尚需实验验证。

Abstract: Efficiently transferring kinetic energy from a slow, high-force driver to a light payload, with a near-uniform output force, requires a transmission whose mechanical advantage rises along a steeply convex profile. Continuous-contour mechanisms (variable-radius drums, cams) become prohibitive at scale through diameter span, inertia and rim burst. The RopeComb bypasses these limits by synthesising profiles ($G = 0 \to 100+$) from discrete constant-radius sheaves deflecting tension members into staggered spans. Each fold's ratio vanishes at first contact, enabling snatch-free engagement at 10 m/s without clutches or dampers. Because the array ratio is closed-form, bounded least squares matches uniform-force targets to within 0.6-1.5% across mass ratios of 100:1 to 10,000:1 using 8-13 members. To carry large carriage reactions without the overturning moment a single array imposes, two independent arrays flank a central guide and drive a partitioned fixed-ratio stage ($n_1 + n_2 = k$). Sizing array ratios and member counts in the fall-count ratio $n_2 : n_1$ cancels 97.7% of the single-array reaction and 95% of its overturning moment in simulated 1,000:1 launches at $k=7$, reducing compliant peak-to-mean payload force from 1.40 to 1.13. All results are numerical.

</details>


### [235] [Short-term solar eclipse predictions using Euclidean geometry and dynamical systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03737)
*Alin-Doru Aluas,Valerian-Alin Fodor,Virgilius-Aurelian Minuta*

Main category: physics.class-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种仅用经典几何工具的低计算成本算法，判定不透明球体是否遮挡发光球体，并以日-地-月三体问题预测日食类型、起始时刻与持续时间，一年期预报误差为分钟量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日食预测通常依赖复杂的数值积分或高精度天体历表，计算代价高。本文旨在以简洁的几何方法在保证可用精度的前提下降低计算成本。

Method: 基于经典几何工具构造遮挡判定算法，结合三体问题（太阳-地球-月球）模型求解天体位置，据此判断是否发生日食并给出日食类型、开始时刻与持续时间。

Result: 在一年时间尺度的预测中，所得结果的误差为分钟量级。

Conclusion: 该算法计算量小且精度可接受，表明无需高成本数值方法即可实现分钟级精度的日食预测，适用于教学与快速估算场景。

Abstract: We propose a computationally inexpensive algorithm to determine whether an opaque spherical body eclipses a luminous one, using only classical geometrical tools. We apply this method to the Sun-Earth-Moon system and predict solar eclipses, including their type, beginning, and duration, using the three-body problem. For predictions over a one-year period, the resulting errors are in the order of minutes.

</details>


### [236] [Restoring Invariance to the Mechanical Wave Equation across Inertial Frames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03776)
*Ashmeet Singh,Heather Romero Michel*

Main category: physics.class-ph

TL;DR: 本文从第一性原理重新审视机械波方程在任意惯性系中的形式，指出其看似不满足相对性原理的根源在于欧拉坐标表述；通过拉格朗日导数形式和左右行波分解的欧拉形式两种重构，说明波的协变性，并讨论伽利略变换作为洛伦兹变换低速极限的局限及多普勒效应中波长变化的观测联系，兼具概念澄清与教学价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械波方程由牛顿定律导出，在介质静止系中形式最简，但换到其他惯性系后似乎完全失去该形式，这引出能否为任意惯性观测者一致地写出波方程的问题。该问题在本科教学中常被忽略，通常以介质静止系为"优先参考系"搪塞过去，却未阐明这对物理定律背后的相对性原理意味着什么，因此需要重新审视。

Method: 从第一性原理出发重新推导波方程，指出表观不协变性源于欧拉坐标的表述方式；提出两种重构：一是基于微观粒子受力定律、用拉格朗日导数写出的显式协变形式；二是分离左右行波解的因子化欧拉形式。同时探讨伽利略变换作为洛伦兹变换低速极限的局限。

Result: 证明波方程的表观非协变性是欧拉坐标表述所致，两种重构形式均能一致描述任意惯性观测者下的波动；揭示伽利略变换作为洛伦兹变换低速极限存在的问题，并给出与多普勒效应中波长变化相关的潜在观测联系。

Conclusion: 机械波方程可在任意惯性系中一致表述，关键在于区分拉格朗日与欧拉表述；本研究为中级和高级本科物理提供概念澄清与教学价值，并提示与多普勒效应等观测现象的联系。

Abstract: The mechanical wave equation, derived from Newton's laws, takes its simplest form in the rest frame of the medium, and appears to lose that form entirely once expressed in the coordinates of another inertial frame --- raising the question of whether, and how, it can be written consistently for an arbitrary inertial observer. This puzzle, often glossed over in undergraduate instruction, is typically set aside by invoking the rest frame of the medium as ``preferred,'' without clarifying what this means for the principle of relativity underlying physical law. We revisit the wave equation from first principles and demonstrate that this apparent non-invariance stems from its expression in Eulerian coordinates. We present two reformulations: a manifestly invariant form using Lagrangian derivatives applied at the level of microscopic particle force laws, and a factorized Eulerian form separating left and right-moving wave solutions. In this context, we explore limitations of Galilean transformation as the low-speed limit of Lorentz transformation, and discuss potential observational connections, particularly regarding wavelength changes in the Doppler effect. Our derivation provides both conceptual clarity and pedagogical value for intermediate and advanced undergraduate physics.

</details>

### [237] [Soliton dynamics in the Gardner-Ostrovsky equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05468)
*R. Fariello,M. S. Soares,Y. A. Stepanyants*

Main category: nlin.PS

TL;DR: 研究了反常色散下非可积Gardner-Ostrovsky方程（旋转修正Gardner方程）中孤子的形成与相互作用，揭示了零总质量孤子及非弹性碰撞导致的"soliton-champion"现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gardner-Ostrovsky方程与等离子体及其他色散介质中的波动力学描述相关，其孤子形成与相互作用机制尚需深入探究。

Method: 针对具有反常（正）色散的非可积Gardner-Ostrovsky方程，通过数值模拟研究脉冲型初始扰动激发孤子的过程及其相互作用。

Result: 发现一类具有零总质量与非单调渐近特性的特殊孤子，可由脉冲型初始扰动生成，并形成规则振幅序孤子列、不规则非定常相互作用局域结构集合或定常传播的多孤子复合体；孤子相互作用本质为非弹性，在周期性边界条件下最终仅存最大振幅孤子，其余小振幅孤子通过连续碰撞被逐渐消除，但仍表现出显著鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Gardner-Ostrovsky方程中孤子相互作用是非弹性的，封闭系统中出现主导性"soliton-champion"，但孤子仍具有显著鲁棒性。

Abstract: We investigate soliton formation and interactions in the non-integrable Gardner-Ostrovsky equation (alias the rotation-modified Gardner equation) with anomalous (positive) dispersion. This equation is relevant to the description of wave dynamics in plasmas and other dispersive media. We show that a special class of solitons, characterized by zero total mass and non-monotonic asymptotics, can emerge from pulse-like initial perturbations. Depending on the initial conditions, these solitons may form regular amplitude-ordered trains, irregular nonstationary ensembles of interacting localized structures, or stationary propagating multi-soliton complexes. We further demonstrate that soliton interactions in the Gardner-Ostrovsky equation are inherently inelastic, leading to the emergence of a dominant "soliton-champion" in closed systems. For example, under periodic boundary conditions, only the largest-amplitude soliton ultimately survives, while all smaller solitons are gradually eliminated through successive interactions. Despite their inelastic interactions, Gardner-Ostrovsky solitons exhibit remarkable robustness.

</details>


### [238] [Whitham modulation theory for the Davey-Stewartson system and stability analysis of its periodic traveling wave solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06403)
*Gino Biondini,Alexander Chernyavsky,Haodong Lin,John Ringland*

Main category: nlin.PS

TL;DR: 本文发展了Davey-Stewartson (DS)系统的Whitham调制理论，系统研究了四类DS变体周期行波解的稳定性，发现仅散焦DSII系统的周期解线性稳定，其余变体均不稳定，且理论预测与线性化分析及数值模拟高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DS系统是二维弱非线性波包与慢变平均流共振耦合的典型模型，广泛描述有限深度水波、二次光学介质和磁性薄膜中的调制波列，但其周期行波解的稳定性缺乏系统的调制理论分析。本文旨在建立DS系统的Whitham调制理论，以统一研究四类变体周期解的稳定性。

Method: 1. 详细研究四类DS变体周期解的参数依赖性，包括其谐波极限与孤子极限；2. 建立DS系统的Whitham调制理论，推导四类变体的Whitham调制方程；3. 利用所得调制方程研究四类DS变体周期行波解的稳定性；4. 将理论预测与DS系统直接线性化及直接数值模拟进行对比验证。

Result: 理论预测与直接线性化分析及数值模拟结果高度吻合。结果表明：散焦DSII系统的周期解线性稳定，而其余所有DS变体的周期解均不稳定。计算还揭示出若干结构性特征，包括非演化型调制方程、容许初值的约束，以及行波解中不存在但对调制系统不可或缺的辅助平均场常数。

Conclusion: 本文成功建立了DS系统的Whitham调制理论并完成四类变体周期解稳定性的系统判定。所揭示的结构性特征预期会在任何具有平均场耦合的二维包络方程中重现，为更广泛的二维非线性波调制问题提供了理论范式。

Abstract: The Davey-Stewartson (DS) system is a canonical model for a weakly nonlinear wave packet in two spatial dimensions that is resonantly coupled to the slowly varying mean flow it generates, and as such it governs modulated wave trains in settings as different as finite-depth water waves, quadratic optical media and magnetic films. Here we develop the Whitham modulation theory for the DS system and use it to study the stability of its periodic traveling wave solutions. Specifically: 1. We perform a detailed study of the parametric dependence of the periodic solutions of all four variants of the DS system, including their harmonic and soliton limits. 2. We present the formulation of Whitham modulation theory for the DS system and write down the resulting Whitham modulation equations for all four variants of the DS system. 3. We use the resulting modulation equations to study the stability of the periodic traveling wave solutions of all four variants of the DS system. 4. We validate the theoretical predictions by comparing them with a direct linearization of the DS system as well as with the direct numerical simulations of the DS system, showing excellent agreement. The results indicate that the periodic solutions of the defocusing DSII system are linearly stable, whereas those of all other variants of the DS system are unstable. The calculations also evidence many structural features (non-evolutionary modulation equations, constraints on the admissible initial data, and auxiliary mean-field constants absent from the traveling wave solutions yet indispensable to the modulation system) are expected to recur in any two-dimensional envelope equation with mean-field coupling.

</details>


<div id='nlin.SI'></div>

# nlin.SI [[Back]](#toc)

### [239] [The Riemann theta function near soliton limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05716)
*Yuji Kodama*

Main category: nlin.SI

TL;DR: 本文将Jacobi椭圆函数(其平方)可表为sech²型单孤子无穷和这一经典结论推广到高亏格情形，证明该结构源于Riemann θ函数的拟周期性：在孤子极限下，实Riemann θ函数对数二阶导数可由R^g中实正则g-孤子解的求和逼近，并据此研究KdV/KP方程的拟周期解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知低亏格(椭圆函数)情形下Jacobi椭圆函数可由sech²型单孤子之和表示，作者希望揭示该现象背后的本质机制，并将其推广到高亏格(高维、拟周期)情形。

Method: 利用实Riemann θ函数的拟周期性；考察log θ二阶导数在孤子极限下的渐近行为，用R^g中的实、正则g-孤子(Hirota型)解之和进行逼近；借助将相应Riemann面单值化的Schottky群构造拟周期解；并通过收缩部分同调圈讨论拟周期背景上的孤子。

Result: 证明log θ二阶导数在孤子极限下可被实正则g-孤子解之和良好逼近，由此得到R^g的一种镶嵌(tessellation)，其瓷砖为斜棱柱，被θ函数中主导指数对应的超平面分成2^g个部分；并将结果应用于KdV与KP方程的拟周期解构造。

Conclusion: Jacobi椭圆函数的孤子和表示是高亏格情形的一般结构的特例，其根源是Riemann θ函数的拟周期性；该框架统一描述了KdV/KP的拟周期解及拟周期背景上的孤子。

Abstract: It has been known that (the square of) Jacobi elliptic function can be expressed by an infinite sum of single solitons of $\sech^2$ shape. In this paper, we show that there exists a similar structure for higher genus cases. It turns out that this is just a consequence of the quasi-periodicity of the Riemann $θ$-function. More precisely, we show that the second derivative of $\log θ$ with the \emph{real} Riemann $θ$-function of genus $g$ near soliton limit can be well approximated by the \emph{sum} of \emph{real} and \emph{regular} $g$-soliton solutions of Hirota-type in $g$-dimensional real space $\R^g$. This leads to a tessellation of $\R^g$, whose tile is an oblique prism divided into $2^g$ sections by hyper-planes of dominant exponents in the theta function. We apply the results to study quasi-periodic solutions to the KdV and KP equations. We construct the quasi-periodic solutions using the Schottky group, which uniformizes the corresponding Riemann surfaces. We also discuss solitons on quasi-periodic background by pinching some of the homological cycles

</details>


<div id='cs.SD'></div>

# cs.SD [[Back]](#toc)

### [240] [Hallucination Reduction for LLM-Based Audio Understanding via Multimodal Direct Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04004)
*Bebe Cosgrove,Aaron Isidore Grace,Weiran Wang*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出将多模态直接偏好优化（mDPO）目标扩展到音频领域，通过对比完整音频与多种声学扰动版本，迫使大型音频语言模型（LALMs）以声学输入为依据生成内容，从而缓解幻觉与文本先验依赖。在Qwen2-Audio上于DCASE 2025与AH Existence数据集验证，DCASE绝对准确率提升14.0%，AH Existence提升27.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型音频语言模型在同时接收音频与文本输入时，容易产生幻觉并过度依赖文本先验，导致生成内容脱离实际声学输入。需要一种机制引导模型以音频信息为生成基础，从而抑制此类幻觉。

Method: 采用多模态直接偏好优化（mDPO）目标，将偏好学习框架扩展至音频域：通过构造"完整音频"与"扰动音频"的对比对，施加多种声学扰动（如时间反转、频率掩蔽、随机噪声等），迫使模型将生成建立在声学输入之上，并以Qwen2-Audio作为骨干模型进行训练与评估。

Result: 在DCASE 2025 Challenge与AH Existence数据集上评估显示：扩展到LALMs的mDPO显著增强了复杂DCASE数据集上的时间推理能力，并改善了AH基准上的基础存在性验证性能。最有效的扰动为时间反转、频率掩蔽和随机噪声。最终在DCASE 2025上取得14.0%的绝对准确率提升，在AH Existence上提升27.4%。

Conclusion: 将mDPO扩展至音频领域能有效缓解LALMs的幻觉与文本先验依赖问题，提升音频时序推理与存在性验证能力，其中特定的声学扰动类型（时间反转、频率掩蔽、随机噪声）效果最佳。

Abstract: Large Audio Language Models (LALMs) are prone to hallucinating and over-relying on text priors when simultaneously presented with audio and text inputs. To mitigate these hallucinations, we propose utilizing the multimodal Direct Preference Optimization (mDPO) objective, which forces the model to ground its generation in the acoustic input by contrasting intact and distorted audio counterparts. We extend this preference learning framework to the audio domain by applying a variety of acoustic perturbations. Evaluating the Qwen2-Audio backbone across the DCASE 2025 Challenge and AH Existence datasets, we demonstrate that extending mDPO to LALMs significantly enhances temporal reasoning in the complex DCASE dataset, and improves performance on basic existence verification in the AH benchmark. We identify temporal reversal, frequency masking, and random noise as the most effective perturbations. Ultimately, our approach achieves an absolute accuracy improvement of 14.0% on the DCASE 2025 dataset and 27.4% on AH Existence.

</details>


### [241] [Temporal Anchors and Editing Sensitivity in Partial Speech Spoofing: A Controlled Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04479)
*Xiaosu Su,Yun Cao,Yiping Ni,Xiaowei Yi*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 该文诊断部分伪造检测器的时序锚点与边界一致性训练，指出常用拼接评测无法隔离独特因果伪迹，并报告了层选择、单元类型、增强策略及阈值设定对漏检/误报的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分伪造检测需在拒绝合成内容的同时接受良性编辑，但现有评测范式可能无法区分编辑误报与合成内容漏检，从而妨碍对因果机制的识别。

Method: 使用冻结WavLM特征、非语言单元控制与源锁定评测；构建真-真与真-假拼接以对比编辑误报和合成内容漏检；考察层6特征下phone单元与soft frames的差异；在500例回溯性PS-eval上做增强实验，并进行200例Llama A随访及固定阈值排序分析。

Result: 层6特征下phone单元PartialSpoof评测frame EER高于soft frames（11.18%对9.59%）；增强降低真拼接误报，在5% PS-development FPR下合成核心漏检增加但1%下不显著；Llama A随访保留误报下降但未确立漏检率上升；构造案例排序可改善而固定阈值漏检上升；一致性无一致增益，编码器层排序随语料变化。

Conclusion: 部分伪造评测应视为回溯性诊断，需结合帧级/事件级定位、多随机种子与单元类型报告结果；现有边界一致性与时序锚点策略缺乏稳健、统一的增益。

Abstract: Partial-spoof detectors must reject synthetic content while accepting benign edits. We diagnose temporal anchors and boundary-consistency training using frozen WavLM features, nonlinguistic unit controls, and source-locked evaluation. Genuine-genuine and genuine-fake splices contrast editing false alarms with synthetic-content misses; they do not isolate a unique causal artifact. With layer-6 features, phone units have higher PartialSpoof evaluation frame EER than soft frames (11.18% versus 9.59%). In 500 retrospective PS-eval cases, augmentation reduces genuine-splice false alarms; synthetic-core misses increase at 5% PS-development FPR but not demonstrably at 1%. A 200-case Llama A follow-up retains the false-alarm reduction but does not establish a miss-rate increase. Constructed-case ranking can improve while fixed-threshold misses rise. Consistency gives no uniform gain, and encoder-layer rankings change across corpora. We report frame and event localization with three seeds, including word units, and treat PS evaluation as retrospective. Audit code, selected training scripts, and result summaries are available at https://github.com/mysxs/partial-spoof-diagnostics.

</details>


### [242] [Can LLM Agents Automate Reinforcement Learning for Text-to-Speech?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04488)
*Xuanjun Chen,Zixiong Su,Hao Shi,Chang Zeng,Kai Li,Jyh-Shing Roger Jang,Hung-yi Lee*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 研究以大语言模型智能体自主接手强化学习后训练TTS调优流程，发现智能体能在缺乏规格说明的情况下复原并改进配方，但自主性带来数据、代理指标、算法三方面的测量陷阱，真正的瓶颈在于测量而非推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习后训练虽能修复零样本TTS的局部片段性错误，但获得可用配方仍依赖繁琐的人工调参。核心疑问是：LLM智能体能否接管这一研究流程，以及它在多大程度上能自动化并可靠地执行该流程。

Method: 提出AgenticTTS-Forge，一种围绕共享工作空间组织人类指导与智能体执行的协作式工作流，并应用于CosyVoice2-0.5B。通过两个维度评估：一是将智能体轨迹按阶段与已发表配方逐段审计，衡量其自动化了什么；二是用智能体不可见的留出观测器（held-out observers）对其策略打分，衡量其利用了哪些未被察觉的信号。

Result: 智能体复原了一份规格不完整的配方并对其加以改进；当收益停滞时，它会主动检索文献，并将载体从LM转向flow，使Bad case减半。但自主性暴露出三类陷阱：留出集通过与合约从未读取的通道发生泄漏；自塑奖励在其中被评分的代理指标上造成虚高；分别调优的策略无法可加地组合。

Conclusion: 制约AI研究智能体的关键约束是测量而非推理能力。这一发现可为设计"合约能读取智能体所接触的每一个通道"的harness（评测脚手架）提供指导，从而避免数据泄漏、代理指标虚高与策略不可组合等问题。

Abstract: Although reinforcement learning (RL) post-training repairs the localized segmental errors of zero-shot text-to-speech (TTS), arriving at a working recipe still relies on tedious manual tuning, and whether LLM agents can take over this research pipeline is unclear. We investigate this question with AgenticTTS-Forge, a collaborative workflow that structures human guidance and agentic execution around a shared workspace, applied to CosyVoice2-0.5B. To measure what the agent automates, we audit its trajectory stage by stage against the published recipe. To measure what it exploits, we score its policies with held-out observers hidden from the agent. Our results show that the agent recovers an underspecified recipe, improves it, and, when gains stall, surveys the literature unprompted and pivots from the LM carrier to the flow carrier, halving Bad cases. However, its autonomy exposes three traps across the data, proxy, and algorithm axes: the held-out set leaks through a channel the contract never reads, a self-shaped reward inflates the proxy where it is scored, and separately tuned policies do not compose additively. These findings show that the binding constraint is measurement rather than reasoning, and can inform the design of harnesses whose contracts read every channel the agent does.

</details>


### [243] [VoiceWeaver: Staged Learning of Structured Controls for Expressive Speech and Sound-Event Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04500)
*Xiaosu Su,Yun Cao,Yiping Ni,Xiaowei Yi*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: VoiceWeaver 通过结构化标签前缀与分阶段训练，在共享文本-音频模型中逐步加入情感、语调与环境属性，同时借助回放蒸馏与正则化手段缓解灾难性遗忘，实现多属性可控语音生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为语音生成器添加情感、语调、环境等表达能力与环境控制时，需要学习异构属性且不丢失已有能力，存在多任务属性融合与能力遗忘的矛盾。

Method: 采用结构化标签前缀（structured label prefixes）与分阶段的情感-语调-事件训练（staged emotion-tone-event training），统一于共享文本-音频模型；使用基于回放的蒸馏（replay-based distillation）保留早期预测，并用嵌入去相关（embedding decorrelation）与属性丢弃（attribute dropout）对条件进行正则化。

Result: 评估分为单属性正确性、情感-事件联合生成与三属性正确性。情感准确率中文 86%、英文 83%；中文情感-事件联合准确率约 70%（混合任务训练 56%、Ming-omni-tts 48%），英文联合准确率 66%；三属性准确率中文 59%、英文 55%（各 500 样本，合并 57%）。文本错误率高于外部 TTS 基线。

Conclusion: VoiceWeaver 在多属性可控语音生成上取得稳定的分项与联合表现，联合与三属性任务优于对比方法，但文本错误率仍高于外部 TTS 基线，文本保真度有待改进。

Abstract: Adding expressive and environmental controls to a speech generator requires learning heterogeneous attributes without losing earlier capabilities. VoiceWeaver addresses this problem with structured label prefixes and staged emotion-tone-event training in a shared text-audio model. Replay-based distillation retains earlier predictions, while embedding decorrelation and attribute dropout regularize conditioning. Evaluation separates single-attribute correctness, joint emotion-event generation, and three-attribute correctness. Rounded to whole percentage points, emotion accuracy is 86% in Chinese and 83% in English. Chinese emotion-event joint accuracy is about 70%, versus 56% for mixed-task training and 48% for Ming-omni-tts; English joint accuracy is 66%. Three-attribute accuracy is 59% in Chinese and 55% in English on 500 samples each (57% pooled). Text error rates exceed those of the external TTS baselines. Audio samples are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/api/repo/VoiceWeaver1-4D88/file/index.html.

</details>


### [244] ["Spectral Harmony" Towards the Unification of Timbre and Speech: Resolution of the Young Mahler Schoenberg Dream](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04570)
*Yusei TAMURA,Shigekazu ISHIHARA,Ken ITO*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于信息几何的音色序列化方法，通过计算多音和弦音色谱的Wasserstein距离并将其嵌入Banach空间，分析了R. Strauss、Mahler、Berg、Webern作品的音色亲和与分化，并尝试统一乐器音色与语音，量化"元音三角形"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mahler与Schoenberg在19世纪末至20世纪初设想的音色序列化未能实现，Schoenberg提出的序列主义、音色旋律与说唱三个历史难题，无论在二人有生之年还是战后Nono、Boulez、Stockhausen一代都未获完全解决。作者希望从音色视角重新审视音乐思维，为诠释、演奏与作曲开辟新可能。

Method: 基于信息几何，计算复调音色谱之间的Wasserstein距离，以三个及以上音构成的和弦谱二阶矩表示（单位Hz²）；将这些量嵌入Banach空间，用于分析具体作品段落的音色亲和与分化；以wah wah弱音器为例，构建将乐器音色与语音纳入同一Banach空间的模型，并尝试以Hz量化"元音三角形"。所有工具已在GitHub公开。

Result: 同一和弦在不同配器下表现出不同的音色亲和与分化；以Wasserstein距离与Banach空间嵌入可对音色关系进行量化；模型可将乐器音色与语音置于同一空间，并以Hz对"元音三角形"进行初步量化。这些工作构成对Schoenberg三个历史难题的部分但整合性的解决方案。

Conclusion: 该研究为音色序列化及Schoenberg三个历史难题提供了部分而整合的解决途径，提出从音色视角整体重思音乐思维，并为诠释、演奏与作曲开启新的可能性；工具公开可复现、可扩展。

Abstract: In this article, we present a method of spectral serialization envisioned by G. Mahler and A. Schoenberg between the late 19th and early 20th centuries that enables the selection of timbres rooted in musical structure. Based on information geometry, we evaluate the Wasserstein distance between polyphonic timbre spectra, as demonstrated in our previous paper, in terms of the second moment of spectral chords comprising three or more notes, expressed in squared hertz. By embedding these quantities in a Banach space, we analyze specific passages from works by R. Strauss, Mahler, A. Berg, and A. Webern from the perspective of timbre, showing how the same chord exhibits different affinities and divergences depending on orchestration. Taking the wah wah mute as an example, we propose a model that treats instrumental timbre and speech within the same Banach space, and we attempt to quantify the "vowel triangle" in Hz. These efforts constitute a partial but integrated solution to the historical three problems of Schoenberg. Serialism, Klangfarbenmelodie, and Sprechgesang which neither Mahler nor Schoenberg in their lifetimes, nor the postwar generation of composers including Nono, Boulez, and Stockhausen, was able to resolve fully. We aim to reconsider musical thought as a whole from the perspective of timbre and to open new possibilities for interpretation, performance, and composition. All tools are publicly available on GitHub, so that anyone can reproduce and extend these analyses.

</details>


### [245] [GS-Codec: A Gaussian-Splatting Bottleneck for Neural Audio Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04651)
*Ron Aluf,Alon Canfi,Eliya Nachmani*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出 GS-Codec，用一维高斯泼溅参数化信号分解替代传统量化器作为神经语音编解码器瓶颈，匹配或超越 EnCodec、DAC 等基线，并支持无需重训的细粒度码率控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流神经音频编解码器（RVQ、FSQ、单码本等）均沿用 VQ-VAE 范式，通过可学习码本或固定标量网格对隐变量进行离散划分。作者质疑这一离散划分是否必要，试图摆脱量化器瓶颈的固有约束。

Method: 将三维场景重建中的高斯泼溅迁移到一维隐变量，用内层优化循环把每个编码器隐段拟合为一维高斯基元（primitive）的加权和，解码器由渲染求和重建波形；为规避推理时迭代开销，训练轻量 GS Predictor Net 单次前向回归基元参数；编解码器通过内层循环端到端训练，训练管线中无量化器，仅在训练后对标量量化拟合参数。码率控制通过调节基元数量与每参数量化位深实现。

Result: 在可比码率下，GS-Codec 在说话人相似度（SIM）、可懂度（STOI）、感知质量（UTMOS）上匹配或超过 EnCodec、DAC 等成熟开源编解码器，语义性能（WER）相当；单一检查点即可后训练调节码率与质量权衡。

Conclusion: 表明量化器并非神经语音编解码器的必要瓶颈，参数化信号分解可作为有效替代，并带来灵活的码率-质量权衡。

Abstract: Neural audio codecs compress waveforms into compact discrete tokens that underpin speech language models, real-time communication, and large-scale audio storage. Almost every dominant design, including residual vector quantization, finite scalar quantization, and single-codebook variants, follows the VQ-VAE template by partitioning the encoder latent through a learned codebook or a fixed scalar grid. We ask whether this partition is necessary. We introduce GS-Codec, a neural speech codec whose bottleneck is a parametric signal decomposition rather than a quantizer. We adapt Gaussian splatting from 3D scene reconstruction to one-dimensional latents. An inner optimization loop fits each encoder segment as a weighted sum of 1D Gaussian primitives. The decoder then reconstructs the waveform from the rendered sum. To avoid the cost of this iterative inner loop at inference time, we additionally train a lightweight GS Predictor Net that regresses the primitive parameters in a single forward pass. The encoder and decoder are trained end-to-end through the inner loop, with no quantizer anywhere in the training pipeline: the bottleneck is the decomposition itself, and scalar quantization is applied only post-training to the fitted parameters. Rather than relying on discrete codebook stages for bitrate control, our representation exposes a fine-grained rate-quality tradeoff: a single trained checkpoint supports post-training bitrate control by varying the number of primitives and the per-parameter bit depth, with no retraining required. GS-Codec matches or exceeds well-established open-source codecs such as EnCodec and DAC on speaker similarity (SIM), intelligibility (STOI), and perceptual quality (UTMOS) at comparable bitrates, while achieving comparable semantic performance (WER). Code and audio samples are available at https://ronaluf.github.io/gs-codec/

</details>


### [246] [Prompt-Consistency Inference for Zero-Shot Flow-Matching Text-to-Speech Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04757)
*Vasily Zadorozhnyy,Can Goksen,Kazuhito Koishida,Dung Tran*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出免训练的Prompt-Consistency Inference (PCI)，在每次速度场评估前将提示块恢复为解析值，纠正其漂移，从而在不增加网络评估的情况下提升零样本TTS的说话人相似度与可懂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配TTS在推理时用ODE求解器演化一个同时涵盖提示(prompt)与生成区域的状态，但最终只保留生成区域。被丢弃的提示状态仍通过耦合两个区域的速度场影响生成，且随采样推进会偏离规定的条件路径，给后续更新引入偏差。而提示路径与生成轨迹不同，可由参考音频和初始噪声以闭式解获得，这一可利用性尚未被利用。

Method: PCI是一个免训练规则：在每次速度场评估之前，将提示块重置为其解析(analytic)值，而生成块保持不变；不增加额外的网络评估。并通过消融实验分析校正所覆盖的采样阶段与校正后提示偏差(discrepancy)的变化。

Result: 在所评估的多个流匹配TTS骨干上，PCI均提升了说话人相似度与可懂度，且未增加网络评估次数。消融实验表明，PCI使每步后的提示偏差更小，且覆盖较晚采样阶段的校正即可恢复大部分相似度增益。

Conclusion: 提示状态的漂移是流匹配TTS推理中的一个可修正误差来源；通过将提示块恢复到其闭式解析值即可获得稳定收益，且该修正的作用主要体现在采样后期阶段。

Abstract: In recent years, flow-matching models have produced significant improvements in zero-shot text-to-speech synthesis. Conditioned on an audio prompt and text, these models learn a velocity field and generate speech by iteratively solving an ODE. During inference, the solver evolves a single state spanning both the prompt and the region to be generated, although only the generated region is ultimately retained. The discarded prompt state, however, still matters; its intermediate values influence generation through the velocity field that couples the two regions. As sampling continues, this state can drift away from the prescribed conditional path, introducing a discrepancy into subsequent generation updates. Unlike the unknown generated trajectory, the prompt path is available in closed form from the reference audio and the initial noise. We exploit this observation with Prompt-Consistency Inference (PCI), a training-free rule that restores the prompt block to its analytic value before each velocity evaluation, while leaving the generated block unchanged. PCI improves speaker similarity and intelligibility across the evaluated flow-matching TTS backbones without additional network evaluations. Our ablation studies further show that PCI keeps post-step prompt discrepancies smaller and that corrections covering the later sampling stages recover much of the observed similarity gain.

</details>


### [247] [EchoChat: Structured Cognitive Reasoning in Empathetic Spoken Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04826)
*Dingdong Wang,Shujie Liu,Yayue Deng,Yuxuan Hu,Yunrui Cai,Jincenzi Wu,Jianwei Yu,Jinyu Li,Helen Meng*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出EchoChat统一框架，将共情口语对话重构为"感知—心理状态推理—回应生成"的结构化认知推理过程，并配套数据集、基准与训练方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前SpeechLLM将共情视为直接的输入到回应映射，导致交互"表面温暖"但情感空洞；且共情依赖多阶段、强步骤间依赖的过程，中间步骤错误会级联传播，而现有训练范式缺乏精确定位并改进此类错误的机制。

Method: 1) 构建EchoDialogue-400K声学丰富数据集，用于多阶段共情监督；2) SFT阶段通过Acoustic-Anchored Attention (AAA)强化声学接地；3) RL阶段引入阶段感知优化目标与Step-Decomposed Credit Assignment (SDCA)，定位推理错误并缓解级联误差传播；4) 提出EchoEval专家标注基准，进行多维度共情评估。

Result: 大量实验表明EchoChat在感知、推理与回应对齐方面均取得state-of-the-art性能。

Conclusion: EchoChat通过将共情口语对话结构化为认知推理过程并结合阶段感知训练与评估，有效缓解了表层共情与误差级联问题。

Abstract: Empathetic spoken dialogue is a sophisticated cognitive process that requires not only recognizing emotions but also inferring a user's latent mental states to provide appropriate support. However, current SpeechLLMs often treat empathy as a direct input-to-response mapping, leading to "superficially warm" but emotionally hollow interactions. In addition, since empathy relies on a multi-stage process with strong inter-step dependency, errors at any intermediate step can cascade through subsequent steps and lead to inappropriate responses, while existing training paradigms lack mechanisms to precisely localize and improve such errors. In this work, we propose EchoChat, a unified framework that reformulates empathetic spoken dialogue as a structured cognitive reasoning process integrating perception, mental-state reasoning, and response generation. To support this paradigm, we first construct EchoDialogue-400K, an acoustically rich dataset for multi-stage empathetic supervision. During the SFT stage, we strengthen acoustic grounding through proposed Acoustic-Anchored Attention (AAA). During the RL stage, we further introduce a novel stage-aware optimization objective with Step-Decomposed Credit Assignment (SDCA) to localize reasoning errors and mitigate cascaded error propagation. In addition, we introduce EchoEval, an expert-annotated benchmark for multi-dimensional empathy evaluation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EchoChat achieves state-of-the-art performance in perception, reasoning, and response alignment. Project page: https://github.com/dingdongwang/EchoChat

</details>


### [248] [A Multidimensional Model for Quantifying Tonal Strength: A Continuous Framework of Analyzing Tonal Evolution Beyond Tonal-Atonal Binary Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04871)
*Yuliang Li,Nan Nan,Meilian Gu,Xiaohong Guan*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于调性理论的多维框架，通过音级分布均匀度、调性中心时间稳定性、局部调性清晰度三个特征量化"调性强度"，并构建二元数据集与历史MIDI语料验证其能刻画从共性实践调性到无调性的连续演变轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二十世纪初从共性实践调性向无调性的渐变无法用二元标签（调性/无调性）刻画，需要一种连续、多维的度量来表征中间调性状态并追踪其历史演变。

Method: 提出多维调性强度量化框架，融合调性理论与调性瓦解机制，用三个互补特征操作化：（1）音级分布均匀度（对应全局统计规律性）；（2）调性中心的时间稳定性（对应时间连贯性）；（3）局部调性清晰度（对应局部知觉一致性）。验证数据包括含196首调性与无调性作品的二元数据集，以及涵盖巴洛克至二十世纪初、21位作曲家共1,561部作品的历史MIDI语料。

Result: 模型在二元分类任务上F_1=0.985，表现优异；定量揭示出从稳固调性经逐步弱化到最终瓦解的连续历史轨迹；对代表性作曲家的计算分析证实晚期浪漫派与现代主义作曲家生涯中调性强度显著下降；在EMOPIA数据集上发现调性强度值与积极情绪效价之间存在弱但统计显著的正相关。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一个统一的调性演变量化模型，为未来关于调性组织、音乐情绪与认知之间关系的行为学与神经生理学研究提供了连续度量工具。

Abstract: Tonality is a fundamental organizing principle of Western music. However, the gradual transition from common-practice tonality to atonality around the turn of the twentieth century cannot be captured by binary labels. To characterize intermediate tonal states and trace this evolution, we propose a multidimensional framework for quantifying tonal strength, grounded in tonal theory and mechanisms underlying tonal dissolution. This framework operationalizes the global statistical regularity, temporal coherence, and local perceptual alignment of tonal organization through three complementary features: pitch-class distribution uniformity, temporal stability of tonal centers, and local tonal clarity, respectively. To validate the model, we introduce two new datasets: a binary dataset comprising 196 tonal and atonal pieces, and a historical MIDI corpus of 1,561 compositions by 21 composers spanning the Baroque era to the early twentieth century. The proposed model achieves high discriminative performance on the binary classification task (F_1=0.985), and quantitatively reveals a continuous historical trajectory from established tonality through progressive weakening to eventual dissolution. Computational analysis of representative composers further confirms significant declines in tonal strength throughout the careers of late-Romantic and modernist figures. Additionally, analysis of EMOPIA dataset reveals a weak but statistically significant positive correlation between higher tonal strength values and positive emotional valence. This work establishes a unified quantitative model for analyzing tonal evolution and provides a continuous measure for future behavioral and neurophysiological investigations of the relationships among tonal organization, musical emotion, and cognition.

</details>


### [249] [TS-SP: Learning Speaker-Preserving Representations in Audio Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04887)
*Junjie Li,Zheng Liang,Zhe Li,Tianchi Liu,Kong Aik Lee*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出TS-SP两阶段说话人保持框架，通过LoRA适配音频编码器与语言模型，在Qwen2.5-Omni-7B上显著降低说话人验证EER。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频大语言模型虽能理解语音内容，但利用说话人身份进行验证的能力仍然有限，需要一种能学习说话人保持表征并让语言模型组件可访问该表征的方法。

Method: 提出TS-SP两阶段框架，在Qwen2.5-Omni-7B上实例化：第一阶段用说话人身份监督适配音频编码器；第二阶段冻结适配后的编码器，训练语言模型进行说话人比较；两阶段均使用低秩适配（LoRA），保持预训练基座权重固定。

Result: 在Vox1-O上，TS-SP将EER从Paired Loss Adaptation基线的7.01%降至4.37%；在未见提示下EER保持在4.31–4.79%。在CN-Celeb跨域评估中，EER与基线相近，但在原生判决阈值下准确率更低。

Conclusion: 结果表明两阶段适配在所评估的骨干模型上能有效提升说话人验证性能，为增强ALLM的说话人验证能力提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Audio large language models (ALLMs) can understand speech content, yet their ability to use speaker identity for verification remains limited. We propose TS-SP (Two-Stage Speaker Preservation), a parameter-efficient framework for learning speaker-preserving representations and making them accessible to an ALLM's language-model component. We instantiate and evaluate TS-SP on Qwen2.5-Omni-7B. First, we adapt the audio encoder with speaker identity supervision. We then freeze the adapted encoder and train the language model to compare speakers. Both stages use low-rank adaptation (LoRA), keeping the pretrained base weights fixed. On Vox1-O, TS-SP reduces the equal error rate (EER) from 7.01\% for the Paired Loss Adaptation Baseline to 4.37\%. EER remains within 4.31--4.79\% under unseen prompts. Cross-domain evaluation on CN-Celeb yields a similar EER to the baseline, but lower accuracy at the native decision threshold. These findings support two-stage adaptation for improving speaker verification on the evaluated backbone.

</details>


### [250] [Tracing a Sparse Emotion-Control Circuit in LLM-Based Text-to-Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05080)
*Hongfei Du,Jiacheng Shi,Yanfu Zhang,Ye Gao*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 该论文通过激活修补揭示了LLM语音合成模型中情感信息的稀疏因果回路：约23–27个注意力头与MLP（占比约5%）即可解释74–88%的情感读出偏移，并在解码语音中引发一致的音高、能量与频谱亮度变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM式TTS已能生成情感丰富的语音，但参考情感如何经由模型内部传递并在解码语音中体现仍缺乏机制性解释，尤其是"内部读出变化"与"声学输出变化"之间的因果链条不清晰。

Method: 在匹配的中性/情感参考条件下，提出两个情感敏感指标——codec轨迹分数与晚期残差方向分数，用于对激活修补干预进行评分；据此定位"源到读出"的组件级回路，并做跨情感激活交换与残差方向匹配基线对照，结合24对匹配样本的声学分析。

Result: 每个情感仅需23–27个注意力头与MLP（约占候选组件5%）即可恢复或抑制held-out样本上74–88%的晚期情感读出偏移；回路由共享骨干加情感特异组件构成；跨情感激活交换在48例中47例降低目标读出；解码语音中音高、能量、频谱亮度出现一致变化；读出匹配的残差方向基线仅产生17–27%的音高效应。

Conclusion: 研究刻画了一条从参考前缀信息到生成语音情感属性的紧凑因果路径，且证明内部读出移动本身不足以解释解码声学的改变，说明情感表达依赖可定位的稀疏组件回路而非单一读出方向。

Abstract: LLM-based text-to-speech (TTS) models can generate emotionally expressive speech, but how reference emotion is routed through the model and realized in decoded speech remains unclear. We introduce two emotion-sensitive metrics for matched neutral and emotional syntheses---a codec trajectory score and a late residual direction score---and use them to score activation-patching interventions. Under controlled matched-reference conditions, this analysis identifies a sparse source-to-readout component-level circuit: 23--27 attention heads and MLPs per emotion, roughly 5% of the components considered, recover or suppress 74--88% of the late emotion-readout shift on held-out cases. The circuit combines a shared component backbone with emotion-specific components; cross-emotion activation swaps reduce the target readout in 47 of 48 cases. In decoded speech, the same intervention produces consistent changes in pitch, energy, and spectral brightness over 24 matched pairs per emotion. A readout-matched residual-direction baseline produces only 17--27% of the intervention's pitch effect, showing that internal readout movement alone does not explain the decoded acoustic changes. These results trace a compact causal route from reference-derived prefix information to emotion-relevant properties of generated speech.

</details>


### [251] [NeuMark-Native: Robust Text-to-Speech-Native Watermarking Through Full Utilization of Neural Audio Codec Latent Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05215)
*Annan Wu,Wen-Chin Huang,Tomoki Toda*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: NeuMark-Native 是一个TTS原生的水印框架，将载荷嵌入codec潜层而非生成波形，从而提升水印在DSP与神经codec重合成下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音水印多为事后处理，在TTS生成波形后再加扰动，易被跳过或绕过，且水印仅停留在浅层波形表示，持久性差。

Method: 在基于神经codec的TTS合成中，于波形解码前将载荷信息嵌入每个生成的codec潜层；冻结预训练TTS模型与神经codec，仅优化水印模块。

Result: 在两个语料库、11种DSP攻击与9种神经codec攻击下实现稳健的水印检测，同时保持接近合成语音的自然度、可懂度与语音质量。

Conclusion: 将水印内嵌于TTS的codec潜层空间，可兼顾鲁棒性与语音质量，为合成语音提供更可靠的可追溯性。

Abstract: Speech watermarking offers proactive traceability for synthetic speech, yet most existing models operate only after text-to-speech (TTS) synthesis by adding a watermark perturbation to the generated waveform. This post-hoc design leaves watermarking as an external step that can be omitted or bypassed and restricts the watermark to a shallow waveform representation. We propose NeuMark-Native, a TTS-native watermarking framework for neural codec-based synthesis. It embeds payload information into every generated codec-latent layer before waveform decoding, improving watermark persistence under downstream digital signal processing (DSP) and neural codec resynthesis. NeuMark-Native keeps the pretrained TTS model and the neural codec frozen, while optimizing only the watermark modules on generated codec tokens. Experiments on two corpora under 11 DSP attacks and 9 neural-codec attacks demonstrate robust watermark detection while preserving naturalness, intelligibility, and speech quality close to synthetic speech.

</details>


### [252] [Task-Aware Joint Pruning and Distillation for Efficient Audio Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05264)
*Miao He,Peng Cheng,Zhongjie Ba,Qing Wen,Li Lu,Xin Yang,Kui Ren*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出任务感知的联合剪枝与蒸馏框架，将SSL语音深伪检测器压缩至31.9M参数，FLOPs减少6.3倍，性能平均仅下降1.30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于自监督学习（SSL）的语音深伪检测器性能领先，但参数量通常超过300M，难以部署于资源受限设备；而现有主要面向内容中心任务的压缩方法直接迁移到深伪检测时性能明显下降。

Method: 任务感知的联合剪枝与蒸馏框架（Task-Aware Joint Pruning and Distillation），结合跨域知识蒸馏与运动引导的结构化剪枝（movement-guided structured pruning），在激进压缩下传递伪造判别知识并保留关键结构。

Result: 模型压缩至31.9M参数，FLOPs减少6.3倍；在多个数据集上相较未压缩基线平均性能仅下降1.30%。

Conclusion: 该框架在保持竞争力的检测性能的同时大幅降低计算开销，展现出端侧部署的潜力。

Abstract: Advances in speech synthesis have made deepfake speeches increasingly convincing, posing growing threats to security. While self-supervised learning (SSL) based detectors achieve state-of-the-art performance, their computational demands (typically 300M+ parameters) prevent deployment on resource-constrained devices. Existing compression methods, designed mainly for content-centric tasks, struggle to maintain competitive performance when directly adapted to deepfake detection. We propose a Task-Aware Joint Pruning and Distillation framework that combines cross-domain knowledge distillation with movement-guided structured pruning to transfer forgery-discriminative knowledge and preserve critical structures under aggressive compression. Our framework reduces the model to 31.9M parameters with 6.3$\times$ FLOPs reduction, with an average performance drop of only 1.30\% across multiple datasets compared to the uncompressed baseline, demonstrating strong potential for on-device deployment.

</details>


### [253] [SheetSage2: Coherent Lead-Sheet Transcription with Synthetic Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05336)
*Junyan Jiang,Ruibin Yuan,Jiahao Pan,Wei Xue,Yike Guo,Gus Xia,Yann LeCun*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: SheetSage2 是一个统一的音乐转谱框架，通过合成数据、任务专用结构化解码和自回归蒸馏，在 15 个基准指标对中的 12 个上超越先前系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将音乐转写为人类可读乐谱需要对节奏、和声、旋律和曲式的连贯理解。该目标受两个障碍限制：带标注的录音稀缺，且精确的局部预测仍可能产生不一致的音乐序列。

Method: 提出 SheetSage2 统一框架，包含三部分：1) 利用自动标注的 MIDI 渲染为音频，为多种音乐理解任务提供可扩展监督；2) 任务专用的结构化解码器，整合互补的音乐线索及其时间依赖关系以生成音乐上连贯的乐谱；3) 自回归蒸馏，在推理时无需任务专用动态规划即可保持转谱精度。

Result: 在八个基准集合上，单个 SheetSage2-AR 模型在评估的 15 个基准-指标对中的 12 个上超越了所列先前系统，显著优于 SheetSage1，并在若干基准上超过任务专用模型。模型权重与推理代码已公开。

Conclusion: SheetSage2 通过合成数据、结构化解码和自回归蒸馏的统一框架，在保持推理高效的同时实现了领先的音乐转谱性能，并公开了模型与代码。

Abstract: Transcribing music into a human-readable score requires a coherent understanding of rhythm, harmony, melody, and form. Two obstacles limit this goal: annotated recordings are scarce, and accurate local predictions can still produce inconsistent musical sequences. We present SheetSage2, a unified music transcription framework that combines synthetic data, task-specific structured decoding, and autoregressive distillation. Automatically annotated MIDI, rendered into audio, provides scalable supervision across music understanding tasks. Task-specific structured decoders integrate complementary musical cues and their temporal dependencies to produce musically coherent scores. Autoregressive distillation further retains transcription accuracy without task-specific dynamic programming at inference. Across eight benchmark collections, a single SheetSage2-AR model exceeds the listed prior systems on 12 of 15 benchmark--metric pairs in our evaluation, substantially improving over SheetSage1 and surpassing task-specific models on several benchmarks. Model weights and inference code are publicly available.

</details>


### [254] [SEA-LM: Egocentric Spatial Audio Understanding for Wearable Microphone Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05610)
*Sonal Kumar,Sinan Hersek,Artem Dementyev,Mengzhen Pan,Ishan Chatterjee,Anurag Kumar,Ramani Duraiswami,Dinesh Manocha,Andrea Colaco*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出 SEA-LM 空间音频理解模型：以 FOACODER 编码器处理可变配置智能眼镜阵列的一阶 Ambisonics，结合两阶段六任务课程训练 MLLM，并用时空加权交叉熵损失平衡转录与方向预测，实现鲁棒的空间音频理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身、自我中心智能需要理解复杂环境中的空间音频；现有多模态大语言模型擅长单通道推理但缺乏空间感知，丢弃了用于声源定位和重叠声源解缠的关键空间线索。

Method: 1) FOACODER：布局灵活的空间音频编码器，通过波束成形处理来自可变数量、可变位置智能眼镜阵列的一阶 Ambisonics，并以声源定位与自我中心语音活动检测为目标训练；2) 用两阶段课程（涵盖六项任务，含声源定位与多说话人、重叠声场景下的空间选择性转录）训练 MLLM 理解空间音频嵌入；3) 引入时空加权交叉熵损失，防止转录输出主导下一 token 预测损失并淹没方向预测。

Result: 在评估集上，SEA-LM 相比基线取得更低的方位角与仰角 MAE、更高的时间 IoU、更低的外部声源幻觉率与漏检率，且多数转录任务 WER 更低；在 1,211 种含 4 至 9 个麦克风的智能眼镜阵列配置下均保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: 将空间音频编码与多任务课程学习相结合，并针对转录与定位损失失衡问题设计加权损失，可有效赋予大音频-语言模型空间感知能力，并具备跨阵列配置的泛化性。

Abstract: Embodied, ego-centric intelligence fundamentally requires the ability to comprehend spatial audio within complex environments. While large audio-language models excel at mono-channel reasoning, they lack spatial awareness, discarding critical spatial cues that enable sound localization and that can improve the disentanglement of overlapping sound sources. To address this, we present SEA-LM, a Spatial Audio Understanding model. First, we introduce FOACODER, a layout-flexible spatial audio encoder trained on source localization and ego-centric voice activity detection objectives to encode First Order Ambisonics derived from variable-count, variable-position smart-glasses arrays via beamforming. We train a Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM) to understand these spatial audio embeddings through a two-stage curriculum spanning six tasks, including sound localization and spatially selective transcription in settings with multiple speakers and overlapping sounds. To prevent the transcription outputs from dominating the next token prediction loss and overwhelming the direction predictions, we introduce a Spatio-temporal Weighted Cross-Entropy Loss. On our evaluation set, SEA-LM achieves lower azimuth and elevation MAE, higher temporal IoU, lower external-source hallucination and missing-source rates, and lower WER on most transcription tasks than compared baselines, while remaining robust across 1,211 smart-glasses array configurations with 4 to 9 microphones.

</details>


### [255] [AudioGAR: Bridging Reconstruction and Generation in Latent Audio Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05691)
*Xianghong Fang,Geeyang Tay,Wentao Ma,Tim G. J. Rudner,Dehan Kong*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出AudioGAR，通过在编码器潜变量上加噪并经由冻结的扩散模型去噪构造中间潜变量轨迹，仅微调解码器以缓解音频生成中的重建-生成失配，低成本大幅提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜音频生成模型通常两阶段训练（先学音频编解码器，再学潜生成模型），导致解码器"训练-生成失配"：解码器在编码器诱导的潜变量上训练，却在推理时接收生成器产生的潜变量。在多种数据集和生成模型下均观察到FD与FAD上的重建-生成差距，说明重建质量好不等于端到端生成质量好。直接微调解码器看似可行，但生成潜变量与源音频无对应关系，缺少成对监督。

Method: AudioGAR：对编码器潜变量加噪，再用冻结的潜扩散模型去噪，构造中间潜变量，形成从"重建端"到"生成端"的轨迹。低噪声潜变量仍保留与源音频的对应关系，从而提供成对监督以微调解码器。仅微调编解码器解码器，编码器与潜生成模型均保持冻结。

Result: 应用于AudioX时显著提升生成性能；仅需原训练音频时长的1.5%、原训练成本的0.26%。

Conclusion: 通过构造从重建到生成的潜变量轨迹可有效弥合解码器的重建-生成失配，是一种高效、低成本的解码器适配方案。

Abstract: Latent audio generative models are typically trained in two stages: an audio codec is learned first, followed by a latent generative model. This decomposition leads to a decoder train-generation mismatch: the codec decoder is trained on encoder-induced latents but deployed on generator-produced latents at inference time. Across diverse datasets and latent generative models, we observe clear reconstruction-generation gaps under both FD and FAD, showing that strong reconstruction quality does not necessarily translate into strong end-to-end generation quality. A natural remedy is to adapt the decoder on generation-produced latents, but generated latents lack correspondence with source audio and therefore cannot directly provide the paired supervision used for decoder fine-tuning. We introduce \textbf{AudioGAR}, which constructs intermediate latents by perturbing encoder latents and denoising them through the frozen latent diffusion model. These latents form a trajectory from reconstruction toward generation, with lower-noise latents retaining source correspondence and supporting paired decoder fine-tuning. We fine-tune only the codec decoder on these latents, while keeping the codec encoder and latent generative model frozen. When applied to AudioX, AudioGAR substantially improves generative performance. It requires only 1.5\% of the original training audio hours and 0.26\% of the original training cost.

</details>


### [256] [Relational Synthesis: Structure-Mediated Concatenative Synthesis for Foley and Retrieval-Augmented Audio Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05768)
*Keren Shao,Ayaka Kawano,Shlomo Dubnov*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出“关系合成”（relational synthesis），一种拼接式合成的变体，将目标代价替换为类Gromov的结构关系代价，从而在保持源音频S声学一致性的同时避免持续复制S或R的片段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Foley音频制作与神经RAG场景中，给定检索源音频S与参考音频R，希望合成既与S声学一致、又不持续复制S或R片段的新音频Y。前者是Foley制作的经典目标，后者是神经RAG中直接注入S与R时的典型问题，亟需一种能同时满足两项要求的方法。

Method: 提出关系合成（relational synthesis），是拼接式合成（concatenative synthesis）的变体：用类Gromov的结构关系代价替代传统目标代价；不模仿R的内容，而是从“山的另一侧”利用R，将R的时间结构与有向振幅运动迁移过来，据此以新颖方式重组并拼接S的音频颗粒（grains），从而保护S的声学信息。该方法可自然与神经RAG集成。

Result: 实验表明，关系合成能与神经RAG自然结合，生成的Foley音频在衡量时间一致性、声学保真度与泄漏持续性的指标上表现良好，同时保持分布级质量与文本对齐。

Conclusion: 通过将拼接式合成中的目标代价替换为关系结构代价，关系合成可同时解决声学一致性与片段复制泄漏两个问题，是一种可有效集成到神经RAG中的Foley音频生成方法。

Abstract: We ask: given a retrieved source audio $S$ and a separate reference audio $R$, can we synthesize novel audio $Y$ out of this pair $(S,R)$ such that $Y$ remains acoustically consistent with $S$, while not persistently copying segments of $S$ or $R$? The first clause is a well-known goal in Foley audio production, and the second is a well-known issue in neural RAG when $S$ and $R$ are naively injected into neural generators. We show that both clauses can be addressed simultaneously using a method we coin relational synthesis, a variation of concatenative synthesis where target cost is replaced by a relational Gromov-like structural cost. Rather than imitating the content of $R$, relational synthesis exploits it from the "other side of the hill": it transfers the temporal structure and directed amplitude motion of $R$ to reorganize and concatenate the grains of $S$ in a novel manner that protects $S$'s acoustic information. Our experiments show that relational synthesis integrates naturally with neural RAG and produces Foley audio that performs well on metrics measuring temporal agreement, acoustic fidelity, and leakage persistence, while maintaining distribution-level quality and text alignment.

</details>


### [257] [A Comprehensive Objective Evaluation of Modern Text-to-Speech for Turkish Using Speech Quality Assessment Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06057)
*Yunus Emre Ozkose,Alperen Kahraman,Ali Haznedaroglu*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文对四个现代TTS系统（Chatterbox、CosyVoice、OmniVoice、VoxCPM2）在土耳其语上进行系统性基准评测，涵盖微调与零样本配置，以VITS为基线并以自然语音为参照，采用18项客观指标进行多维度评估，并分析质量随语句长度的变化及长语音的时序一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代TTS系统虽能通过短参考片段克隆说话人或微调目标语音，但其在形态丰富、低资源语言（如土耳其语）上的表现尚未被充分刻画，缺乏系统评测。

Method: 对四个当代TTS系统（Chatterbox、CosyVoice、OmniVoice、VoxCPM2）在微调与零样本两种配置下进行系统基准评测，与传统VITS基线对比并以自然金标准语音为锚点。每个配置使用18项互补客观指标评分，涵盖学习型自然度预测器（UTMOS v2、DNSMOS-Pro、SCOREQ、WhisQA、AudioBox-PQ、NatScore、SpeechLMScore）、可懂度与信号质量估计器（SQUIM PESQ/SI-SDR/STOI、Brouhaha）、说话人相似度、分布保真度（TTSDS）及低层声学描述符。进一步分析质量随语句长度的变化，并通过分块语音上的说话人身份与自然度漂移量化长语音时序一致性。

Result: 通过18项指标对四种TTS系统在土耳其语的微调与零样本表现进行了全面刻画，揭示了不同配置与基线/自然语音的差异，并呈现了质量随语句长度的变化规律以及长语音中的说话人身份和自然度漂移情况。（摘要未给出具体数值结论）

Conclusion: 该工作为低资源形态丰富语言（土耳其语）的TTS提供了系统性评测框架，并公开评测代码以支持可复现的TTS评估。

Abstract: Modern text-to-speech (TTS) systems can clone a target speaker from a short reference clip or be fine-tuned on a target voice, yet their behaviour on morphologically rich, lower-resource languages such as Turkish remain under-characterised. We present a systematic benchmark of four contemporary systems (Chatterbox, CosyVoice, OmniVoice, and VoxCPM2) evaluated across fine-tuned and zero-shot configurations, contrasted with a conventional VITS baseline and anchored to natural gold speech. Each configuration is scored with eighteen complementary objective metrics spanning learned naturalness predictors (UTMOS v2, DNSMOS-Pro, SCOREQ, WhisQA, AudioBox-PQ, NatScore, SpeechLMScore), intelligibility and signal-quality estimators (SQUIM PESQ/SI-SDR/STOI, Brouhaha), speaker similarity, distributional fidelity (TTSDS) and low-level acoustic descriptors. We further analyse how quality varies with utterance length and quantify long-form temporal consistency through speaker-identity and naturalness drift over chunked utterances. We release our evaluation code to support reproducible TTS evaluation.

</details>


### [258] [Smorph: Playable Sound Morphing with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06478)
*Annie Chu,Hugo Flores García,Johannes Imort,Oriol Nieto,Bryan Pardo,Jordan Rudess,Prem Seetharaman,Justin Salamon*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出 smorph：一种免训练引导框架，在扩散式声音变形中解耦时间结构与音色身份，实现固定一种、变换另一种的平滑变形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的声音变形方法将时间结构与音色身份纠缠在一起，无法在保持其中一项不变的同时变换另一项，限制了音乐声音设计中的可控性。

Method: smorph 为免训练（training-free）引导框架，通过结构锚定（structural anchoring）保留声音的时间行为，同时变换其身份；支持三种变形模式：prompt-to-prompt、audio-to-prompt、audio-to-audio。

Result: 在多种数据集上，smorph 能生成平滑的变形轨迹，并相对基线显著提升时间结构与源声音的保持度；但在部分设置中向目标方向的变换更为保守。

Conclusion: 探索性案例研究中，音乐人认为 smorph 的轨迹富有表现力且易于演奏，表明结构锚定可作为乐器交互中的有效约束。

Abstract: Sound morphing, generating intermediate sounds that transition from one sonic identity to another, can be a powerful tool for musical sound design. Existing diffusion-based morphing approaches entangle temporal structure and timbral identity, offering no mechanism to hold one fixed while transforming the other. We present smorph, a training-free guidance framework that preserves how a sound behaves over time while transforming what the sound is, allowing users to morph, for instance from brass to strings at a fixed pitch. We demonstrate across three morphing modes: prompt-to-prompt, audio-to-prompt, and audio-to-audio. Evaluations across diverse datasets show that smorph effectively produces smooth morph trajectories while substantially improving temporal-structure and source preservation over baselines, albeit with more conservative target-ward transformation in some settings. In an exploratory case study, musicians found smorph trajectories to be expressive and playable, suggesting structural anchoring can serve as a productive constraint for instrumental interaction.

</details>


### [259] [Paradee: Distilling Kokoro-82M into an 8M-Parameter Single-Voice Text-to-Speech Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06817)
*Sahil Mahendrakar*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 该论文将开源多说话人TTS模型Kokoro-82M蒸馏为单说话人小模型Paradee（8.07M参数），采用教师数据合成、两半分离训练、int8量化，最终8.5MB、单CPU线程25倍实时速度，UTMOS 4.41接近教师4.52，并用无参数相位锁定滤波消除音色嗡鸣。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型TTS（如Kokoro-82M）虽质量高，但参数与算力开销大，难以在普通设备部署。作者希望保留其架构与语音质量的前提下，获得可单机运行、低资源的小模型，同时避免依赖对齐学习与联合训练等复杂流程。

Method: 以Kokoro-82M为冻结教师，先用教师合成语料并保存其时长、音高、能量与音素特征。学生Paradee沿用教师架构但层更窄，分为文本侧与解码器两半分别独立训练：文本侧预测上述特征；解码器将教师保存的特征还原为教师音频，先采用频谱损失，再进行对抗训练。最后连接两半并将权重量化为int8。针对学生残留的嗡鸣，定位到2–8 kHz浊音相位问题，在合成后加入相位锁定滤波，无需训练与额外参数。

Result: Paradee参数减少10倍、算力需求减少15倍；int8存储仅8.5 MB；在单CPU线程上达25倍实时速度；UTMOS为4.41，教师为4.52。相位锁定滤波有效消除大部分嗡鸣且不增加参数与训练成本。

Conclusion: 通过教师蒸馏、两半分离训练与int8量化，可在无需对齐学习和联合训练的情况下，将多说话人TTS压缩为可在单台笔记本运行的单说话人小模型，并保持接近教师的语音质量；后处理相位锁定滤波可低成本消除特定频段嗡鸣。

Abstract: We distill Kokoro-82M, a widely used open text-to-speech model with 54 voices, into Paradee, an 8.07M-parameter model that speaks one of them. Paradee keeps Kokoro's architecture with much narrower layers, and each of its two halves is trained separately against the frozen teacher. It has 10x fewer parameters and needs 15x less compute. We first synthesize a corpus with the teacher and keep its durations, pitch, energy and phoneme features. We then train a small text side to predict these values, and a small decoder to turn the teacher's saved values into the teacher's audio, first with spectral losses and then adversarially. Finally, we connect the two halves and quantize the weights to int8. It needs no alignment learning and no joint training, and it runs on one laptop. Stored in int8, Paradee is 8.5 MB, runs 25x faster than real time on one CPU thread, and scores 4.41 on UTMOS against the teacher's 4.52. The student initially kept a slight buzz, which we trace to the phase of voiced speech between 2 and 8 kHz. A phase-locking filter applied after synthesis removes most of it, with no training and no extra parameters. Code, model files and audio samples are at https://github.com/sahilmahendrakar/paradee

</details>


### [260] [Mind the Accent Gap: British Accent Robustness in Speech-Driven Financial Voice Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06587)
*Aadam Haq,Oggi Rudovic,Malcolm Chadwick,Jay Rainey,Shucong Zhang,Ricardo Guerrero,Sourav Bhattacharya,Maja Pantic*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出 CavaBench——首个内部采集的金融口语查询基准，用于评估多种 ASR 模型及其端到端 ASR-LLM 流水线在英式口音（苏格兰、爱尔兰、威尔士）下的表现，发现 WER 与下游工具调用准确率高度相关但对任务级性能反映不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI 语音助手多采用 ASR 加 LLM 推理，但 ASR 模型主要基于美式英语数据训练，对英式地方口音识别不佳；识别错误会传导至 LLM 阶段，导致工具调用参数错误或响应缺失，在金融场景代价高昂。同时可部署 ASR 还需满足严格的延迟与内存预算，使口音鲁棒性建模选择更加困难。

Method: 构建 CavaBench 基准，包含内部采集的金融口语查询数据，并按自报英式口音分类；在该基准上评估多种 ASR 模型，并分析其端到端 ASR-LLM 流水线行为，考察 WER 与下游工具调用准确率的关系以及不同模型和声学条件下的口音相关失败情况。

Result: WER 与下游工具调用准确率呈强负相关（r = -0.93），但 WER 有时无法反映任务级性能；口音相关失败在不同模型和声学条件下差异显著。

Conclusion: 上述发现为设计更具包容性和可靠性的语音金融助手提供了指导。

Abstract: AI voice assistants often use Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) with LLM-based reasoning, yet existing systems struggle with regional British accents, including Scottish, Irish, and Welsh accents, since most ASR models are trained predominantly on American English voice data. Consequently, errors can carry through to the LLM stage, corrupting tool-call arguments and producing wrong or missing responses, which is especially costly in finance. Deployable ASR must also meet tight latency and memory budgets, making an accent-robust model choice even harder. We introduce CavaBench, the first internally collected benchmark of spoken financial queries, and use it to evaluate a range of ASR models and their end-to-end ASR-LLM pipeline behaviour across self-reported British accents. We find that WER strongly predicts downstream tool-calling accuracy ($r = -0.93$) but can fail to reflect task-level performance, with accent-related failures varying substantially across models and acoustic conditions. These findings guide the design of more inclusive, reliable voice-based financial assistants.

</details>


### [261] [AuraSE: Low-Hallucination Generative Speech Enhancement via Multimodal Flow Matching and Inference Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06632)
*Yingda Shen,Yao Qian,Yuxuan Hu,Junan Zhang,Yuxiang Wang,Hardik Hansrajbhai Chauhan,Yudong Li,Yufei Xia,Yufei Liu,Zhizheng Wu*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出 AuraSE 流匹配语音增强框架，通过双流转单流 MMDiT 多模态结构与推理策略优化（IPO）抑制幻觉，在合成与真实测试集上均取得领先性能，且部署时仅需固定 10 步 ODE 解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式语音增强虽能产生更干净自然的语音，但存在幻觉风险，即改变语音内容或说话人身份，即使有转录文本条件约束也无法避免。此外，最优解码配置（引导尺度、采样温度、步数）因语句而异，固定配置难以通用。

Method: AuraSE 采用流匹配框架，包含两项设计：1）双流转单流多模态 Diffusion Transformer（MMDiT），使转录与声学表征交互，同时为退化输入保留专用通路；2）推理策略优化（IPO），一种在线同策略偏好优化方法，从当前模型在不同推理配置下生成多候选，用多目标奖励排序并学习相对偏好。

Result: AuraSE-IPO 在合成测试集的 12 项指标中 11 项排名第一；在真实 DNS 盲测集上，DNSMOS 与盲听得分在受评系统中最高。部署时使用固定 10 步 ODE 解码器，无需 CFG 或逐句配置搜索。

Conclusion: 通过多模态结构设计与在线同策略偏好优化相结合，AuraSE 有效缓解了生成式语音增强的幻觉问题，并在多个评测维度上达到领先水平，同时保持了简洁高效的推理部署方案。

Abstract: Generative speech enhancement models can produce cleaner and more natural-sounding speech than conventional discriminative approaches, but may hallucinate by changing speech content or speaker identity, even with transcript conditioning. We present AuraSE, a flow-matching framework that addresses hallucination through complementary modality and inference designs. First, a double-stream-to-single-stream multimodal Diffusion Transformer (MMDiT) allows transcript and acoustic representations to interact while preserving a dedicated pathway for the degraded input. Second, we find that the best decoder configuration, governed by guidance scale, sampling temperature, and step count, varies substantially across utterances. This observation motivates Inference Policy Optimization (IPO), an online, on-policy preference optimization method. IPO generates multiple candidates from the current model under different inference configurations, ranks them with a multi-objective reward, and learns from their relative preferences. AuraSE-IPO ranks first on 11 of 12 metrics across the synthetic test sets and obtains the highest DNSMOS and blind-listening scores among the evaluated systems on the real DNS blind test set. At deployment, it uses a fixed $10$-step ODE decoder without classifier-free guidance (CFG) or per-utterance configuration search.

</details>


### [262] [Revisiting Label-Free Speaker Embedding Enhancement with vMF Profile Likelihood](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06691)
*Seunghwan Kim,Jinyong Kim,Sooyoung Yang,Youngjin Ko,Myungjoo Kang*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出一种基于vMF似然的免标签说话人嵌入增强方法，以闭式目标实现自适应加权，在多个数据集上取得稳健增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在冻结主干网络的条件下，声学失配会导致说话人验证性能下降，现有免标签嵌入增强方法常采用日益复杂的结构化建模，而本文认为在该设定下清洁目标在训练中可直接观测，增强本质上是单位超球面上的匹配问题。

Method: 用von Mises--Fisher（vMF）似然对清洁目标建模，并对样本级浓度参数进行剖分（profile out），从而得到一个具有自适应权重的简单闭式目标函数。

Result: 在VoxCeleb1、VoxSRC23、CN-Celeb、VOiCES和VC-Mix上，方法基本保持基线性能，并在困难失配集上获得更明显的提升；在宽泛的单视图训练配方下保持稳定，而近期扩散基线在受控对比中可靠性下降。

Conclusion: 该设定下有效的免标签嵌入增强并不需要高度结构化的建模，简单的闭式vMF目标即可实现自适应加权与稳健增益。

Abstract: Embedding enhancement improves speaker verification under acoustic mismatch without modifying a frozen backbone. Recent work has established a practical label-free setting for this task, but often adopts increasingly structured formulations. Here, the clean target is directly observed during training, making enhancement a matching problem on the unit hypersphere. We model the clean target with a von Mises--Fisher (vMF) likelihood and profile out a sample-wise concentration parameter, yielding a simple closed-form objective with adaptive weighting. Across VoxCeleb1, VoxSRC23, CN-Celeb, VOiCES, and VC-Mix, the proposed method largely preserves the baseline and gives clearer gains on challenging mismatch sets. It also remains stable under a broad single-view recipe, where a recent diffusion baseline becomes less reliable in controlled comparisons. These results suggest that effective label-free embedding enhancement in this setting does not require a highly structured formulation.

</details>


<div id='eess.AS'></div>

# eess.AS [[Back]](#toc)

### [263] [MOV-AAD: A Large-Scale Multimodal Dataset for Auditory Attention Decoding During Moving Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04180)
*Xiaomin He,Vishal Choudhari,Tristan J. Spratt,Aarya Raghavan,Richard T. Lee,Nima Mesgarani*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 该论文提出MOV-AAD数据集，用于研究动态、自然对话场景下的听觉注意解码（AAD）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有听觉注意解码评估多基于静态、简化的语音场景，与日常真实聆听环境差距较大，难以反映真实空间动态下的选择性听觉注意。

Method: 构建MOV-AAD大规模数据集：采集64通道EEG并同步记录多种生理信号（眼动、呼吸、皮肤电、心率、血氧饱和度、体温、身体运动、光电容积脉搏波），采用更具生态效度的聆听范式，包含动态移动的对话语音源及注意投入的行为学测量。

Result: MOV-AAD支持在真实空间动态下开展鲁棒AAD、多模态注意建模、聆听努力及跨被试神经响应等研究，并提供自然聆听中选择性听觉注意的基准测试资源。

Conclusion: MOV-AAD为自然场景下选择性听觉注意的基准评测与多模态研究提供了新资源。

Abstract: Auditory attention decoding (AAD) is often evaluated on static, simplified speech scenes that poorly match everyday listening. We introduce MOV-AAD, a large-scale dataset for studying auditory attention under moving, naturalistic conversations. MOV-AAD combines 64-channel EEG with synchronized physiological recordings, including eye tracking, respiration, galvanic skin response, heart rate, peripheral oxygen saturation, body temperature, body motion, and photoplethysmography, enabling analysis of cross-modal neural and physiological markers of attention and listening effort. This dataset uses a more ecologically valid listening paradigm with dynamically moving conversational speech sources and behavioral measures of attentional engagement. MOV-AAD supports research on robust AAD in realistic spatial dynamics, multimodal attention modeling, listening effort, and intersubject neural responses, providing a resource for benchmarking selective auditory attention in naturalistic listening.

</details>


### [264] [Factorized Delayed Streams Modeling for LLM-based Streaming ASR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04333)
*Tatsunari Takagi,Kai Washizaki,Atsushi Kojima,Lianbo Liu,Koki Nikaido,Yui Sudo*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 该论文提出Factorized DSM（F-DSM），通过将等待概率与原始词表分布分离，去除ASR专用token，实现更优识别性能、更低显存占用及更好的文本困惑度保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DSM通过向LLM词表添加<p>和<w>token实现流式ASR，但这会污染文本预测空间、增大softmax计算量，并导致文本困惑度退化。

Method: 首先验证<w>可被移除且性能不受显著影响；进而提出F-DSM，将<p>的等待概率从原始LLM词表分布中因式分解出来，使大词表softmax在等待步骤可被跳过。

Result: 在Corpus of Spontaneous Japanese和LibriSpeech上，F-DSM识别性能优于DSM；大幅降低GPU显存占用，训练吞吐相近，推理因softmax跳过而略快，且文本困惑度退化减轻。

Conclusion: F-DSM通过因子化设计消除了ASR专用token对文本预测空间的干扰，在性能、显存效率与文本建模能力上均优于原DSM，是一种更优的流式ASR方案。

Abstract: Delayed Streams Modeling (DSM) enables LLM-based streaming automatic speech recognition (ASR) by aligning acoustic and text streams on a common timeline. DSM adds the padding token <p> and the word-start token <w> to the LLM vocabulary and predicts them together with normal text tokens using the same softmax. We first show that <w> can be removed while maintaining competitive recognition performance. Based on this result, we propose Factorized DSM (F-DSM), which separates the waiting probability for <p> from the distribution over the original LLM vocabulary. This factorization removes ASR-specific tokens from the text prediction space and allows the large-vocabulary softmax to be skipped on waiting steps. Experiments on the Corpus of Spontaneous Japanese and LibriSpeech show that F-DSM achieves better recognition performance than DSM. It also greatly reduces GPU memory use while maintaining similar training throughput, provides a small inference speed improvement through softmax skipping, and reduces the degradation in text-only perplexity observed with DSM.

</details>


### [265] [SepRQ : Self-Supervised Speech Mixture Representation Learning via Mask-Free, Multi-Scale Source Separation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04690)
*Séverin Baroudi,Hervé Bredin,Ricard Marxer*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: SepRQ是一个开源自监督语音表示学习框架，用伪源分离目标替代掩码预测，在多说话人场景（说话人日志、语音分离）取得SOTA，并开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流自监督语音模型针对单说话人音频设计，在多说话人场景中适用性受限；且现有的鸡尾酒会场景SSL稀缺且闭源（仅C-HuBERT和基于enrollment的SA-WavLM）。

Method: SepRQ用基于冻结随机投影码本的伪源分离目标替代掩码预测，并采用新颖的无掩码、多分辨率方法。

Result: 在SUPERB的说话人日志和语音分离任务上达到SOTA，Base和Large规模均超越WavLM及其他鸡尾酒会衍生SSL，推理参数量仅85.68M；在需要enrollment的目标说话人任务（如TS-ASR）及DIHARD 3上表现强劲；在WSJ0-3Mix三说话人混合上展现强分离能力。

Conclusion: SepRQ在保持低推理参数量的同时，在多说话人任务上超越现有SSL，并在三说话人混合等难点上表现突出，已开源以填补鸡尾酒会SSL稀缺且闭源的空白。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) is standard for speech representation learning, but mainstream models are designed around single-speaker audio, limiting their usefulness in multi-speakers scenarios. We present SepRQ, an open-source SSL framework that replaces masked prediction with a pseudo-source-separation objective over frozen random-projection codebooks. By adopting a novel mask-free, multiresolution approach, SepRQ achieves state-of-the-art performance in Speaker Diarization and Speech Separation on the SUPERB benchmark, surpassing WavLM and other cocktail-party derived SSLs at both Base and Large scales, while requiring only 85.68M inference parameters. SepRQ also demonstrates strong performance across target-speaker tasks requiring enrollment (such as Target-Speaker Automatic Speech Recognition), and on the challenging multi-domain DIHARD 3 diarization dataset. Notably, we report strong separation capabilities on three-speaker mixtures (WSJ0-3Mix), where current SSL literature struggles. While cocktail-party SSLs remain scarce and closed-source, limited to C-HuBERT and the enrollment-based SA-WavLM, we open-source SepRQ to the community.

</details>


### [266] [Influence of Geometrical Acoustic Simulator Complexity on a Trained Multisource Localizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05055)
*Fabian Staub,Nils Meyer-Kahlen,Thomas Deppisch,Sergio de las Heras,Florian Klein,Stephan Werner,Johannes M. Arend*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文研究了房间声学几何仿真复杂度对多声源声源定位与检测（SSLD）模型真实场景泛化性能的影响，发现中等复杂度的镜像源仿真即可带来较强实测性能，进一步提升复杂度收益边际递减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于学习的声源定位与检测（SSLD）需要覆盖多样声学条件的大规模标注数据，但实测数据获取成本高，通常采用仿真数据训练。提高仿真复杂度会增加数据生成成本，而获得可靠泛化所需的复杂度尚不明确，因此需要量化仿真复杂度与真实场景性能之间的权衡关系。

Method: 采用几何声学仿真（主要是镜像源法）构造不同复杂度的训练数据，从无回声条件到高阶镜像源仿真，并可选加入扩散混响、阵列仿真和随机化镜像源位置；用这些仿真数据训练同一多声源SSLD模型，并在三个基于实测的测试数据集上评估其性能。

Result: 无回声仿真训练不足；中等复杂度镜像源仿真已能提供较强的真实场景性能；继续提高复杂度仅带来边际增益。最佳性能出现在最高测试阶数的镜像源仿真，并结合阵列仿真与随机化镜像源位置，此时实测域性能接近同域仿真性能。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了仿真复杂度与性能之间的权衡，可用于指导未来数据驱动的SSLD训练策略；镜像源位置随机化是一种提升泛化能力的有效且高效手段，进一步增加仿真复杂度的收益有限。

Abstract: Learning-based acoustic sound source localization and detection (SSLD) requires large labeled datasets covering diverse acoustic conditions. Since obtaining measured data is costly, training commonly uses simulated data, while practical devices must operate under real-world conditions. However, higher simulation complexity increases data-generation cost, and the complexity required for reliable generalization remains unclear. In practice, SSLD methods often rely on efficient geometrical acoustic simulation, typically the image-source method. This work investigates how geometrical acoustic simulation complexity affects the real-world performance of a common multi-source SSLD model. We train the model with simulators ranging from anechoic conditions to high-order image-source simulations, optionally including diffuse reverberation, array simulation, and randomized image-source positions, and evaluate them on three measurement-based test datasets. Results show that anechoic simulation is insufficient, while medium-complexity image-source simulations already provide strong real-world performance. Further increases in complexity yield only marginal gains, with the best performance obtained using the highest tested image-source order, array simulation, and randomized image-source positions. For this configuration, measured-domain performance approaches within-domain simulated performance, suggesting limited benefit from further increasing simulation complexity. These findings show how the complexity-performance trade-off can be exploited in future data-driven SSLD, and highlight image-source randomization as an efficient way to improve generalization.

</details>


### [267] [UltraM2M: Leveraging Text Transcripts and Mixture Constraints for Weakly-Supervised Speech Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05155)
*Liu,Jiachen,Wu,Fulin,Wang,Zhong-Qiu*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 提出 UltraM2M，一种弱监督语音增强算法，在无监督 M2M 基础上引入基于文本转写的 ASR 损失，以增强噪声抑制能力，并在 CHiME-4 上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: M2M 虽能通过真实录制的含噪混响多通道混合信号实现无监督语音增强，但其混合约束（MC）损失可能过弱，导致噪声抑制不足。

Method: UltraM2M 扩展 M2M：在 MC 损失基础上，利用真实录制混合信号的文本转写设计 ASR 损失，对估计的语音信号进行惩罚，将该 ASR 损失视为一种弱监督形式来辅助无监督增强。

Result: 在 CHiME-4 数据集上的评测结果验证了 UltraM2M 的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过引入文本转写的 ASR 弱监督损失，可弥补 M2M 混合约束过弱的问题，从而提升语音增强的噪声抑制效果。

Abstract: We propose UltraM2M, a weakly-supervised speech enhancement algorithm building upon the recent unsupervised mixture-to-mixture (M2M) algorithm. M2M realizes unsupervised speech enhancement by training deep neural networks on a set of real-recorded noisy-reverberant multi-channel mixture signals to estimate target speech and non-target signals. The two estimated signals are penalized by a so-called mixture-constraint (MC) loss, which constrains them to reconstruct the observed mixture signals. Although shown to be effective, the mixture constraint may be too weak to enable sufficient noise reduction. To deal with this, UltraM2M extends M2M by further leveraging text transcripts of real-recorded mixtures to design an automatic speech recognition (ASR) loss to penalize the estimated speech signal. The ASR loss can be viewed as a form of weak supervision that could help unsupervised enhancement. Evaluation results on the CHiME-4 dataset show the effectiveness of UltraM2M.

</details>


### [268] [Transferable Adversarial Robustness for Speech Foundation Models via Hierarchical Stabilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05310)
*Aref Mousavi,Shahab Sherafat,Kiarash Kiani Feriz,Amirparsa Safari,Raoof Zare Moayedi,Mohammad Hossein Rohban,Mohammad Sabokrou*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 该论文提出冻结语音基础模型（SFM）的高效鲁棒适应方案：先对隐藏层表征做分层鲁棒化，再在固定层融合后仅放大分类间隔，无需下游对抗样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全量对抗微调虽能提升鲁棒性，但需为每个任务生成对抗样本并更新主干，牺牲了冻结SFM的高效性。作者希望在未来任务未知前就学习鲁棒性。

Method: 对冻结主干与线性分类器，从表征稳定性与决策边界间隔的交互理解鲁棒性。设计上：跨隐藏层稳定表征而不仅限最后一层，并保持干净表征；在干净适应选定层融合后将其固定，仅放大分类器间隔，不使用下游对抗样本。

Result: 在Wav2Vec2、HuBERT、WavLM Large上，针对四个任务、自适应30 dB攻击评估。12个主干-任务组合中，分层鲁棒化提升鲁棒准确率46.4个百分点，间隔精炼再增4.0个百分点，而干净准确率仅损失1.1个百分点。

Conclusion: 鲁棒性可在任务未知前通过分层表征稳定化与间隔精炼获得，避免逐任务对抗微调，保持冻结SFM的参数高效性。

Abstract: Frozen speech foundation models (SFMs) make downstream adaptation efficient: the backbone can stay fixed while a task learns layer fusion and a lightweight classifier. Full adversarial fine-tuning is a standard route to robustness, but generating adversarial examples and updating the backbone for every task sacrifices that efficiency. We ask whether robustness can instead be learned before future tasks are known. For a frozen backbone and linear classifier, robustness can be understood through the interaction between representation stability and decision-boundary margin. This leads directly to our design: we stabilize representations across the hidden layers, rather than only the final layer, while preserving clean representations; after clean adaptation selects the layer mixture, we keep it fixed and enlarge only the classifier margin, without downstream adversarial examples. We evaluate Wav2Vec2, HuBERT, and WavLM Large on four tasks under adaptive 30 dB attacks. Across 12 backbone-task pairs, hierarchical robustification improves robust accuracy by 46.4 pp, while margin refinement adds 4.0 pp for 1.1 pp of clean accuracy. Code and configurations are available at https://github.com/arefmousavi/hierarchical-robust-sfm.

</details>


### [269] [Speaker Tracking: Segment-online Multi-talker Organization with a Varying Number of Speakers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05693)
*Vahid A. Kalkhorani,Daniel Wong,Jacob Donley,Ashutosh Pandey,Buye Xu,DeLiang Wang*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 提出一种分段在线的模块化说话人跟踪框架，结合复谱映射分离、VAD与两阶段序列组织策略，在LibriCSS和AMI上取得最优分段在线跟踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 说话人跟踪需同时处理重叠语音、语音起止、说话人身份及随时间变化的说话人数量，现有方法在这些方面仍有不足。

Method: 分段在线模块化框架：先对每段进行说话人分离，并用VAD计算语音活动；采用复谱映射估计各说话人的实部与虚部频谱；随后通过两阶段序列组织策略生成轨迹——基于重叠的拼接用于连续分组，基于记忆的说话人验证用于非连续分组；支持单通道与多通道。

Result: 在LibriCSS和AMI数据集上达到当前最优的分段在线跟踪性能，显著降低DER与cpWER。

Conclusion: 该模块化分段在线框架能有效应对重叠语音与说话人数量变化，验证了两阶段序列组织与复谱映射分离的有效性。

Abstract: Speaker tracking is the task of separating and following multiple speakers over time. It must address overlapped speech, speech onset and offset, talker identity, and time-varying speaker count. We propose a segment-online, modular framework for single- and multi-channel speaker tracking. The proposed system first performs speaker separation in each segment and computes speech activity via voice activity detection (VAD). To generate speaker tracks over time, we introduce a two-stage sequential organization strategy: Overlap-based stitching for continuous grouping and memory-based speaker verification for discontinuous grouping. For speaker separation, we employ complex spectral mapping to estimate the real and imaginary spectrograms of underlying speakers. The proposed system achieves state-of-the-art segment-online tracking performance on the LibriCSS and AMI datasets. Our framework significantly reduces diarization error rate (DER) and concatenated minimum-permutation word error rate (cpWER) compared to other methods.

</details>


### [270] [Revisiting Frame-Wise Saliency for Audio Moment Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05737)
*Tatsuya Munakata,Hokuto Munakata*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 在AMR任务中，逐帧显著性序列本身即可作为有效的时刻预测来源：用无参数的分割规则将其转为排序时刻，在CASTELLA上一致优于解码器输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于DETR的AMR模型中，逐帧显著性序列通常仅作为辅助输出，其预测潜力未被充分挖掘；作者希望验证该序列是否可直接用于时刻检索。

Method: 将逐帧显著性序列通过受SED启发的、无学习参数的分割规则转换为排序后的候选时刻，从而直接从帧级时间信息完成时刻检索，并据此替换推理阶段的输出。

Result: 在CASTELLA数据集上，同一模型的显著性预测在QD-DETR与CG-DETR全部18次运行中均优于解码器预测；QD-DETR的R1@0.7由21.0升至36.1，在各自最优epoch下优势仍保持7-15个点。该趋势在TaskWeave与UVCOM上同样存在，而TR-DETR相反。对短时刻（标注平均≤2s）提升尤为显著，QD-DETR的R1@0.7从6.3升至27.8。解码器监督仍有助于显著性预测。

Conclusion: 显著性序列可作为独立且更强的时刻预测来源，其推理输出比解码器输出更有效；但训练中解码器监督仍对显著性预测有益，说明其训练作用与推理输出的效用不同。显著性构建方式可能影响该方法的泛化性（如TR-DETR表现相反）。

Abstract: This paper revisits frame-wise saliency for audio moment retrieval (AMR). We show that the frame-wise saliency sequence, conventionally used only as an auxiliary output in DETR-based AMR models, can itself serve as an effective source of moment predictions. We convert the saliency sequence into ranked moments using a simple SED-inspired segmentation rule with no learned parameters, enabling moment retrieval directly from frame-wise temporal information. On the CASTELLA dataset, saliency-based prediction consistently outperforms decoder-based prediction from the same model across all 18 runs of QD-DETR and CG-DETR. For QD-DETR, simply replacing the inference output improves R1@0.7 from 21.0 to 36.1. The advantage remains 7-15 points when the two outputs are evaluated at their independently selected best epochs. The same tendency extends to TaskWeave and UVCOM, whereas TR-DETR shows the opposite behavior, suggesting that how saliency construction may matter. The performance gap is especially pronounced for short moments: for queries whose annotated moments average at most 2 s, R1@0.7 improves from 6.3 to 27.8 with QD-DETR. Decoder supervision nevertheless benefits saliency-based prediction, indicating that its role during training differs from the utility of its inference output.

</details>


### [271] [Enhancing Pathological Speech through Articulatory Bottlenecks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05944)
*Ailín Pollio San Pedro,Olivier Perrotin,Thomas Hueber*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文提出一种互补的构音障碍语音重建（DSR）思路：不依赖受损语音的语言/音素表征，而是在神经网络分析-合成框架中对与语音产出相关的表征进行干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DSR方法主要依赖从受损语音中提取的语言学或音素表征来生成更可懂的波形；作者希望探索一种互补途径，即直接干预与语音产出（构音）相关的表征，以提升可懂度。

Method: 在神经网络分析-合成框架中引入一个残差映射器（residual mapper），用于修改与构音对齐的潜在空间，同时保留说话人和韵律信息。该映射器先在合成的健康语音与人工构音障碍语音的平行数据上预训练，再通过音素引导和对抗目标适配到真实构音障碍语音。此外，将构音瓶颈与维度匹配的无监督表征进行对比，以评估显式构音监督的收益。

Result: 摘要未提供具体实验结果或量化指标，仅说明通过对比构音瓶颈与维度匹配的无监督表征来评估显式构音监督的收益。

Conclusion: 该工作提出并验证了一种基于构音表征干预的DSR互补方案，并通过消融式对比探讨显式构音监督是否带来增益。

Abstract: Dysarthric speech reconstruction (DSR) typically relies on linguistic or phonetic representations extracted from impaired speech to generate a more intelligible waveform. We investigate a complementary approach that instead intervenes in a representation related to speech production. Starting from a neural analysis--synthesis framework, we introduce a residual mapper that modifies an articulatory-aligned latent space while preserving speaker and prosodic information. The mapper is pretrained on parallel synthetic healthy and artificially dysarthric speech, then adapted to natural dysarthric speech using phoneme-guided and adversarial objectives. We further compare the articulatory bottleneck with a dimension-matched unsupervised representation to assess the benefit of explicit articulatory supervision.

</details>


### [272] [Character Identity is not Speaker Identity: KyaraBench and KyaraEmbed for Character Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06013)
*Joonyong Park,Jerry Li*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 提出KyaraBench基准与KyaraEmbed编码器，直接评测"角色声纹"而非"说话人声纹"。基准含85个经人工审核的配音动画角色身份，设计跨表演者验证与同演员区分两类挑战条件，并由78名被试的听测提供人类参考分数；说话人验证基线在角色特有条件上错误率上升。KyaraEmbed采用多语言角色监督、同演员负样本与语言对齐项，在主对比的全部角色特有条件上取得最佳表现，并公开基准、协议与编码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配音角色在声优更换时仍保持同一身份，说明角色身份与说话人身份是同一录音的两个不同属性，但主流说话人验证仅度量后者。这一测量目标与实际需求之间的错位构成了本文要填补的空白。

Method: 构建KyaraBench：基于配音动画语料的85个经人工审核身份，设定两类条件——固定角色更换表演者、固定表演者更换角色；组织78人听测，获得跨表演者验证与同演员区分的人类参考分数。训练KyaraEmbed紧凑编码器，使用多语言角色监督、同演员负样本及语言对齐项。

Result: 听测提供人类参考分数；说话人验证基线在角色特有条件上错误增加；KyaraEmbed在主对比的全部角色特有条件上取得最佳表现。

Conclusion: 角色声纹可作为独立于说话人声纹的评测目标，KyaraBench与KyaraEmbed为该方向提供了基准、协议与公开编码器，支撑后续研究。
