Abstract: A dubbed character keeps its identity while the voice actor changes, so character identity and speaker identity are distinct properties of one recording, yet speaker verification measures only the latter. To address this gap, we propose KyaraBench, a benchmark that scores character voice directly instead of speaker voice, built from 85 human-audited identities in a dubbed anime corpus. It poses two challenging conditions: one that swaps the performer under a fixed character, and one that fixes the performer under changing characters. Listening studies with 78 participants provide human reference scores for cross-performer verification and same-actor discrimination. Speaker-verification baselines show increased errors under these character-specific conditions. We then train KyaraEmbed, a compact encoder using multilingual character supervision, same-actor negatives, and a language-alignment term. The model achieves the best performance on all character-specific conditions in the main comparison. We release the benchmark, protocol, and encoder publicly.

</details>


### [273] [Preemptive defense against re-identification attacks on voice anonymization via adversarial perturbation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06074)
*Michele Panariello,Yibo Bai,Massimiliano Todisco,Nicholas Evans*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 提出"先发式"语音匿名化范式:对参考语音施加对抗噪声,与测试端匿名化结合,可将等错误率从14%提升至45%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音匿名化(VA)只在测试语音上进行保护,而攻击者可将其与同样匿名化的参考语音进行比对以推断说话人身份,导致防御是"单向"的,隐私保护能力受限。

Method: 提出先发式(preemptive)VA范式:对参考语音施加对抗噪声,降低其被用于身份推断的潜力;该方法独立于具体ASV系统,并可测试端VA联合使用。

Result: 先发式保护不损害参考语音的感知质量;结合常规测试端VA后,ASV等错误率(EER)由14%提升至45%,接近完全隐私。

Conclusion: 将防御扩展到参考语音一侧可显著增强语音匿名化效果,为隐私保护提供了新思路。

Abstract: Voice anonymization (VA) is used to conceal the voice identities in speech data. Performance is usually estimated using automatic speaker verification (ASV) as a proxy to judge the capability of an attacker to re-identify speakers after anonymization. The defender anonymizes test utterances; the attacker compares them to equally anonymized reference utterances to infer voice identity, with degraded ASV performance indicating successful anonymization. Thus, the defender's protection is one-sided and only applied to test utterances via VA. Though unexplored so far, there is an opportunity to enhance anonymization by protecting reference utterances too. We present a new, preemptive VA paradigm: reference utterances are protected using adversarial noise to degrade their potential to infer voice identity. Preemptive protection does not degrade the perceived quality of reference utterances and is independent of the specific ASV system used for identity inference. By combining preemptive protection and regular test-side VA, ASV equal error rates can be increased from 14\% to 45\% (near-perfect privacy).

</details>


### [274] [Lyric: Wave-Domain Computing for Efficient Spoken-Digit Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06433)
*Jeeven Balasubramaniam,Nakul Garg*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: Lyric 是一种将频谱滤波移入声学结构（无源声学谐振器）的语音识别前端，在数字化前完成特征提取，从而大幅降低采样、预处理与推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低功耗语音识别不仅需要降低神经推理开销，还需要降低音频采集与特征处理成本；本文旨在把频谱滤波从数字域前移到声学结构中以减少数据量和处理量。

Method: 采用波域计算（wave-domain computing）在数字化之前提取特征：用被动声学谐振器按频率分离语音，将其缓慢变化的包络送入紧凑的时序神经网络；并构建了原型系统进行评估。

Result: 在说话人分离的 AudioMNIST 数字识别任务上，跨九类分类器族验证：前端采集的标量样本比 16 kHz 波形少 32 倍；在 Raspberry Pi 4 上与 EdgeSpeechNet-A 对比，预处理加推理延迟及每推理估计处理器能耗降低 98.6%，准确率下降 3.58 个百分点至 95.70%。

Conclusion: 将频谱滤波嵌入声学前端可显著降低采样率与计算能耗，虽带来小幅精度损失，但为低功耗语音识别提供了可行的波域计算路径。

Abstract: Low-power speech recognition requires reducing audio acquisition and feature-processing costs as well as neural inference. We present Lyric, a speech-recognition front end that uses wave-domain computing to extract features before digitization. Our proposed system uses passive acoustic resonators to separate speech by frequency and supplies their slowly varying envelopes to a compact temporal neural network. This moves spectral filtering into the acoustic structure, reducing the data and processing needed for recognition. We build a prototype and evaluate spoken-digit recognition on a speakerdisjoint AudioMNIST split across nine classifier families. The front end acquires 32 times fewer scalar samples than a 16-kHz waveform. In the EdgeSpeechNet-A comparison on a Raspberry Pi 4, we reduce preprocessing-plus-inference latency and estimated processor energy per inference by 98.6%, with a 3.58-percentage-point decrease in accuracy to 95.70%.

</details>


### [275] [When Layer Selection Misleads Speech Depression Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06465)
*Paula A. Perez-Toro,David Gimeno-Gómez,Daniel Rückert,Andreas Maier*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 在小型临床语音数据集上探测编码器层表征时,朴素地挑选在评估数据上表现最好的层会严重高估性能(DAIC-WOZ 上 AUC 虚高至多 0.09);应采用嵌套模型选择协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练语音表征越来越多用于抑郁检测,但常在与评估同一份数据上选择最佳编码器层,导致所报告性能存在选择偏差;论文旨在量化并定位这一偏差,并给出无泄漏的公平比较基线。

Method: 在 DAIC-WOZ 上对五个深度编码器家族采用重复嵌套交叉验证,比较朴素 best-of-25 层探测与嵌套选择;并在打乱标签的真实隐表征上验证偏差来源;在第二个语料(Androids,意大利语)上复现;在无泄漏选择下跨表征家族进行公平比较;并扩展到单个 PHQ-8 症状层面。

Result: 朴素 best-of-25 层探测将 AUC 抬高至多 0.09;在打乱标签下朴素选择仍达 AUC 0.59(嵌套选择为 0.50),且偏差随探测层数增加和样本量减小而增大,说明来源是选择而非信号;该效应在 Androids(意大利语)上复现;无泄漏选择下,一个紧凑的任务对齐情感-韵律模型可与复杂得多的 SSL、深度学习、音频 LLM 及基于 codec 的方法相当,而下游可训练参数约少 10^3 倍;更细粒度的 PHQ-8 单症状分析中偏差依旧存在。

Conclusion: 在小型临床语音数据集上探测编码器层表征时,应将嵌套模型选择协议作为标准做法。

Abstract: Pretrained speech representations are increasingly used for depression detection, but selecting the best encoder layer on the same data used for evaluation biases reported performance. On DAIC-WOZ, with repeated nested cross-validation across five deep encoder families, naive best-of-25-layer probing inflates AUC by up to $0.09$. On shuffled labels over the \emph{real} latent representations, naive best-of-25 still reaches AUC $0.59$ versus $0.50$ under nested selection, and this bias grows with the number of probed layers and with smaller samples, isolating selection, not signal, as the cause. The effect replicates on a second clinical corpus and language (Androids, Italian). Under leakage-free selection, a fair comparison across representation families identifies a compact, task-aligned affect--prosody model as competitive with substantially more complex SSL, deep-learning, audio-LLM, and codec-based approaches, while using $\sim\!10^{3}\times$ fewer trainable downstream parameters. Extending the analysis to individual PHQ-8 symptoms shows that the same selection bias persists at this finer-grained level. We argue nested model-selection protocols should be standard when probing encoder layer representations on small clinical speech datasets.

</details>


### [276] [Ensemble-Based Perceptual Audio Quality Assessment with Confidence Intervals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06569)
*Pablo M. Delgado,Andreas Brendel,Konstantin Schmidt,Jürgen Herre*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量级侵入式音频质量指标：将PEAQ感知前端与bagging回归集成结合，通过主观数据校准输出逐条目分数分布，实现不确定性评估与置信区间，MOS精度与更复杂方法相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 客观音频质量指标通常只给出点估计，而主观听音测试给出分数分布，可导出MOS、置信区间及显著性判断。本文希望弥补这一差距，使客观指标也能输出不确定性信息。

Method: 方法为轻量级侵入式指标：前端采用PEAQ风格感知模型（ITU-R BS.1387），后端使用bagging回归器集成；仅使用11个固定PEAQ特征与低容量回归器，并用公开数据训练；再通过主观数据校准，将集成输出对齐到听者打分，从而得到逐条目相关的分数分布。

Result: 校准在所有评估数据集上改善了与主观分布的一致性及置信区间覆盖率，同时保持MOS精度；其表现与更耗数据的端到端方法相当。输出分布可支持不确定性感知评估，并将目标听音测试导向不确定条件；也能进行近似成对比较，但尚不足以实现可靠的显著性推断。

Conclusion: 该轻量级方法以少量特征和低容量模型实现了带不确定性量化的音频质量预测，可指导听音测试的资源分配；但显著性推断仍需进一步改进。

Abstract: Objective audio quality metrics typically provide point estimates, whereas listening tests yield score distributions from which mean opinion scores (MOS), confidence intervals (CIs), and significance decisions are derived. We propose a lightweight intrusive metric that combines a PEAQ-style perceptual front-end (ITU-R BS.1387) with a bagging ensemble of regressors. Calibration with subjective data aligns ensemble outputs with listener scores. The resulting item-dependent score distributions enable uncertainty assessment, panel-size-matched CIs, and identification of less conclusive predictions. Calibration improves agreement with subjective distributions and CI coverage across all evaluated datasets while preserving MOS accuracy. Using only 11 fixed PEAQ features, low-capacity regressors, and public training data, the method performs comparably to more data-intensive end-to-end approaches. Its output can support uncertainty-aware assessment and target listening tests towards uncertain conditions. The distributions also enable approximate pairwise comparisons, but not yet reliable significance inference.

</details>
